SkillOpt: Executive Strategy for Self-Evolving Agent Skills
Authors: Yifan Yang, Ziyang Gong, Weiquan Huang, Qihao Yang, Ziwei Zhou, Zisu Huang, Yan Li, Xuemei Gao, Qi Dai, Bei Liu, Kai Qiu, Yuqing Yang, Dongdong Chen, Xue Yang, Chong Luo
First: 2026-05-22T17:59:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:59:50+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 4 figures, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agent skills today are hand-crafted, generated one-shot, or evolved through loosely controlled self-revision, none of which behaves like a deep-learning optimizer for the skill, and none of which reliably improves over its starting point under feedback. We argue the skill should instead be trained as the external state of a frozen agent, with the same discipline that makes weight-space optimization reproducible. SkillOpt is, to our knowledge, the first systematic controllable text-space optimizer for agent skills: a separate optimizer model turns scored rollouts into bounded add/delete/replace edits on a single skill document, and an edit is accepted only when it strictly improves a held-out validation score. A textual learning-rate budget, rejected-edit buffer, and epoch-wise slow/meta update make skill training stable while adding zero inference-time model calls at deployment. Across six benchmarks, seven target models, and three execution harnesses (direct chat, Codex, Claude Code), SkillOpt is best or tied on all 52 evaluated (model, benchmark, harness) cells and beats every per-cell competitor among human, one-shot LLM, Trace2Skill, TextGrad, GEPA, and EvoSkill skills. On GPT-5.5 it lifts the average no-skill accuracy by +23.5 points in direct chat, by +24.8 inside the Codex agentic loop, and by +19.1 inside Claude Code. Transfer experiments further show that optimized skill artifacts retain value when moved across model scales, between Codex and Claude Code execution environments, and to a nearby math benchmark without further optimization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SkillOpt：自进化智能体技能的执行策略
当前智能体技能多为人工编写、一次性生成或通过松散控制的自我修订演化，这些方法均不具备深度学习优化器对技能的优化特性，且无法在反馈下可靠地提升初始性能。我们主张应将技能训练为冻结智能体的外部状态，采用与权重空间优化相同的严谨方法。SkillOpt据我们所知是首个系统性的可控文本空间智能体技能优化器：一个独立的优化器模型将评分的 rollout 转化为对单个技能文档的有界增删改编辑，且仅当编辑严格提升保留验证集得分时才被接受。文本学习率预算、被拒编辑缓冲区及按周期的慢/元更新机制使技能训练稳定，且部署时无需额外模型调用。在六个基准、七种目标模型及三种执行环境（直接对话、Codex、Claude Code）中，SkillOpt在全部52个评估单元（模型-基准-执行环境组合）中均表现最佳或并列第一，且在人类、一次性LLM、Trace2Skill、TextGrad、GEPA、EvoSkill等所有技能方法中均优于每个单元的对手。在GPT-5.5上，SkillOpt在直接对话中提升无技能准确率+23.5分，在Codex智能体循环内提升+24.8分，在Claude Code内提升+19.1分。迁移实验进一步表明，优化后的技能构件在跨模型规模、在Codex与Claude Code执行环境间、以及迁移到相近数学基准时仍保持价值，无需进一步优化。
Summary / 总结
Agent skills today are hand-crafted, generated one-shot, or evolved through loosely controlled self-revision, none of which behaves like a deep-learning optimizer for the skill, and none of which reliably improves over its starting point under feedback.
为了解决当前人工设计、一次性生成或松散自演化的代理技能缺乏像深度学习优化器那样可靠自我改进的问题，作者提出了SkillOpt，一种将代理技能视为冻结外部状态并采用可复现优化纪律进行训练的文本空间优化器。该方法使用独立优化器模型，根据评分回放生成有界编辑（增删改）操作于单一技能文档，仅当编辑严格提升验证集得分时才被接受。通过学习率预算、被拒编辑缓冲区和慢速/元更新机制，训练过程稳定，且部署时无需额外模型调用。在六个基准、七种目标模型和三种执行环境的52个评估单元中，SkillOpt在所有单元中表现最佳或并列第一，全面超越人类、一次性LLM及Trace2Skill、TextGrad、GEPA、EvoSkill等现有技能方法。在GPT-5.5上，其将无技能准确率分别提升23.5分（直接对话）、24.8分（Codex循环）和19.1分（Claude Code），且优化后的技能可跨模型规模、执行环境及数学基准迁移使用，无需重新优化。
Geo-Align: Video Generation Alignment via Metric Geometry Reward
Authors: Zizun Li, Haoyu Guo, Runzhe Teng, Chunhua Shen, Tong He
First: 2026-05-22T17:59:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:59:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Camera-controlled video generation has achieved remarkable progress in recent years. However, existing video-to-video re-rendering methods primarily rely on Supervised Fine-Tuning using synthetic datasets. At present, there is an extreme scarcity of synchronized, multi-view real-world video data. Consequently, the prevailing paradigm often exhibits limited generalization when processing out-of-distribution real-world videos, with models struggling to accurately adhere to physical scales and camera trajectories. To bridge this gap, we propose Geo-Align, the first Reinforcement Learning framework specifically designed for camera-controlled video re-rendering. Built upon a pretrained model, we optimize the model through a scale-aware perceptual reward mechanism. Specifically, we introduce a metric 3D estimator to extract precise camera trajectories from generated videos, explicitly penalizing deviations in rotation and translation. Furthermore, we meticulously designed a data pipeline strategy based on real-world conditioning videos and target camera trajectories derived from synthetic data, eliminating the reliance on paired data. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Geo-Align consistently outperforms existing supervised learning baselines in both precise camera controllability and visual fidelity, indicating the effectiveness of our method.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Geo-Align：通过度量几何奖励实现视频生成对齐
近年来，相机控制的视频生成取得了显著进展。然而，现有的视频到视频重渲染方法主要依赖于使用合成数据集的监督微调。目前，同步多视角真实世界视频数据极度稀缺。因此，当前主流范式在处理分布外的真实世界视频时往往泛化能力有限，模型难以准确遵循物理尺度和相机轨迹。为弥合这一差距，我们提出Geo-Align，这是首个专为相机控制视频重渲染设计的强化学习框架。基于预训练模型，我们通过尺度感知的感知奖励机制优化模型。具体而言，我们引入了一个度量3D估计器，从生成视频中提取精确的相机轨迹，明确惩罚旋转和位移偏差。此外，我们精心设计了一种基于真实世界条件视频和从合成数据中推导出的目标相机轨迹的数据管道策略，消除了对配对数据的依赖。大量实验表明，Geo-Align在精确相机可控性和视觉保真度方面均显著优于现有监督学习基线，证明了我们方法的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of synchronized real-world multi-view video data and the poor generalization of supervised methods to out-of-distribution camera-controlled videos, Geo-Align introduces a reinforcement learning framework to improve video re-rendering. It leverages a pretrained model and optimizes it using a scale-aware perceptual reward based on a metric 3D estimator that extracts camera trajectories from generated videos, penalizing deviations in rotation and translation. The method also employs a data pipeline using real-world conditioning videos and synthetic camera trajectories, avoiding paired data. Experiments show Geo-Align significantly outperforms supervised baselines in both camera controllability and visual fidelity.
为解决现有监督方法在真实世界视频中相机控制能力差、泛化能力弱的问题，Geo-Align提出首个基于强化学习的视频重渲染框架，通过度量几何奖励机制优化生成视频的相机轨迹。该方法利用预训练模型与3D度量估计器提取生成视频的相机运动，对旋转与平移偏差进行惩罚，并设计基于真实视频与合成轨迹的无配对数据管道。实验表明，Geo-Align在相机可控性与视觉保真度上显著优于现有监督基线方法。
PiD: Fast and High-Resolution Latent Decoding with Pixel Diffusion
Authors: Yifan Lu, Qi Wu, Jay Zhangjie Wu, Zian Wang, Huan Ling, Sanja Fidler, Xuanchi Ren
First: 2026-05-22T17:59:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:59:42+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://research.nvidia.com/labs/sil/projects/pid/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Most practical high-resolution text-to-image systems, including latent diffusion and autoregressive models, perform generation in a compact latent space, and a decoder maps the generated latents back to pixels. Yet the latent-to-pixel decoder is reconstruction-oriented, optimized to invert the encoder rather than synthesize more details, and becomes increasingly costly at megapixel scale. This drawback calls for a more expressive and efficient decoding paradigm. Motivated by recent progress in scalable pixel-space diffusion, we introduce PiD, a Pixel diffusion Decoder that reformulates latent decoding as conditional pixel diffusion, unifying decoding and upsampling into one generative module. By denoising directly in high-resolution pixel space, PiD synthesizes $4\times$ and even $8\times$ upscaled images with low latency. For latent conditioning, a lightweight sigma-aware adapter injects noise-corrupted latents into the pixel diffusion backbone, enabling PiD to decode partially denoised latents and terminate the latent diffusion process early. To further improve efficiency, we distill the model using DMD2, reducing inference to just 4 steps. PiD applies to both conventional VAE latents and semantic latents (e.g., SigLIP, DINOv2) used in recent RAE-based models. PiD decodes latents of $512 \times 512$ images into $2048 \times 2048$ pixels in under 1 second with 13 GB peak memory on a consumer RTX 5090, and as fast as 210 ms on a GB200 GPU, about $6\times$ faster than cascaded diffusion-based super-resolution pipelines with better visual fidelity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PiD：基于像素扩散的快速高分辨率潜在解码
大多数实用的高分辨率文本到图像系统（包括潜在扩散和自回归模型）在紧凑的潜在空间中进行生成，解码器将生成的潜在表示映射回像素。然而，潜在到像素的解码器是重建导向的，旨在反演编码器而非合成更多细节，且在兆像素级别时成本急剧上升。这一缺陷呼唤更表达力强且高效的解码范式。受近期可扩展像素空间扩散进展的启发，我们引入PiD（像素扩散解码器），将潜在解码重新表述为条件像素扩散，将解码与上采样统一为一个生成模块。通过直接在高分辨率像素空间去噪，PiD可在低延迟下合成4倍甚至8倍上采样的图像。为实现潜在条件化，一个轻量级的sigma感知适配器将噪声污染的潜在表示注入像素扩散主干网络，使PiD能够解码部分去噪的潜在表示并提前终止潜在扩散过程。为进一步提升效率，我们使用DMD2进行模型蒸馏，将推理步骤减少至仅4步。PiD适用于传统VAE潜在表示和近期基于RAE模型所使用的语义潜在表示（如SigLIP、DINOv2）。在消费级RTX 5090显卡上，PiD可在1秒内将512×512图像潜在表示解码为2048×2048像素，峰值内存占用13GB；在GB200 GPU上速度可达210毫秒，比基于扩散的级联超分辨率流程快约6倍，且视觉保真度更优。
Summary / 总结
Most practical high-resolution text-to-image systems, including latent diffusion and autoregressive models, perform generation in a compact latent space, and a decoder maps the generated latents back to pixels.
为解决传统潜空间解码器在高分辨率图像生成中效率低下的问题，PiD提出一种基于像素扩散的解码范式，将上采样与生成统一为单个模块。通过在像素空间直接去噪，并使用轻量级sigma感知适配器对潜空间输入进行条件控制，PiD支持提前终止扩散过程同时保留细节。实验表明，PiD可在消费级硬件上将512x512图像上采样至2048x2048，耗时不到一秒，比级联扩散超分流程快6倍，且视觉质量更优。
LLMs as Noisy Channels: A Shannon Perspective on Model Capacity and Scaling Laws
Authors: Xu Ouyang, Deyi Liu, Yuhang Cai, Jing Liu, Yuan Yang, Chen Zheng, Thomas Hartvigsen, Yiyuan Ma
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-22T17:59:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:59:38+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing scaling laws for Large Language Models (LLMs), predominantly monotonic power laws, fail to explain emerging non-monotonic phenomena such as catastrophic overtraining and quantization-induced degradation, where performance deteriorates despite increased compute. We propose the Shannon Scaling Law, a unified theoretical framework that models LLM training as information transmission over a noisy channel, grounded in the Shannon-Hartley theorem. By mapping model parameters to channel bandwidth and training tokens to signal power, our formulation explicitly captures the interaction between learning signal and intrinsic noise. This perspective reveals a fundamental Shannon capacity for LLMs: scaling model size or data without preserving a sufficient signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) inevitably amplifies noise, inducing a transition from monotonic improvement to U-shaped performance degradation. We validate our theory through experiments on Pythia and OLMo2 under perturbations, including Gaussian noise, quantization and supervised fine-tuning on math, QA and code tasks. The Shannon Scaling Law consistently outperforms classical scaling laws and recent perturbation-aware laws, achieving strong $R^2$ scores and accurately capturing loss basins missed by prior approaches. It also extrapolates: fitted on $\leq$6.9B Pythia models with $\leq$180B tokens, it predicts the unseen 12B model up to 307B tokens at pooled $R^2{=}0.847$, while monotonic baselines collapse.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型作为噪声信道：从香农视角看模型容量与缩放定律
现有大语言模型（LLMs）的缩放定律，主要为单调幂律，无法解释诸如灾难性过训练和量化导致性能下降等非单调现象——即计算量增加时性能反而恶化。 我们提出香农缩放定律，一个统一的理论框架，将LLM训练建模为在噪声信道中传输信息，基于香农-哈特利定理。通过将模型参数映射为信道带宽、训练token映射为信号功率，我们的公式明确捕捉了学习信号与固有噪声的相互作用。该视角揭示了LLMs的香农容量：若在扩大模型规模或数据量时未保持足够的信噪比（SNR），噪声必然放大，导致性能从单调提升过渡到U型退化。 我们在Pythia和OLMo2模型上通过扰动实验（包括高斯噪声、量化及数学、问答、代码任务的监督微调）验证了该理论。香农缩放定律持续优于经典缩放定律和近期扰动感知定律，取得高R²分数，并准确捕捉先前方法遗漏的损失谷底。它还能外推：在≤6.9B参数的Pythia模型和≤180B token数据上拟合，可准确预测未见的12B模型在307B token时的性能（池化R²=0.847），而单调基线则崩溃。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the failure of existing monotonic scaling laws to explain non-monotonic performance drops in LLMs under perturbations like quantization or overtraining, the authors propose the Shannon Scaling Law, framing LLM training as information transmission over a noisy channel using the Shannon-Hartley theorem. They map model parameters to channel bandwidth and training tokens to signal power, revealing that performance degrades when scaling without maintaining sufficient signal-to-noise ratio, leading to a U-shaped curve. Experiments on Pythia and OLMo2 under Gaussian noise, quantization, and fine-tuning show the Shannon Scaling Law outperforms prior models with higher R² scores and accurately predicts loss basins, even extrapolating to unseen 12B models trained on 307B tokens with R²=0.847, while monotonic laws fail.
为了解释现有单调缩放定律无法解释的LLM在量化、过训练等扰动下出现的非单调性能下降现象，作者提出香农缩放定律，将模型训练建模为在噪声信道上的信息传输，依据香农-哈特利定理。他们将模型参数映射为信道带宽，训练token映射为信号功率，揭示了与信噪比相关的固有容量极限。在Pythia和OLMo2模型上进行的实验表明，该定律在噪声、量化和微调条件下表现优于传统模型，具有高R²值，能准确捕捉损失谷值，并可外推预测未见模型性能（如12B模型训练307B tokens时R²=0.847），而单调定律在此失效。
From Raw Experience to Skill Consumption: A Systematic Study of Model-Generated Agent Skills
Authors: Zisu Huang, Jingwen Xu, Yifan Yang, Ziyang Gong, Qihao Yang, Muzhao Tian, Xiaohua Wang, Changze Lv, Xuemei Gao, Qi Dai, Bei Liu, Kai Qiu, Xue Yang, Dongdong Chen, Xiaoqing Zheng, Chong Luo
First: 2026-05-22T17:59:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:59:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language agents increasingly improve by reusing \emph{skills} -- structured procedural artifacts distilled from past experience. In particular, \emph{domain-level} and \emph{model-generated} skills are especially promising. They offer fast adaptation within a domain by encoding domain-specific recurring procedures, and they scale beyond labor-intensive hand-crafting. However, while extraction methods continue to proliferate, understanding remains limited, with no comprehensive study spanning the full skill lifecycle -- \textbf{experience generation}, \textbf{skill extraction}, and \textbf{skill consumption} -- to ask whether such skills actually work, when they work, and what makes them succeed or fail. To close this gap, we build a utility-grounded evaluation framework that provides systematic experimental results across extractors and target agents, covering five diverse agentic task domains. We find that model-generated skills are beneficial on average but exhibit non-trivial negative transfer, and that neither extractors nor targets behave uniformly. A model can be a strong extractor yet a weak consumer, or vice versa, with skill utility independent of model scale or baseline task strength. To explain these patterns, we then dissect each lifecycle stage in depth, analyzing how experience composition shapes skill quality, what properties characterize useful skills, and how the same skill transfers across different consumers. Finally, we translate these findings into a concrete \emph{meta-skill} that guides skill extraction toward the features tied to actual utility, which consistently improves skill quality across domains and substantially reduces negative transfer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从原始经验到技能消费：模型生成代理技能的系统研究
语言代理通过重用从过往经验中提炼出的\emph{技能}——结构化的程序化产物——不断改进。特别是\emph{领域级}和\emph{模型生成}技能尤为有前景：它们通过编码特定领域的重复性程序，实现领域内快速适应，并超越耗时的手工构建。然而，尽管提取方法不断涌现，理解仍显不足，尚无全面研究覆盖技能全生命周期——\textbf{经验生成}、\textbf{技能提取}和\textbf{技能消费}——以探究此类技能是否有效、何时有效，以及成功或失败的原因。为弥合这一差距，我们构建了一个以效用为导向的评估框架，系统性地在多种提取器和目标代理间进行实验，涵盖五个多样化的代理任务领域。我们发现，模型生成技能总体有益，但存在非平凡的负迁移现象，且提取器与目标代理表现并不统一：一个模型可能作为强提取器却为弱消费者，反之亦然，技能效用与模型规模或基线任务强度无关。为解释这些模式，我们深入剖析每个生命周期阶段，分析经验组合如何塑造技能质量、哪些属性构成有用技能，以及同一技能如何在不同消费者间迁移。最后，我们将这些发现转化为一个具体的\emph{元技能}，引导技能提取聚焦于实际效用相关的特征，该元技能在各领域中持续提升技能质量，并显著减少负迁移。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the gap in understanding how model-generated agent skills perform across their full lifecycle — from experience generation to extraction and consumption — by building a utility-grounded evaluation framework across five diverse task domains. The authors find that while model-generated skills generally improve agent performance, they often exhibit non-trivial negative transfer, and neither skill extractors nor target agents behave uniformly: a strong extractor may be a weak consumer, and vice versa. Skill utility does not correlate with model scale or baseline task strength. Through detailed analysis of each lifecycle stage, the study identifies key properties of useful skills and how they transfer across agents, ultimately proposing a meta-skill that prioritizes features tied to actual utility, which consistently improves skill quality and reduces negative transfer across domains.
该研究旨在填补对模型生成技能在完整生命周期（经验生成、技能提取与技能消费）中表现理解不足的空白，构建了一个基于实用性的评估框架，在五个不同任务领域进行系统实验。结果发现，模型生成技能总体上能提升代理性能，但常伴随非平凡的负迁移现象，且提取器与目标代理的表现并不统一：强提取器可能弱于消费者，反之亦然，技能效用与模型规模或基础任务能力无关。通过对各阶段的深入分析，研究识别了有效技能的关键属性及其跨代理迁移机制，最终提出一种元技能，优先选择与实际效用相关的特征，显著提升技能质量并减少负迁移。
SPACENUM: Revisiting Spatial Numerical Understanding in VLMs
Authors: Jianshu Zhang, Yijiang Li, Huifeixin Chen, Haoran Lu, Letian Xue, Bingyang Wang, Han Liu
First: 2026-05-22T17:58:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:58:36+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://sterzhang.github.io/SpaceNum-Home
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed in embodied environments, where they need produce numerical outputs such as action magnitudes and spatial coordinates. Although these numbers appear meaningful, it remains unclear whether these numerical outputs are genuinely grounded in spatial perception. Therefore, in this work, we revisit spatial numerical understanding through SpaceNum, a unified framework that captures two complementary settings: numbers as dynamic transitions during spatial exploration, and numbers as static layouts in spatial reasoning. We formulate two bidirectional tasks, Num2Space and Space2Num, to evaluate how well VLMs map between vision-side spatial structure and language-side numerical representations. We systematically study whether current VLMs truly understand numerical values in spatial settings. Across dynamic transitions and static layouts, we find that models largely fail to ground numbers in spatial meaning and often perform close to random guess. Through error analysis, reasoning trace analysis, and controlled interventions, we show that current VLMs rely heavily on shallow spatial cues, struggle to build stable coordinate-aware representations, and fail to abstract structured spatial layouts from visual observations. We further show that explicit reasoning provides only marginal gains, while tuning can partially improve spatial numerical understanding and transfer to external spatial reasoning benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SPACENUM：重新审视视觉-语言模型中的空间数值理解
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）越来越多地部署在具身环境中，需要生成如动作幅度和空间坐标等数值输出。尽管这些数字看似有意义，但尚不清楚这些数值输出是否真正根植于空间感知。因此，本文通过SpaceNum框架重新审视空间数值理解，该框架统一了两种互补场景：数值作为空间探索中的动态过渡，以及数值作为空间推理中的静态布局。我们设计了两个双向任务Num2Space和Space2Num，以评估VLMs在视觉空间结构与语言数值表示之间映射的能力。我们系统研究当前VLMs是否真正理解空间场景中的数值。在动态过渡和静态布局中，我们发现模型大多未能将数值根植于空间意义，表现接近随机猜测。通过错误分析、推理轨迹分析和受控干预，我们表明当前VLMs过度依赖浅层空间线索，难以构建稳定的坐标感知表示，且无法从视觉观察中抽象出结构化的空间布局。进一步表明，显式推理仅带来微小提升，而调优可部分改善空间数值理解，并能迁移到外部空间推理基准。
Summary / 总结
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed in embodied environments, where they need produce numerical outputs such as action magnitudes and spatial coordinates.
为评估视觉语言模型（VLMs）在具身任务中是否真正将数值输出与空间感知关联，本研究提出SpaceNum统一框架，通过双向任务Num2Space与Space2Num检验模型在视觉空间结构与语言数值表示间的映射能力。实验表明，当前VLMs在动态过渡与静态布局场景中普遍无法有效将数值锚定于空间语义，表现接近随机猜测。错误分析与干预实验进一步揭示模型依赖浅层空间线索、难以构建稳定的坐标感知表示、无法从视觉输入中抽象结构化空间布局。尽管显式推理提升有限，定向调优可部分改善空间数值理解并迁移至外部空间推理基准。
ETCHR: Editing To Clarify and Harness Reasoning
Authors: Beichen Zhang, Yuhong Liu, Jinsong Li, Yuhang Zang, Jiaqi Wang, Dahua Lin
First: 2026-05-22T17:58:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:58:28+00:00
Comments: Code, model and data are open-sourced at https://github.com/InternLM/ETCHR
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models have advanced visual reasoning, yet a purely textual chain of thought remains a bottleneck for questions that require fine-grained focus or view transformations. The ''think with images'' paradigm narrows this gap, but existing approaches are either constrained by fixed predefined toolkits or produce noisy intermediate images from unified multimodal methods. We pursue a third option: using a dedicated image editing model and decouple it with an understanding model. However, off-the-shelf image editors fail as reasoning assistants with two complementary gaps: a language-side gap, where editors trained as passive instruction-followers cannot map an abstract question to an appropriate visual transformation, and a generation-side gap, where edit correctness degrades as reasoning depth grows. Guided by this analysis, we introduce ETCHR (Editing To Clarify and Harness Reasoning), a question-conditioned, reasoning-aware image editor decoupled from the downstream understanding model and trained with a two-stage recipe targeted at the two gaps: Reasoning Imitation via supervised fine-tuning on edit trajectories, followed by Reasoning Enhancement with VLM-derived rewards for edit correctness and downstream reasoning accuracy. Since the editor is decoupled, ETCHR plugs into different open- and closed-source MLLMs in a training-free manner. Across five task families (fine-grained perception, chart understanding, logic reasoning, jigsaw restoration, and 3D understanding), ETCHR raises average Pass@1 from 55.95 to 60.77 (+4.82) with Qwen3-VL-8B, from 65.08 to 70.55 (+5.47) with Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite, and from 76.55 to 81.16 (+4.61) with the 1T-parameter MoE model Kimi K2.5.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ETCHR：通过编辑澄清并驾驭推理
多模态大语言模型已推动视觉推理发展，但对需要精细聚焦或视角变换的问题，纯文本思维链仍存在瓶颈。'边看图边思考'范式缩小了这一差距，但现有方法要么受限于固定预定义工具集，要么从统一多模态方法中生成噪声中间图像。我们提出第三种方案：使用专用图像编辑模型，并与理解模型解耦。然而，现成图像编辑器作为推理助手存在两大互补缺陷：语言端缺陷，即训练为被动指令跟随者的编辑器无法将抽象问题映射到合适的视觉变换；生成端缺陷，即编辑正确性随推理深度增加而下降。基于此分析，我们提出ETCHR（通过编辑澄清并驾驭推理），这是一种受问题条件驱动、具备推理感知能力的图像编辑器，与下游理解模型解耦，并通过两阶段训练方案针对性解决上述缺陷：第一阶段通过编辑轨迹监督微调实现推理模仿，第二阶段通过视觉语言模型衍生奖励提升编辑正确性及下游推理准确性。由于编辑器解耦，ETCHR可无训练地适配不同开源与闭源多模态大语言模型。在五大任务类别（细粒度感知、图表理解、逻辑推理、拼图恢复、3D理解）中，ETCHR使Qwen3-VL-8B的平均Pass@1从55.95提升至60.77（+4.82），Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite从65.08提升至70.55（+5.47），1T参数MoE模型Kimi K2.5从76.55提升至81.16（+4.61）。
Summary / 总结
To address the limitation of textual chain-of-thought in multimodal reasoning, ETCHR introduces a decoupled image editing model trained to map abstract questions into precise visual transformations. It overcomes two key gaps in off-the-shelf editors—language understanding and edit fidelity under complex reasoning—via a two-stage training: first, supervised fine-tuning on edit trajectories to mimic reasoning steps; second, reinforcement learning using VLM rewards to enhance edit correctness and downstream accuracy. Evaluated across five reasoning tasks, ETCHR consistently boosts Pass@1 scores by 4.6–5.5 percentage points across multiple MLLMs, including Qwen3-VL-8B, Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite, and Kimi K2.5, without requiring retraining of the underlying models.
为解决视觉推理中纯文本思维链的局限，ETCHR提出一种解耦的图像编辑方法，通过生成中间视觉变换来增强推理能力。它针对现有图像编辑器在语言理解与复杂推理下的编辑准确性两大缺陷，采用两阶段训练：首先通过编辑轨迹监督微调对齐推理步骤；其次利用视觉语言模型奖励提升编辑质量与下游准确率。在五个视觉推理任务上，ETCHR在多个大模型（如Qwen3-VL-8B、Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite、Kimi K2.5）中均提升Pass@1 4.6–5.5个百分点，且无需重新训练底层模型。
Is Capability a Liability? More Capable Language Models Make Worse Forecasts When It Matters Most
Authors: Nick Merrill, Jaeho Lee, Ezra Karger
First: 2026-05-21T16:14:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:57:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We document inverse scaling in LLMs on forecasting problems whose underlying time series exhibit superlinear growth and tail risk of regime change, a structure common in finance and epidemiology. On these tasks, more capable models produce worse distributional forecasts. The pattern appears on ForecastBench-Sim (FBSim), a contamination-free, simulated-world benchmark we release, in forecasting synthetic SIR epidemics with a matched linear control, and replicates in real-world datasets on COVID-19, measles, housing markets, and hyperinflation. A per-quantile decomposition shows the failure concentrates at the upper tail, which more capable models shift upward to track aggressive extrapolations of growth, while the lower tail stays put. A within-family study of Llama-3.1 shows that both model scale and post-training independently contribute to this effect. Domain knowledge does not reliably rescue calibration. This inverse scaling does not appear on single-threshold metrics common in LLM forecasting benchmarks, reversing the sign of the capability--accuracy relationship on identical outputs. Single-threshold scoring at conventional cutoffs misses the upper-tail cost; tail-inclusive scoring reverses the sign of the capability--accuracy relationship on the same outputs. We recommend that LLM forecasting evaluations use continuous (and unbounded) measures of accuracy alongside bounded binary threshold metrics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：能力是否是一种负担？在最关键的时候，能力更强的语言模型反而产生更差的预测
我们在具有超线性增长和制度转变尾部风险的时间序列预测问题上，观察到大型语言模型（LLM）的反向缩放现象，这种结构在金融和流行病学中很常见。在这些任务中，能力更强的模型生成的分布预测更差。该模式在我们发布的无污染模拟世界基准ForecastBench-Sim（FBSim）上显现，用于预测具有匹配线性控制的合成SIR流行病，并在真实世界数据集（如COVID-19、麻疹、房地产市场和恶性通货膨胀）中得到重现。按分位数分解显示，失败集中在上尾部，能力更强的模型将上尾向上移动以追踪激进的增长外推，而下尾保持不变。对Llama-3.1系列模型的族内研究显示，模型规模和后训练均独立贡献于这一效应。领域知识无法可靠地挽救校准效果。这种反向缩放现象在LLM预测基准中常见的单阈值指标上不出现，从而在相同输出上逆转了能力-准确率关系。在传统截止值下的单阈值评分忽略了上尾成本；包含尾部的评分则在相同输出上逆转了能力-准确率关系。我们建议LLM预测评估应同时使用连续（且无界）的准确率度量和有界二元阈值指标。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that highly capable language models perform worse on forecasting tasks involving superlinear growth and regime change risk—common in finance and epidemiology—the study investigates inverse scaling in LLMs using a simulated benchmark (ForecastBench-Sim) and real-world datasets including COVID-19, measles, housing, and hyperinflation. The method involves comparing forecast distributions across model scales and post-training, with per-quantile decomposition revealing that failure concentrates in the upper tail, where larger models shift forecasts upward to extrapolate aggressively, while the lower tail remains stable. Results show that both model scale and post-training contribute to this degradation, and domain knowledge does not reliably improve calibration. Crucially, single-threshold metrics mask the upper-tail cost, while tail-inclusive scoring reverses the capability-accuracy relationship, suggesting evaluations should use continuous accuracy measures alongside binary thresholds.
该研究探讨为何能力更强的语言模型在具有超线性增长和制度突变风险的领域（如金融与流行病学）中反而预测效果更差。通过模拟基准（ForecastBench-Sim）及真实数据集（如新冠、住房市场），作者发现更大、更强大的模型在分布预测上系统性表现更差，尤其在上尾部分。分位数分析表明，这些模型在高分位数处向上偏移以跟随激进外推，而下尾则保持稳定。该效应在模型家族内普遍存在，即使引入领域知识也难以缓解，且仅在包含尾部的评分方式下显现，传统单阈值指标无法捕捉这一问题。作者建议评估应同时使用连续无界准确率指标与二元阈值指标。
From Activation to Causality: Discovery of Causal Visual Representations in the Human Brain
Authors: Yuval Golbari, Navve Wasserman, Matias Cosarinsky, Roman Beliy, Aude Oliva, Antonio Torralba, Michal Irani, Tamar Rott Shaham
First: 2026-05-22T17:56:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:56:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Identifying which brain regions represent a visual concept in the human brain is a central challenge in neuroscience. Existing approaches have localized coarse functional regions (e.g., faces, places) through activation maximization, identifying regions that activate strongly for a target concept relative to other concepts. Yet strong activation alone does not establish that a region represents the concept itself, as responses may instead be driven by correlated visual or semantic cues. We introduce BrainCause, an automated framework that combines generative and brain models to synthesize controlled stimuli and validate neural representations through targeted causal testing. Given a query specifying a concept of interest, our framework constructs targeted stimulus sets comprising concept images, counterfactual edits that remove the target concept while preserving other image content, and images with candidate correlated distractors. It then uses an image-to-fMRI encoding model to predict brain responses and searches for representations that respond specifically to the target concept over correlated alternatives. BrainCause returns validated candidate representations and proposes follow-up fMRI experiments to further test or extend its discoveries. Our approach successfully recovers known functional localizations and identifies new candidate representations across dozens of concepts, validated on both predicted and measured fMRI data. Critically, we show that without causal validation, a large fraction of localizations would be false positives, confirming that activation alone is insufficient evidence of representation.
Summary / 总结
Identifying which brain regions represent a visual concept in the human brain is a central challenge in neuroscience.
为区分大脑中真正的概念表征与单纯的激活模式，作者提出了BrainCause框架，结合生成模型与fMRI编码模型进行因果验证。给定目标概念，该框架生成包含概念图像、移除目标概念但保留其他内容的反事实图像，以及具有相关特征的干扰图像。随后利用图像到fMRI的预测模型，筛选出仅对目标概念产生特异性响应的脑区。在数十个概念上测试表明，该方法成功恢复已知功能定位并发现新候选区，且在预测与实测fMRI数据中均得到验证。关键发现是，若无因果验证，大量激活结果将为假阳性，证明仅靠激活无法确认表征。
Complete-muE: Optimal Hyperparameter Transfer and Scaling for MoE Models
Authors: Hongwu Peng, Ohiremen Dibua, Yuanjun Xiong, Yifan Gong, Jianming Zhang, Yan Kang
First: 2026-05-22T17:56:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:56:13+00:00
Comments: 27 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose Complete-muE, a framework which targets hyperparameter transfer across dense FFN and any Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) setups in transformer blocks. Existing tools such as $μ$P (requires fixed architectue) or SDE (requires fixed per-step token count) cannot directly solve the hyperparameter transfer problem in MoE setups because Dense to MoE transfer or MoE total experts scaling changes both architecture and tokens per expert. Complete-muE solves this challenge with a two-bridge system: Bridge~I maps between dense FFN and Dense MoE by active-width $μ$P with a normalized router scale. Bridge~II maps between Dense MoE and sparse MoE by activated-expert scaling, where the first-order SDE LR/WD correction cancels while a bounded residual $σ_0$ shift remains. The resulting transfer rule, which we term as Complete muE, covers changes in activated experts, total capacity, granularity, and shared/group-balanced hybrids for MoE models as well as network width/depth, batch size, and duration changes for general Transformer models. Extensive language model and diffusion model pretraining experiments confirm that complete-muE yields relatively stable hyperparameter optima across model architectures and parameter counts -- with only minor drift consistent with the non-strict SDE behavior of Bridge~II. In practice this drift is small enough that hyperparameters tuned on a single dense reference transfer near-optimally to all MoE configurations -- \emph{tune dense once, transfer to all} is the practical recipe at the core of Complete-muE. This enables MoE models to achieve accelerated convergence speedup over dense models when scaling model capacity without costly hyperparameter search.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Complete-muE：面向MoE模型的最优超参数迁移与扩展框架
我们提出Complete-muE框架，旨在实现稠密前馈网络（FFN）与任意混合专家（MoE）架构在Transformer模块间的超参数迁移。现有工具如μP（要求固定架构）或SDE（要求固定每步token数）无法直接解决MoE架构中的超参数迁移问题，因为从稠密到MoE的迁移或MoE总专家数扩展会同时改变架构和每专家token数。Complete-muE通过双桥系统解决此挑战：Bridge I通过带归一化路由缩放的激活宽度μP，映射稠密FFN与稠密MoE；Bridge II通过激活专家缩放映射稠密MoE与稀疏MoE，其中一阶SDE学习率/权重衰减修正项被抵消，仅保留有界残差σ₀偏移。所得迁移规则即Complete muE，覆盖MoE模型中激活专家数、总容量、粒度及共享/分组平衡混合架构的变化，以及通用Transformer模型中网络宽度/深度、批量大小和训练时长的变化。大量语言模型与扩散模型预训练实验表明，Complete-muE能在不同模型架构与参数量下保持相对稳定的超参数最优值——仅存在与Bridge II非严格SDE行为一致的微小漂移。实践中该漂移极小，使得在单一稠密参考模型上调优的超参数可近乎最优地迁移到所有MoE配置——即Complete-muE的核心实践原则为“稠密模型调优一次，迁移至所有MoE配置”。这使MoE模型在扩展模型容量时，无需昂贵的超参数搜索即可实现比稠密模型更快的收敛速度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of transferring hyperparameters from dense feedforward networks to flexible Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures, the paper introduces Complete-muE, a two-bridge framework that enables optimal hyperparameter scaling across diverse MoE configurations and general Transformer setups. Bridge I translates dense FFN to dense MoE using active-width μP with normalized router scaling, while Bridge II handles transitions between dense and sparse MoE via activated-expert scaling, incorporating first-order SDE corrections and a bounded residual shift. Experimental results across language and diffusion models show that Complete-muE maintains stable hyperparameter optima despite changes in expert count, capacity, granularity, and network dimensions, with only minor drift attributable to Bridge II’s non-strict SDE behavior. This allows practitioners to tune hyperparameters on a single dense model and transfer them nearly optimally to all MoE variants, enabling accelerated convergence during scaling without costly re-tuning.
为解决从稠密前馈网络向灵活的专家混合（MoE）架构迁移超参数的难题，本文提出Complete-muE框架，通过双桥结构实现跨多种MoE配置及通用Transformer架构的最优超参数缩放。第一桥通过激活宽度μP与归一化路由缩放，将稠密FFN映射至稠密MoE；第二桥通过激活专家缩放处理稠密与稀疏MoE间的转换，结合一阶SDE校正与有界残差偏移。实验表明，该框架在专家数量、容量、粒度及网络维度变化下仍能保持超参数最优值的稳定性，仅存在微小偏差（源于第二桥的非严格SDE行为）。这使得用户只需在单一稠密模型上调参，即可近似最优地迁移到所有MoE变体，从而在扩展模型容量时实现加速收敛，无需耗时的超参数搜索。
Good Token Hunting: A Hitchhiker's Guide to Token Selection for Visual Geometry Transformers
Authors: Shuhong Zheng, Michael Oechsle, Erik Sandström, Marie-Julie Rakotosaona, Federico Tombari, Igor Gilitschenski
First: 2026-05-22T17:55:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:55:13+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://zsh2000.github.io/good-token-hunting.github.io, Code: https://github.com/zsh2000/gotohunt
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Visual geometry transformers have become powerful architectures for multi-view 3D reconstruction, enabling joint prediction of multiple 3D attributes in a feed-forward manner. However, their computational cost grows quadratically with the input sequence length due to the global attention layers inside these models. This limits both their scalability and efficiency. In this work, we address this challenge with a simple yet general strategy: restricting the number of key/value tokens that each query interacts with during global attention. To achieve effective token selection, we introduce a two-stage framework. First, an inter-frame selection step operates at the frame level to identify frames that should be preserved. Second, an intra-frame selection step further discards more redundant tokens within the selected frames. Our analysis highlights the advantage of a diversity-based strategy for inter-frame selection, which ensures broad coverage of the scene. For intra-frame selection, we show that layer-aware sparsification is necessary, with the selection process guided by the entropy of the global attention pattern. Our approach offers a superior speed-accuracy trade-off compared to existing solutions. Extensive experiments show that it accelerates visual geometry transformers by over 85% for scenes with 500 images while maintaining, or even improving, baseline performance, which hints that how our token selection strategy can play a crucial role in future applications of visual geometry transformers. Our project website is available at https://zsh2000.github.io/good-token-hunting.github.io.
中文标题/摘要
标题：好的令牌狩猎：视觉几何变换器令牌选择的 hitchhiker 指南
视觉几何变换器已成为多视角 3D 重建的强大架构，能够以前馈方式联合预测多个 3D 属性。然而，由于模型内部的全局注意力层，其计算成本随输入序列长度呈二次增长，限制了其可扩展性和效率。本文提出一种简单而通用的策略：限制每个查询在全局注意力中交互的键/值令牌数量。为实现有效的令牌选择，我们引入两阶段框架：第一阶段在帧级别进行帧间选择，识别应保留的帧；第二阶段在选定帧内进一步剔除冗余令牌。分析表明，基于多样性的帧间选择策略可确保场景广泛覆盖；对于帧内选择，我们证明层感知稀疏化是必要的，选择过程由全局注意力模式的熵引导。本方法相比现有方案提供更优的速度-精度权衡。大量实验表明，对于 500 张图像的场景，该方法可将视觉几何变换器加速 85% 以上，同时保持或提升基线性能，暗示其令牌选择策略在未来视觉几何变换器应用中将发挥关键作用。项目网站：https://zsh2000.github.io/good-token-hunting.github.io。
Summary / 总结
To address the quadratic computational cost of visual geometry transformers caused by global attention layers, this work proposes a token selection strategy that restricts query-key interactions. It employs a two-stage framework: first, an inter-frame selection identifies critical frames based on scene diversity, then an intra-frame selection removes redundant tokens within those frames using layer-aware sparsification guided by attention entropy. Experiments show the method accelerates 3D reconstruction by over 85% on 500-image scenes while preserving or improving accuracy, demonstrating its effectiveness in scaling visual geometry transformers.
为解决视觉几何变换器因全局注意力层导致的计算复杂度随输入序列长度二次增长的问题，本文提出一种两阶段的令牌选择策略：首先在帧级筛选关键帧以保留场景信息，其次在帧内进一步剔除冗余令牌。帧间选择强调多样性以覆盖场景全局，帧内选择则依据注意力熵进行分层稀疏化。实验表明，该方法在500张图像场景下可提速85%以上，同时保持或提升基线精度，展现出其在视觉几何变换器规模化应用中的潜力。
MedVIGIL: Evaluating Trustworthy Medical VLMs Under Broken Visual Evidence
Authors: Hanqi Jiang, Junhao Chen, Mingyu Kang, Hyeokjae Kwon, Yi Pan, Lifeng Chen, Weihang You, Haozhen Gong, Ruiyu Yan, Jinglei Lv, Lin Zhao, Hui Ren, Quanzheng Li, Tianming Liu, Xiang Li
First: 2026-05-08T15:55:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:54:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Medical vision--language models (VLMs) are usually evaluated on intact image--question pairs, but trustworthy clinical use requires a stronger property: a model must recognise when the evidential basis for an answer has failed. We study this through silent failures under perturbed evidence, where a vision-required medical question is paired with a false premise, wording perturbation, knowledge-only rewrite, or ROI-corrupted image, yet the model returns a fluent non-refusal answer. We introduce medvigil, a 300-case evaluation suite drawn from four public medical VQA sources, supervised end to end by four board-certified radiologists: every gold answer, refusal option, candidate-answer set, paraphrase, false-premise trap, ROI box, and clinical risk tier is clinician-authored. Two attending radiologists annotate every case in parallel, a senior radiologist consolidates the released manifest, and a separate fourth radiologist independent of construction answers every probe to provide the human reference baseline. The release contains 2556 MCQ probes, 240 counterfactual triplets, physician-adjudicated risk-tier and answerability flags, ROI boxes, and a paired open-ended variant. We report seven correctness-conditioned audit metrics that summarise into the medvigil Composite Score (MCS), and audit 16 vision-capable models plus two text-only baselines. The independent radiologist scores MCS 83.3 at silent-failure rate 5.8%, leaving a 14.1-point composite headroom above the strongest audited model (Claude Opus 4.7 at 69.2). The benchmark and evaluation harness are publicly released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MedVIGIL：在视觉证据失效情况下评估可信的医疗视觉-语言模型
医疗视觉-语言模型（VLMs）通常在完整的图像-问题对上进行评估，但可信的临床应用需要更强的属性：模型必须能识别当答案的证据基础失效时。我们通过在扰动证据下的“静默失败”进行研究，即视觉必需的医疗问题与错误前提、措辞扰动、仅知识重写或ROI受损图像配对，但模型仍返回流畅的非拒绝答案。我们引入medvigil，一个从四个公开医疗VQA数据源中抽取的300例评估套件，由四位认证放射科医生全程监督：每个黄金答案、拒绝选项、候选答案集、改写句、错误前提陷阱、ROI框和临床风险等级均由临床医生编写。两位主治放射科医生并行标注每个案例，资深放射科医生整合发布清单，第四位独立于构建过程的放射科医生对每个探针进行独立评分，以提供人类参考基准。发布内容包含2556个多项选择题探针、240个反事实三元组、医生裁定的风险等级和可回答性标志、ROI框及配对开放式变体。我们报告七项基于正确性的审计指标，汇总为medvigil综合得分（MCS），并审计16个视觉能力模型及两个纯文本基线模型。独立放射科医生在静默失败率为5.8%时获得MCS 83.3，比最强审计模型（Claude Opus 4.7，69.2）高出14.1分。该基准和评估工具已公开发布。

To address the lack of robustness in medical vision-language models (VLMs) when visual evidence is compromised, the paper introduces MedVIGIL, a 300-case benchmark curated by four board-certified radiologists to evaluate models’ ability to detect silent failures—cases where models answer fluently despite false premises, image corruption, or wording perturbations. The dataset includes MCQs, counterfactual triplets, risk tiers, and ROI annotations, all clinically validated. An independent radiologist scored 83.3 MCS with a 5.8% silent-failure rate, outperforming the best model (Claude Opus at 69.2), leaving a 14.1-point gap in performance.
为评估医疗视觉-语言模型在视觉证据受损时的可靠性，作者构建了MedVIGIL基准数据集，包含300个由四名认证放射科医生标注的病例，用于检测模型在视觉输入被篡改（如虚假前提、ROI破坏或措辞扰动）时是否仍能给出流畅但错误的答案（即‘沉默失败’）。数据集包含多项选择题、反事实三元组、风险等级及人工标注的拒绝选项，另有一名独立放射科医生提供基准评分。在评估16个视觉模型和两个纯文本基线时，研究报告了七项审计指标，汇总为MedVIGIL综合得分（MCS）。人类基准得分为83.3（沉默失败率5.8%），而最强模型（Claude Opus）仅69.2，差距达14.1分，凸显医疗VLM在鲁棒性方面的不足。
Smart-Insertion-V: Photorealistic Video Insertion via a Closed-Loop Feedback Dual-Stream Framework
Authors: Xiao Cao, Yansong Qu, Xiangzhen, Chang, Wen Xiao, Jiakui Hu, Heyuan Li, Jialun Liu, Zhiyong Huang, Xuelong Li
First: 2026-05-22T17:54:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:54:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mask-free video object insertion has emerged as a challenging task, requiring harmonious integration of reference objects into source videos. However, existing methods struggle when references exhibit severe stylistic domain gaps with the source scene. To overcome this, we propose \textit{\textbf{Smart-Insertion-V}}, an end-to-end \textbf{Dual-Stream} framework that concurrently conducts video insertion and image style transfer. Within this framework, the image stream synchronously guides the video generation process, while a \textbf{Closed-loop Feedback} mechanism is further incorporated to ensure robust insertion. Inevitably, integrating these diverse conditioning signals results in feature entanglement and style leakage. To tackle this issue, we design \textbf{Dual-World-View RoPE} to distinguish different signals via spatial-temporal offsets without incurring heavy training overhead. Furthermore, to facilitate spatial grounding and stylistic adaptation, we introduce a \textbf{Decoupled Guidance Module} that leverages a Vision-Language Model for semantic reasoning while preserving original temporal guidance with native text encoder. To bridge data gap for harmonious reference insertion task, we propose a data curation pipeline and will release an \textbf{open-source dataset}. Experiments demonstrate that our method can insert objects into plausible positions while achieving the most harmonious results.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of inserting objects into videos without masks when the reference style differs significantly from the source, Smart-Insertion-V introduces a dual-stream framework that simultaneously performs video insertion and image style transfer, guided by a closed-loop feedback mechanism. To prevent feature entanglement and style leakage from mixed conditioning signals, it employs Dual-World-View RoPE to separate spatial-temporal signals via offsets without heavy training costs, and a decoupled guidance module that uses a vision-language model for semantic reasoning while preserving temporal cues via a native text encoder. Experimental results show that this approach achieves the most harmonious and plausible object insertions across diverse stylistic domains, outperforming existing methods in visual coherence and spatial grounding.
为解决无掩码视频对象插入中因风格域差异大导致的插入效果不佳问题，Smart-Insertion-V 提出一种双流闭环反馈框架，同步执行视频插入与图像风格迁移。该框架通过 Dual-World-View RoPE 机制利用时空偏移区分不同条件信号，避免训练开销，并引入解耦引导模块，结合视觉-语言模型进行语义推理，同时保留原始时间引导。实验表明，该方法在风格差异显著的场景下仍能实现更自然、更和谐的对象插入效果。
Divergent Paths to Depolarization: Dialogue Design Determines the Prosocial Benefits of AI-Assisted Political Argumentation
Authors: Jianlong Zhu, Syed Muhammad Jhon Raza Naqvi, Carolin-Theresa Ziemer, Usman Naseem, Ingmar Weber
First: 2026-05-22T17:51:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:51:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Argumentative dialogues across political divides can reduce polarization, yet opportunities for citizens to engage with opposing views in accessible and structured ways remain limited. AI dialogue partners offer a scalable framework for such open-mindedness exercises, but how the format of human-AI dialogues shapes their benefits remains unclear. In a two-session online experiment, 469 US participants were assigned to argue either for or against their own attitude on a contested political issue with an AI chatbot. Our experimental findings show attitude-congruent dialogues produced greater immediate reduction in both affective and opinion polarization than attitude-incongruent dialogues. By contrast, attitude-incongruent dialogues elicited weaker cognitive state empathy than the non-AI reference task but increased cognitive trait empathy in the two-week period between sessions, suggesting the effects of active generation of attitude-incongruent arguments may emerge over time. These findings highlight dialogue design as a key determinant of effective AI-mediated behavioral interventions.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce political polarization through structured dialogue, this study tested how AI-assisted argumentation formats influence prosocial outcomes. In a two-session online experiment with 469 U.S. participants, subjects engaged in dialogues with an AI chatbot either aligned or opposed to their own views on a political issue. Results showed that attitude-congruent dialogues led to greater immediate reductions in both affective and opinion polarization, while attitude-incongruent dialogues, though less effective immediately, increased cognitive trait empathy over time, suggesting delayed benefits from challenging one’s own position.
为减少政治极化，本研究探讨了AI辅助辩论形式对亲社会效果的影响。在一项包含469名美国参与者的双阶段在线实验中，参与者与AI聊天机器人就支持或反对自身立场进行辩论。结果表明，态度一致的对话在短期内显著降低情感与观点极化，而态度不一致的对话虽即时效果较弱，但在两周期间后提升了认知特质共情，说明与对立观点互动可能带来延时性益处。
HorizonStream: Long-Horizon Attention for Streaming 3D Reconstruction
Authors: Chong Cheng, Peilin Tao, Nanjie Yao, Guanzhi Ding, Xianda Chen, Yuansen Du, Xiaoyang Guo, Wei Yin, Weiqiang Ren, Qian Zhang, Zhengqing Chen, Hao Wang
First: 2026-05-22T17:50:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:50:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Online 3D reconstruction requires estimating camera pose and scene geometry under strict causal and bounded-memory constraints. Existing methods often suffer from drift, jitter, or collapse on long sequences. We trace these failures to a fundamental mismatch. Streaming geometry is inherently temporally heterogeneous, with evidence ranging from short-lived correspondences to persistent global scale. However, current architectures impose uniform and pathological influence patterns. For example, sliding windows enforce hard cutoffs, while ungated recurrence and causal attention cause cache saturation and spike-like attention sinks. To resolve this, we formalize geometric propagation as an \emph{evidence influence kernel} and propose HorizonStream, a long-horizon Transformer that explicitly factorizes this kernel. For the long-range temporal factor, Geometric Linear Attention learns channel-wise decay rates to enable bounded, multi-timescale propagation of geometric evidence. For the short-range spatial factor, Geometric Local Attention with Spatiotemporal RoPE performs reliable 3D matching while suppressing attention sinks. Finally, Metric Readout Tokens recover stable scale and rigid pose directly from the persistent geometric state. Extensive experiments show that HorizonStream, trained on only 48-frame clips, generalizes stably to sequences exceeding 10,000\ frames with constant memory and linear time, achieving state-of-the-art streaming 3D reconstruction performance. Project Page: https://3dagentworld.github.io/horizonstream/
中文标题/摘要
标题：HorizonStream：面向流式3D重建的长时程注意力机制
在线3D重建需在严格的因果性和有限内存约束下估计相机位姿与场景几何。现有方法在长序列中常出现漂移、抖动或坍塌问题。我们将其归因于根本性不匹配：流式几何本质上具有时间异质性，证据范围从短暂对应到持久全局尺度。但当前架构强制采用统一且病态的影响模式，例如滑动窗口强制硬截断，无门控递归与因果注意力导致缓存饱和和突刺式注意力沉降。为解决此问题，我们将几何传播形式化为“证据影响核”，并提出HorizonStream——一种显式分解该核的长时程Transformer。对于长程时间因子，几何线性注意力学习通道级衰减率，实现有界、多尺度几何证据传播；对于短程空间因子，时空RoPE的几何局部注意力实现可靠3D匹配并抑制注意力沉降。最后，度量读出标记直接从持久几何状态恢复稳定尺度与刚体位姿。大量实验表明，仅用48帧片段训练的HorizonStream，可稳定泛化至超10,000帧序列，保持常量内存与线性时间复杂度，实现当前最优流式3D重建性能。项目页面：https://3dagentworld.github.io/horizonstream/

Motivated by the failure of existing methods to handle long sequences in streaming 3D reconstruction due to drift, jitter, or collapse, the authors propose HorizonStream, a long-horizon Transformer that models geometric evidence propagation as an evidence influence kernel. It combines Geometric Linear Attention for multi-timescale, bounded temporal propagation and Geometric Local Attention with Spatiotemporal RoPE for reliable 3D matching while avoiding attention sinks, complemented by Metric Readout Tokens for stable scale and pose estimation. Experiments show that HorizonStream, trained on short 48-frame clips, generalizes to sequences over 10,000 frames with constant memory and linear time, outperforming prior methods in streaming 3D reconstruction.
为解决流式3D重建中因注意力机制僵化导致的漂移、抖动或坍塌问题，该研究提出HorizonStream架构，将几何传播形式化为证据影响核并显式分解为长时序与短空间两个因子。长时序部分采用几何线性注意力，学习通道级衰减率以实现多尺度、有界传播；短空间部分结合时空RoPE的局部注意力，实现稳定匹配并抑制注意力突降。最终通过度量读出标记直接提取稳定尺度与刚体位姿。实验表明，仅用48帧训练的模型可稳定泛化至超10,000帧序列，保持常量内存与线性时间复杂度，性能优于现有方法。
GenRecon: Bridging Generative Priors for Multi-View 3D Scene Reconstruction
Authors: Katharina Schmid, Nicolas von Lützow, Jozef Hladký, Angela Dai, Matthias Nießner
First: 2026-05-22T17:49:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:49:59+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://kasothaphie.github.io/GenRecon/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We introduce a new approach to high-fidelity 3D scene reconstruction from multi-view RGB images that tightly couples reconstruction with a strong generative 3D prior. We cast scene reconstruction as conditional 3D generation over a set of spatially-localized, overlapping chunks that together tile the scene, scaling generation to large scene extents. Crucially, we inherit the fidelity and completeness of state-of-the-art generative shape models -- we use Trellis.2 as an example -- which we generalize to the scene level. To this end, we propose a projection-based conditioning mechanism that lifts posed multi-view image features into a coherent 3D representation aligned with the generative model, independent of view ordering and spatially anchored to the scene, yielding high-fidelity, multi-view consistent generated geometry. This enables lifting the strong object-level prior of Trellis.2 to multi-view, scene-scale generation, producing faithful, editable PBR mesh reconstructions of indoor environments. As a result, we obtain high-fidelity results that outperform cutting-edge reconstruction methods by 16%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GenRecon：利用生成先验桥接多视角3D场景重建
我们提出了一种新的方法，从多视角RGB图像实现高保真3D场景重建，该方法紧密耦合重建与强生成3D先验。我们将场景重建视为在一组空间局部重叠的块上进行条件3D生成，这些块共同覆盖整个场景，从而扩展生成范围至大型场景。关键在于，我们继承了当前最先进的生成形状模型（如Trellis.2）的保真度与完整性，并将其推广至场景级别。为此，我们提出了一种基于投影的条件机制，将带姿态的多视角图像特征提升为与生成模型对齐的连贯3D表示，独立于视角顺序且空间锚定于场景，从而生成高保真、多视角一致的几何结构。这使得Trellis.2的强物体级先验得以扩展至多视角、场景级生成，从而生成忠实且可编辑的PBR网格重建的室内环境。结果表明，我们的方法在保真度上优于当前最先进重建方法16%。

Experimental results show that this approach produces high-fidelity, editable PBR mesh reconstructions of indoor scenes, outperforming state-of-the-art methods by 16% in reconstruction quality.
