CHRONOS: Temporally-Aware Multi-Agent Coordination for Evolving Data Marketplaces
Authors: Joydeep Chandra
First: 2026-05-22T17:47:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:47:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Temporal knowledge-graph data marketplaces face three coupled failures in static designs: stale hybrid index shortcuts reduce recall as edges evolve, stationary Shapley pricing misattributes value after distribution shifts, and uncoordinated agents over-consume a shared differential-privacy budget. We present CHRONOS, a three-layer architecture providing a unified treatment of these challenges with explicit public and private separation. Layer one applies neural-ODE temporal decay to shortcut edges, providing a per-query expected recall-loss bound of Big-O of Pq lambda delta t, with a monotone-envelope guarantee reducing bound looseness to 1.8 to 3.2 times observed loss. Layer two conditions Shapley valuation on detected changepoints and provides finite-sample error guarantees under noise. Layer three uses EXP3-IX to achieve Big-O of the square root of T log T regret while enforcing epsilon and delta differential privacy via moments accounting. CHRONOS releases a privatized affinity matrix per epoch using the Gaussian mechanism; all retrieval and ranking are post-processing, incurring no extra privacy cost. We provide multi-epoch settlement, scalability analysis for 500 sellers, and comparisons against accelerated baselines. Across four benchmarks, CHRONOS shows 0.937 recall at ten, 2.74 queries per second, 161 ms latency, and total epsilon of 4.25 at delta of 10 to the power of negative 6 under zCDP composition. These results indicate a competitive operating point. A limitation is that at this privacy level, released valuations remain noise-dominated; utility derives primarily from public index routing and adaptive scheduling driven by low-sensitivity statistics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CHRONOS：面向演化数据市场的时序感知多智能体协调
静态设计的时序知识图谱数据市场面临三类耦合失效：过时的混合索引捷径随边演化导致召回率下降，静态Shapley定价在分布偏移后误归价值，且未协调的智能体过度消耗共享差分隐私预算。我们提出CHRONOS，一种三层架构，通过显式公私分离统一处理这些挑战。第一层采用神经ODE时序衰减处理捷径边，为每查询提供预期召回损失上界为O(Pq λ δt)，并以单调包络保证将上界松弛度降至观测损失的1.8至3.2倍。第二层根据检测到的突变点调整Shapley估值，并在噪声下提供有限样本误差保证。第三层采用EXP3-IX实现O(√T log T)的遗憾，同时通过矩会计法强制满足ε和δ差分隐私。CHRONOS每周期通过高斯机制发布私有化亲和矩阵；所有检索与排序均为后处理，不增加额外隐私成本。我们提供多周期结算、500卖家规模的可扩展性分析，并与加速基线对比。在四个基准测试中，CHRONOS在十召回率0.937，每秒2.74查询，延迟161毫秒，zCDP组合下δ=10⁻⁶时总ε=4.25。结果表明其具有竞争力的运行点。局限在于该隐私水平下，发布估值仍受噪声主导；效用主要源于公共索引路由及由低敏感性统计驱动的自适应调度。
Summary / 总结
Temporal knowledge-graph data marketplaces face three coupled failures in static designs: stale hybrid index shortcuts reduce recall as edges evolve, stationary Shapley pricing misattributes value after distribution shifts, and uncoordinated agents over-consume a shared differential-privacy budget.
Multilingual Knowledge Transfer under Data Constraints via Lexical Interventions
Authors: Anastasiia Sedova, Natalie Schluter, Skyler Seto, Maartje ter Hoeve
First: 2026-05-22T17:45:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:45:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cross-lingual knowledge transfer is critical for building high-performing multilingual language models for languages with insufficient training data. When target language data is scarce, the knowledge required for many downstream tasks involving scientific reasoning, commonsense inference, and world knowledge must be acquired primarily from the high-resource language, making effective knowledge transfer essential. Existing methods for improving such cross-lingual knowledge transfer require large amounts of parallel data, translation systems, auxiliary models, or additional training stages that are largely unavailable for many languages. We propose LINK - a data-level intervention method that improves knowledge transfer during model pretraining through lexical substitutions in high-resource part of pretraining data using bilingual vocabularies. For a given replacement ratio, randomly selected words in a portion of the high-resource (English) training corpus are swapped with their word-level translations, requiring no additional model training and only a bilingual vocabulary, which can be obtained at near-zero cost for virtually any language. Evaluation on eight languages across five model sizes shows notable improvements on downstream tasks in the target language, with up to a 2x speedup in training to reach equivalent performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过词汇干预在数据约束下实现多语言知识迁移
跨语言知识迁移对于构建在训练数据不足的语言上表现优异的多语言语言模型至关重要。当目标语言数据稀缺时，许多涉及科学推理、常识推断和世界知识的下游任务所需的知识必须主要从高资源语言中获取，因此有效的知识迁移至关重要。现有改进此类跨语言知识迁移的方法通常需要大量平行数据、翻译系统、辅助模型或额外的训练阶段，而这些对许多语言而言大多不可用。我们提出LINK——一种数据层面的干预方法，通过在高资源部分的预训练数据中进行词汇替换（使用双语词汇表），在模型预训练期间提升知识迁移效果。对于给定的替换比例，随机选取高资源（英语）训练语料库的一部分词汇，替换为其词级翻译，无需额外模型训练，仅需一个双语词汇表，几乎可为任何语言以近零成本获得。在五种模型规模、八种语言上的评估显示，该方法在目标语言的下游任务上取得显著提升，训练速度最高可达两倍，以达到同等性能。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of transferring knowledge from high-resource to low-resource languages under data constraints, the paper proposes LINK, a lexical intervention method that substitutes random words in the high-resource training corpus with their translations using a bilingual vocabulary, without requiring additional model training or parallel data. Evaluated across eight languages and five model sizes, LINK consistently improved downstream task performance in target languages, with gains up to 2x faster training time to reach equivalent accuracy.
为解决在数据受限条件下从高资源语言向低资源语言迁移知识的难题，本文提出LINK方法，通过在高资源语料中随机替换词汇为对应翻译词（利用双语词汇表）来干预预训练过程，无需额外训练或平行语料。在八种语言和五种模型规模的实验中，LINK显著提升了目标语言下游任务表现，部分场景下训练速度提升达两倍。
PGT: Procedurally Generated Tasks for improving visual grounding in MLLMs
Authors: Rim Assouel, Amir Bar, Michal Drozdzal, Adriana Romero-Soriano
First: 2026-05-22T17:45:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:45:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite remarkable progress in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), these models still struggle with fine-grained understanding tasks. In this work, we propose Procedurally Generated Tasks (PGT), a simple data-driven framework that serves a dual purpose: inducing fine-grained visual understanding and acting as a low-cost diagnostic tool to identify the source of perception failures. By overlaying unambiguous geometric primitives on images, PGT generate additional dense supervision that disentangles visual grounding capability from semantic priors. Extensive experiments on relational, quantitative, and 3D/depth understanding benchmarks show that PGT yields remarkable gains across diverse architectures. Instruction tuning MLLMs on LLaVA-v1.5-Instruct augmented with PGT data results in improvements of up to +20% on the What'sUp benchmark and +13.3% on CV-Bench-2D, while maintaining general perception capabilities. Moreover, finetuning state-of-the-art MLLMs on PGT data leads to boosts of up to +5.5% on What'sUp and +8.3% on CV-Bench-2D. These findings demonstrate that PGT effectively address the bottleneck of fine-grained perception, revealing that many spatial reasoning deficits stem from inadequate supervision signals rather than inherent architectural or resolution limitations.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent challenges MLLMs face in fine-grained visual understanding, this work introduces Procedurally Generated Tasks (PGT), a data-driven framework that overlays unambiguous geometric primitives on images to generate dense supervision signals. This approach disentangles visual grounding from semantic priors and serves as a diagnostic tool for perception failures. Experiments across relational, quantitative, and 3D/depth benchmarks show that instruction-tuning LLaVA-v1.5-Instruct with PGT data improves performance by up to +20% on What'sUp and +13.3% on CV-Bench-2D, while fine-tuning state-of-the-art models yields gains of +5.5% and +8.3% respectively, without compromising general perception abilities.
为解决多模态大语言模型在细粒度视觉理解上的瓶颈，本文提出Procedurally Generated Tasks（PGT）框架，通过在图像上叠加明确几何元素生成密集监督信号，从而分离视觉定位与语义先验，并作为感知失败的诊断工具。在关系、量化及3D/深度理解基准测试中，PGT显著提升性能：在What'sUp上指令微调模型提升最高达+20%，在CV-Bench-2D上提升+13.3%；对SOTA模型微调则分别提升+5.5%和+8.3%，且未损害通用感知能力，表明感知缺陷主要源于监督信号不足而非架构或分辨率限制。
RoIt-XMASA: Multi-Domain Multilingual Sentiment Analysis Dataset for Romanian and Italian
Authors: Andrei-Marius Avram, Aureliu Valentin Antonie, Cosmin-Mircea Croitoru, Vlad Andrei Muntean, Dumitru-Clementin Cercel
Venue: AAAI
First: 2026-04-18T20:21:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:43:48+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the International AAAI Conference on Web and Social Media (ICWSM 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present RoIt-XMASA, a multilingual dataset that extends the Cross-lingual Multi-domain Amazon Sentiment Analysis to Italian and Romanian, comprising 36,000 labeled reviews across three domains (books, movies, and music) and 202,141 unlabeled samples. To address cross-lingual and cross-domain challenges, we propose a multi-target adversarial training framework that employs loss reversal with meta-learned coefficients to dynamically balance sentiment discrimination with domain and language invariance. XLM-R achieves an F1-score of 66.23% with our approach, outperforming the baseline by 4.64%. Few-shot evaluation shows that Llama-3.1-8B achieves 58.43% F1-score, revealing a meaningful trade-off between the efficiency of prompting-based approaches and the higher performance of task-specific fine-tuning.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for multilingual and multi-domain sentiment analysis resources in Romanian and Italian, this paper introduces RoIt-XMASA, a dataset with 36,000 labeled reviews across three domains and 202,141 unlabeled samples. To tackle cross-lingual and cross-domain challenges, the authors propose a multi-target adversarial training framework using loss reversal with meta-learned coefficients to balance sentiment discrimination against domain and language invariance. Experiments show that XLM-R achieves 66.23% F1-score, a 4.64% improvement over baseline, while Llama-3.1-8B scores 58.43% in few-shot settings, highlighting a performance-efficiency trade-off between prompting and fine-tuning.
为满足罗马尼亚语和意大利语多语言、多领域情感分析的需求，作者构建了RoIt-XMASA数据集，包含36,000条跨三个领域（书籍、电影、音乐）的标注评论及202,141条未标注样本。他们提出一种多目标对抗训练框架，通过损失反转与元学习系数动态平衡情感判别与领域及语言不变性。实验表明，XLM-R在该框架下F1分数达66.23%，较基线提升4.64%；Llama-3.1-8B在少样本设置下F1分数为58.43%，揭示了提示式方法与任务特定微调在效率与性能间的权衡。
DFIR-DETR: Frequency-Domain Iterative Refinement and Dynamic Feature Aggregation for Small Object Detection
Authors: Bo Gao, Jingcheng Tong, Xingsheng Chen, Han Yu, Zichen Li
First: 2025-12-08T01:25:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:42:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Small object detection in complex scenes exposes a fundamental tension in neural network design: backbone attention distributes computation uniformly regardless of content, pyramid necks inflate activation magnitudes during upsampling without norm compensation, and bottleneck convolutions progressively smooth high-frequency edge components through accumulated spatial filtering. In response, we develop DFIR-DETR by tracing each proposed module back to a specific, measurable deficiency in the RT-DETR baseline: uniform attention that ignores spatial complexity, norm drift that destabilises upsampled features, and spatial convolutions that progressively suppress the high-frequency components small objects depend on. On NEU-DET and VisDrone, DFIR-DETR achieves 92.9% and 51.6% mAP50 with only 11.7M parameters and 47.2 GFLOPs, demonstrating consistent gains across two qualitatively different detection domains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DFIR-DETR：频域迭代优化与动态特征聚合用于小目标检测
复杂场景中小目标检测揭示了神经网络设计中的根本矛盾：骨干注意力无论内容如何均均匀分配计算，金字塔颈部在上采样过程中放大激活幅值却无归一化补偿，瓶颈卷积通过累积空间滤波逐步平滑高频边缘成分。为此，我们开发了DFIR-DETR，将每个模块追溯至RT-DETR基线中的特定可测量缺陷：忽略空间复杂性的均匀注意力、导致上采样特征不稳定的归一化漂移，以及逐步抑制小目标所依赖的高频成分的空间卷积。在NEU-DET和VisDrone数据集上，DFIR-DETR仅用1170万参数和47.2 GFLOPs，分别实现92.9%和51.6%的mAP50，展示了在两个定性不同的检测领域中的一致性能提升。
Summary / 总结
Small object detection in complex scenes exposes a fundamental tension in neural network design: backbone attention distributes computation uniformly regardless of content, pyramid necks inflate activation magnitudes during upsampling without norm compensation, and bottleneck convolutions progressively smooth high-frequency edge components through accumulated spatial filtering.
On the Stability of Spherical Hellinger-Kantorovich Flows and Their Implications for Differential Privacy
Authors: Aratrika Mustafi, Soumya Mukherjee
First: 2026-05-22T17:38:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:38:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Gradient-flow sampling interprets a Gibbs distribution as the minimizer of an energy functional over probability measures and generates dynamics converging to this target. Under spherical Hellinger-Kantorovich (SHK) geometry, the flow couples transport and reaction and coincides with birth-death Langevin dynamics. In this work, we develop a perturbation theory for SHK gradient flows. For two potentials $V$ and $V^{\prime}$, we compare the associated flows from a common initialization and quantify how potential discrepancies propagate over time. A uniform perturbation bound yields dimension-free, pointwise control of the log-likelihood ratio and Rényi divergence, while additional structure allows us to derive bounds for the KL divergence as well. We apply these results to approximate sampling for the exponential mechanism in differential privacy. The likelihood-ratio control provides explicit time-dependent Pure-DP guarantees for SHK-based samplers, while the KL bound yields Approximate-DP certificates via hockey-stick divergence. We also derive a utility bound separating intrinsic exponential-mechanism suboptimality from finite-time sampling error.
中文标题/摘要
标题：球形Hellinger-Kantorovich流的稳定性及其对差分隐私的影响
梯度流采样将Gibbs分布解释为概率测度上能量泛函的最小化器，并生成收敛至该目标的动力学。在球形Hellinger-Kantorovich（SHK）几何下，该流耦合了传输与反应，且等价于生灭Langevin动力学。本文发展了SHK梯度流的扰动理论：对于两个势函数V与V'，我们从共同初始化比较其关联流，并量化势函数差异随时间的传播。统一的扰动界可提供无维、逐点的对数似然比与Rényi散度控制；附加结构还可导出KL散度界。我们将这些结果应用于差分隐私中指数机制的近似采样。似然比控制为SHK采样器提供显式的时变Pure-DP保证，而KL界则通过曲棍球棒散度导出Approximate-DP证书。此外，我们推导了一个效用界，分离指数机制固有次优性与有限时间采样误差。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for stable sampling in differential privacy, this work studies spherical Hellinger-Kantorovich gradient flows, which model Gibbs distributions as energy minimizers and connect to birth-death Langevin dynamics. The authors develop a perturbation theory to analyze how differences in potentials propagate over time, deriving uniform bounds on log-likelihood ratios and Rényi divergence that are dimension-free and pointwise, and extending to KL divergence under additional structure. Applied to exponential mechanism sampling, these bounds yield explicit time-dependent Pure-DP guarantees and Approximate-DP certificates via hockey-stick divergence, while also providing a utility bound that separates mechanism inefficiency from finite-time sampling error.
为分析球形Hellinger-Kantorovich几何下梯度流采样方法的稳定性与差分隐私保障，本文构建了SHK梯度流的扰动理论，比较不同势函数诱导的动态过程，并量化初始差异随时间的传播。方法推导出对数似然比与Rényi散度的统一界，并在附加结构下扩展至KL散度。实验表明，这些界可为SHK采样器提供显式的Pure-DP保证及通过hockey-stick散度获得的Approximate-DP证书，同时分离出指数机制固有次优性与有限时间采样误差的影响。
LaMo: Self-Supervised Latent Motion Priors for Physical Realism in Video Generation
Authors: Bo Jiang, Depu Meng, Yihan Hu, Yichen Xie, Tianshuo Xu, Wei Zhan
First: 2026-05-22T17:34:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:34:42+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://lamo-ai.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Modern video generators produce visually compelling clips but still struggle with physical and motion consistency, limiting their use as reliable world simulators. Existing remedies often rely on external simulators, teacher models, or curated physics-focused data. We explore a complementary self-supervised direction: extracting motion cues from the unlabeled videos already used to train video diffusion models. We propose LaMo, which formulates a latent motion prior over frame-to-frame latent changes conditioned on the current latent and prompt. This prior is exposed through two lightweight readouts: a macro motion drift used during training as a Motion Drift Loss, and a learned micro motion field used during sampling as Motion Prior Guidance. Both components are plug-and-play with existing video diffusion backbones, requiring no architectural or I/O changes. On VideoPhy and VideoPhy2, LaMo improves CogVideoX backbones and outperforms recent physics-aware baselines that use external supervision. On VBench, it preserves overall generation quality while improving motion-related dimensions. These results suggest that unlabeled video contains useful motion supervision for improving physical fidelity in modern video diffusion models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LaMo：用于视频生成中物理真实性的自监督潜在运动先验
现代视频生成器虽能生成视觉上引人注目的片段，但仍难以实现物理与运动一致性，限制其作为可靠世界模拟器的应用。现有解决方案常依赖外部模拟器、教师模型或专门的物理数据集。我们探索一种互补的自监督方法：从已用于训练视频扩散模型的无标签视频中提取运动线索。我们提出LaMo，其在当前潜在表示和提示条件下，对帧间潜在变化建模为运动先验。该先验通过两个轻量级读出模块实现：训练时使用的宏观运动漂移（作为运动漂移损失），以及采样时使用的学得的微观运动场（作为运动先验引导）。两个组件均可无缝集成至现有视频扩散骨干网络，无需架构或输入输出修改。在VideoPhy和VideoPhy2数据集上，LaMo显著提升CogVideoX骨干网络性能，并优于近期依赖外部监督的物理感知基线模型。在VBench数据集上，其保持整体生成质量的同时提升了运动相关维度。结果表明，无标签视频蕴含可用于提升现代视频扩散模型物理真实性的运动监督信号。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance physical realism in video generation without relying on external simulators or curated data, the authors propose LaMo, a self-supervised method that extracts motion priors from unlabeled training videos. LaMo models frame-to-frame latent changes conditioned on current latent and prompt, using two lightweight readouts: a motion drift loss for training and a motion prior guidance for sampling. Experiments show LaMo improves physical consistency in video diffusion models like CogVideoX on VideoPhy and VideoPhy2, outperforming physics-aware baselines with external supervision, while maintaining overall generation quality on VBench.
为提升视频生成中的物理真实性，避免依赖外部模拟器或专用数据，作者提出LaMo方法，从无标签训练视频中自监督提取运动先验。该方法建模帧间潜在变化，基于当前潜在表示与提示，通过两个轻量读出模块：训练时的运动漂移损失与采样时的运动先验引导。实验表明，LaMo在VideoPhy和VideoPhy2上显著提升CogVideoX等扩散模型的物理一致性，超越依赖外部监督的最新方法，同时在VBench上保持整体生成质量。
Entropy-Aware On-Policy Distillation of Language Models
Authors: Woogyeol Jin, Taywon Min, Yongjin Yang, Swanand Ravindra Kadhe, Yi Zhou, Dennis Wei, Nathalie Baracaldo, Kimin Lee
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-03-07T07:26:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:34:18+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 11 figures, ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
On-policy distillation is a promising approach for transferring knowledge between language models, where a student learns from dense token-level signals along its own trajectories. This framework typically uses reverse KL divergence, encouraging the student to match the teacher's high-confidence predictions. However, we show that the mode-seeking property of reverse KL reduces generation diversity and yields unstable learning signals when the teacher distribution has high entropy. To address this, we introduce Entropy-Aware On-Policy Distillation. Our key idea is augmenting the standard reverse KL objective with forward KL when teacher entropy is high, capturing the full range of plausible outputs while retaining precise imitation elsewhere. It balances mode-seeking precision with mode-covering robustness without sacrificing on-policy training efficiency. Experiments show that our method maintains generation diversity (sustained token-level entropy) and improves student-teacher alignment (lower forward KL on high-entropy tokens). Across six math reasoning benchmarks, this yields Pass@8 accuracy gains of +1.37 for Qwen3-0.6B-Base, +2.39 for Qwen3-1.7B-Base, and +5.05 for Qwen3-4B-Base compared to baseline on-policy distillation methods. These results demonstrate that accounting for teacher uncertainty is essential for maintaining diversity and achieving effective knowledge transfer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于熵感知的在线策略蒸馏语言模型
在线策略蒸馏是语言模型间知识迁移的一种有前景方法，学生模型从自身轨迹中的密集token级信号学习。该框架通常使用逆KL散度，鼓励学生匹配教师的高置信度预测。然而，我们发现逆KL的模式寻求特性在教师分布熵值较高时会降低生成多样性并导致学习信号不稳定。为解决此问题，我们提出熵感知在线策略蒸馏。核心思想是在教师熵值较高时，将标准逆KL目标与前向KL结合，从而在保持其他区域精确模仿的同时，捕捉所有可能输出的完整范围。该方法在保持模式寻求精度的同时，兼顾模式覆盖的鲁棒性，且不牺牲在线训练效率。实验表明，该方法维持了生成多样性（持续的token级熵），并提升了学生-教师对齐度（高熵token的前向KL降低）。在六个数学推理基准测试中，相比基线在线策略蒸馏方法，Qwen3-0.6B-Base准确率提升+1.37，Qwen3-1.7B-Base提升+2.39，Qwen3-4B-Base提升+5.05。结果表明，考虑教师不确定性对于维持多样性与实现有效知识迁移至关重要。
Summary / 总结
On-policy distillation is a promising approach for transferring knowledge between language models, where a student learns from dense token-level signals along its own trajectories.
为了解决标准反向KL散度在教师分布高熵时导致生成多样性下降和学习信号不稳定的问题，本文提出熵感知的在线策略蒸馏方法。该方法根据教师熵值动态融合反向和前向KL散度，低熵区域保持精确模仿，高熵区域则覆盖更广的可能输出。在六个数学推理基准测试中，该方法保持了生成多样性并提升了对齐效果，相比基线在线策略蒸馏方法，Qwen3-0.6B-Base、Qwen3-1.7B-Base和Qwen3-4B-Base的Pass@8准确率分别提升+1.37、+2.39和+5.05。
Training-Free Looped Transformers
Authors: Lizhang Chen, Jonathan Li, Chen Liang, Ni Lao, Qiang Liu
First: 2026-05-22T17:31:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:31:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce training-free looped transformers, in which a lightweight inference-time wrapper loops a contiguous mid-stack block of layers of a frozen checkpoint without additional fine-tuning, continued training, or architectural changes. Unlike prior looped transformer methods that train with the looped structure end-to-end, we retrofit recurrence onto pretrained models at test time. We show that naive block reapplication usually degrades performance, highlighting the importance of the loop application strategy. Motivated by viewing a pre-norm transformer block as a forward Euler step on an ODE, we instead treat looping as a refinement of the same approximation, replacing one large update with smaller damped sub-steps. Across seven dense, sparse MoE, and MLA+MoE model families, our method improves Qwen3-4B-Instruct by +2.64 pp on MMLU-Pro, Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct by +1.14 pp on CommonsenseQA, and Moonlight-16B-A3B-Instruct by +1.20 pp on OpenBookQA.
Summary / 总结
We introduce training-free looped transformers, in which a lightweight inference-time wrapper loops a contiguous mid-stack block of layers of a frozen checkpoint without additional fine-tuning, continued training, or architectural changes.
Move on Muon : A Hamiltonian probability gradient flow perspective of Muon optimizer
Authors: Aratrika Mustafi, Soumya Mukherjee, Bharath K. Sriperumbudur
First: 2026-05-22T17:28:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:28:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We develop a gradient flow on the space of probability measures defined on matrix-valued parameters induced by regularized Muon, an analytically smoothed version of the idealized Muon optimizer. The key observation is that the regularized orthogonalization map is the gradient of a smooth Fenchel-dual smoothing of the nuclear norm. This identifies the (regularized) Muon update as a mirror/prox step in the update variable, with momentum acting as the dual coordinate. We use this structure to lift Muon from a single matrix parameter to finite-particle probability objectives of the form $J(ρ)=R\left(\int F d ρ\right)$, a setting motivated by mean-field descriptions of neural-network training, and derive the inertial continuous-time limit. Using this structure, we derive the finite-particle continuous-time limit under the inertial scaling of step size and momentum, and then pass to a phase-space mean-field equation over probability laws on parameter-momentum pairs. The resulting flow can be shown to be a damped Hamiltonian probability dynamics whose kinetic energy is induced by the regularized Muon mirror potential. We prove an exact Hamiltonian dissipation identity, showing that the Hamiltonian energy decreases monotonically. While the target objective itself need not be monotone along the inertial Muon dynamics, under additional gradient-dominance, bounded-momentum, and curvature/alignment assumptions, we obtain continuous and discrete-time exponential convergence rates for the objective gap. We also study the well-posedness of the mean-field limit equation and establish propagation of chaos guarantees for the interacting particle system. Finally, we extend the formulation to Hilbert-valued feature maps on product matrix spaces, yielding a blockwise Muon probability flow applicable to smooth transformer mixture-of-experts models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：移动穆恩：从哈密顿概率梯度流视角看穆恩优化器
我们为正则化穆恩（理想化穆恩优化器的解析平滑版本）在矩阵值参数上的概率测度空间构建了梯度流。关键观察是，正则化正交化映射是核范数的光滑Fenchel对偶平滑的梯度。这将（正则化）穆恩更新识别为更新变量中的镜像/近端步骤，动量作为对偶坐标。我们利用此结构将穆恩从单矩阵参数推广到有限粒子概率目标形式 $J(ρ)=R\left(\int F d ρ\right)$，该形式受神经网络训练的平均场描述启发，并推导出惯性连续时间极限。基于此结构，我们在步长与动量的惯性缩放下推导出有限粒子连续时间极限，进而过渡到参数-动量对的概率律上的相空间平均场方程。所得流可被证明为由正则化穆恩镜像势诱导动能的阻尼哈密顿概率动力学。我们证明了精确的哈密顿耗散恒等式，表明哈密顿能量单调递减。虽然目标函数本身沿惯性穆恩动力学未必单调递减，但在额外的梯度主导、有界动量及曲率/对齐假设下，我们获得了目标间隙的连续与离散时间指数收敛率。我们还研究了平均场极限方程的适定性，并建立了相互作用粒子系统的混沌传播保证。最后，我们将该框架扩展至希尔伯特值特征映射在矩阵积空间上的应用，得到适用于平滑Transformer混合专家模型的块状穆恩概率流。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the dynamics of the Muon optimizer in a continuous and probabilistic framework, the paper introduces a Hamiltonian probability gradient flow derived from a regularized version of Muon, which treats matrix-valued parameters as probability measures. The method leverages the Fenchel-dual smoothing of the nuclear norm to reinterpret Muon updates as mirror descent steps with momentum as the dual variable, enabling extension to mean-field objectives of the form J(ρ)=R(∫F dρ). The authors derive inertial continuous-time limits and phase-space mean-field equations, proving that the resulting dynamics are damped Hamiltonian flows with monotonic energy dissipation. Under gradient-dominance, bounded momentum, and curvature conditions, exponential convergence rates for the objective gap are established in both continuous and discrete time. The formulation is further extended to Hilbert-valued feature maps, enabling blockwise Muon flows for transformer mixture-of-experts models.
为理解Muon优化器在连续时间和概率空间中的动态行为，本文提出一种哈密顿概率梯度流框架，将正则化Muon的更新视为镜像步，动量作为对偶坐标。该方法利用核范数的Fenchel对偶平滑，推导出在矩阵参数概率测度空间上的阻尼哈密顿流，将Muon推广至均场目标并推导惯性连续时间极限。实验结果表明哈密顿能量单调递减，且在梯度主导、动量有界等条件下，目标函数间隙在连续和离散时间下均呈现指数收敛率，同时证明粒子系统的适定性及混沌传播性质。
Gen-Searcher: Reinforcing Agentic Search for Image Generation
Authors: Kaituo Feng, Manyuan Zhang, Shuang Chen, Yunlong Lin, Kaixuan Fan, Yilei Jiang, Hongyu Li, Dian Zheng, Chenyang Wang, Xiangyu Yue
First: 2026-03-30T17:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:26:49+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://gen-searcher.vercel.app Code: https://github.com/tulerfeng/Gen-Searcher
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent image generation models have shown strong capabilities in generating high-fidelity and photorealistic images. However, they are fundamentally constrained by frozen internal knowledge, thus often failing on real-world scenarios that are knowledge-intensive or require up-to-date information. In this paper, we present Gen-Searcher, as the first attempt to train a search-augmented image generation agent, which performs multi-hop reasoning and search to collect the textual knowledge and reference images needed for grounded generation. To achieve this, we construct a tailored data pipeline and curate two high-quality datasets, Gen-Searcher-SFT-10k and Gen-Searcher-RL-6k, containing diverse search-intensive prompts and corresponding ground-truth synthesis images. We further introduce KnowGen, a comprehensive benchmark that explicitly requires search-grounded external knowledge for image generation and evaluates models from multiple dimensions. Based on these resources, we train Gen-Searcher with SFT followed by agentic reinforcement learning with dual reward feedback, which combines text-based and image-based rewards to provide more stable and informative learning signals for GRPO training. Experiments show that Gen-Searcher brings substantial gains, improving Qwen-Image by around 16 points on KnowGen and 15 points on WISE. We hope this work can serve as an open foundation for search agents in image generation, and we fully open-source our data, models, and code.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Gen-Searcher：强化代理搜索以实现图像生成
近期图像生成模型在生成高保真、逼真图像方面表现出强大能力，但受限于冻结的内部知识，常在需要知识密集或最新信息的真实场景中失败。本文提出Gen-Searcher，首次尝试训练一个增强搜索能力的图像生成代理，通过多跳推理与搜索收集生成所需的文本知识和参考图像。为此，我们构建了定制数据管道，整理了两个高质量数据集Gen-Searcher-SFT-10k和Gen-Searcher-RL-6k，包含多样化的搜索密集提示及其对应的真实合成图像。我们进一步引入KnowGen基准，明确要求图像生成需依赖搜索获取的外部知识，并从多个维度评估模型。基于这些资源，我们采用SFT训练Gen-Searcher，随后结合双奖励反馈的代理强化学习（GRPO），融合文本与图像奖励以提供更稳定、信息丰富的学习信号。实验表明，Gen-Searcher显著提升性能，在KnowGen上提升Qwen-Image约16分，在WISE上提升约15分。我们希望本工作能为图像生成领域的搜索代理提供开放基础，并完全开源数据、模型与代码。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of frozen knowledge in current image generation models, this work introduces Gen-Searcher, the first search-augmented agent that performs multi-hop reasoning and external search to gather textual and visual knowledge for grounded image synthesis. The method constructs two curated datasets and trains the agent via supervised fine-tuning followed by agentic reinforcement learning with dual reward signals combining text and image feedback. Experimental results demonstrate that Gen-Searcher significantly outperforms baseline models, improving Qwen-Image by 16 points on KnowGen and 15 points on WISE, validating its effectiveness in knowledge-intensive image generation tasks.
为解决现有图像生成模型因内部知识冻结而难以处理知识密集型或需实时信息的真实场景问题，本文提出Gen-Searcher，这是首个能进行多跳推理与在线搜索以获取文本知识和参考图像的图像生成代理。该方法构建了两个高质量数据集，并采用监督微调后结合双奖励反馈的强化学习训练策略，奖励信号同时包含文本与图像维度。实验表明，Gen-Searcher显著优于基线模型，在KnowGen上提升Qwen-Image 16分，在WISE上提升15分，验证了其在搜索引导图像生成中的有效性。
Vision Transformers Need Better Token Interaction
Authors: Linxiang Su
First: 2026-05-22T17:25:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:25:16+00:00
Comments: 7 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision Transformers (ViTs) can learn strong image-level representations while their patch representations become less effective for dense prediction during prolonged training. We revisit this dense degradation phenomenon and argue that it is not fully explained by high-norm artifacts alone. Instead, we characterize \emph{semantic diffusion}: an optimization shortcut in which global semantic information spreads through patch tokens beyond what is locally justified. Our analysis shows that dense representation quality is not captured by locality alone: shallow features can remain better aligned with foreground regions yet underperform deeper features, and \texttt{[CLS]} features remain complementary for dense prediction. These observations suggest that the goal should not be to remove global context, but to make token interactions more selective. We therefore study sparse attention as a minimal intervention, replacing softmax attention with entmax-1.5 while preserving global token connectivity. On DINOv1 ViT-S/16 trained for 200 epochs on ImageNet-1K, this change preserves ImageNet linear probing accuracy and substantially improves semantic segmentation performance: VOC mIoU increases from 42.80 to 48.78, ADE20K from 19.85 to 21.97, and Cityscapes from 36.79 to 37.87. These results suggest that selective token mixing is a simple and effective bias for improving dense ViT representations.
Summary / 总结
Vision Transformers (ViTs) can learn strong image-level representations while their patch representations become less effective for dense prediction during prolonged training.
该论文指出，尽管视觉Transformer在图像级任务表现优异，但在密集预测任务中其patch表示会退化，归因于语义扩散——即全局语义在优化过程中过度扩散至非局部token。为解决此问题，作者提出采用entmax-1.5替代softmax注意力，以实现更选择性的token交互，同时保留全局连接。在DINOv1 ViT-S/16模型上，该改动保持了线性探测准确率，显著提升了分割性能：VOC mIoU从42.80升至48.78，ADE20K从19.85升至21.97，Cityscapes从36.79升至37.87。
Human Decision-Making with Persuasive and Narrative LLM Explanations
Authors: Laura R. Marusich, Mary Grace Kozuch Dhooghe, Jonathan Z. Bakdash, Murat Kantarcioglu
First: 2026-05-22T17:25:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:25:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) have the potential to aid and improve human decision-making in classification tasks, not only by providing fairly accurate predictions, but also in their ability to generate cogent narrative explanations of those predictions. Prior work has demonstrated that people generally find AI narrative explanations to be understandable, trustworthy, and convincing for changing beliefs and opinions; however, less is known about the impact of narrative explanations on objective human decision-making performance. Here we conduct a large-scale human behavioral experiment to evaluate decision-making performance with LLM-generated narrative explanations of varying persuasiveness. We found the degree of persuasiveness, or lack thereof, for LLM-based explanations did not meaningfully impact decision accuracy over a simple AI prediction alone, in agreement with typical results with explainable AI based on feature importance. We found evidence that narratives increased reliance on AI, but both when the AI prediction was correct and incorrect. Exploratory analyses also indicated that the more persuasive narratives may have had a detrimental effect on decision response times and the ability to discriminate between a correct and incorrect AI prediction. Overall, this work indicates that including narrative explanations with AI predictions may involve tradeoffs for decision-making performance, and more work is needed to determine how and when narrative explanations impact human decision-making.
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how persuasive narrative explanations generated by large language models (LLMs) influence human decision-making in classification tasks. Motivated by prior findings that narratives are perceived as trustworthy and convincing, the researchers conducted a large-scale behavioral experiment to assess whether such explanations improve objective decision accuracy. Results showed that the persuasiveness of LLM-generated narratives did not meaningfully enhance decision accuracy compared to simple AI predictions. However, narratives increased reliance on AI regardless of whether the prediction was correct or incorrect. Additionally, more persuasive narratives were associated with slower response times and reduced ability to distinguish correct from incorrect AI predictions, suggesting potential trade-offs in decision performance.
本研究探讨了由大语言模型（LLM）生成的具有说服力的叙事解释如何影响人类在分类任务中的决策表现。鉴于先前研究发现叙事被普遍认为可信且具有说服力，研究者通过大规模行为实验评估此类解释是否能提升决策准确性。结果表明，LLM生成的叙事解释的说服力并未显著提升决策准确率，与基于特征重要性的可解释AI结果一致。然而，叙事增加了人类对AI预测的依赖，无论预测正确与否。此外，更具说服力的叙事还导致反应时间延长，且降低了区分AI预测正确与否的能力，暗示叙事解释可能带来决策表现上的权衡。
Robotic Strawberry Harvesting with Robust Vision and Deep Reinforcement Learning based Sim-to-Real Control
Authors: Al Bashir, Shao-Yang Chang, Partho Ghose, Prem Raj, Chen-Kang Huang, Azlan Zahid
First: 2026-05-22T17:21:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:21:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This study presents a closed-loop robotic strawberry harvesting system that combines a robust vision module, simulation-trained deep reinforcement learning (DRL) control, and ROS-based realrobot execution. For perception, we propose HRAttnEdge-YOLO26-seg, a modified YOLO26-seg architecture that incorporates a high-resolution P2 branch, segmentation-path attention, and edgesupervised prototype learning to improve instance segmentation in cluttered scenes. For control, we train a target-conditioned Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) policy in Isaac Lab to produce smooth joint-position commands for a UR10e manipulator and deploy it on a UR10e robot for targetfruit reaching and harvesting. This simulation-based approach reduces hardware dependency, lowers development cost, and allows scalable policy training without exhaustive physical trials before real deployment. The proposed vision model demonstrated the highest overall performance among the evaluated methods. On both self-collected and public datasets, the model showed a 10 to 14% improvement in segmentation performance. In controlled in-house tests, the PPO controller produced stable and dynamically smoother motion than a inverse kinematics (IK)-based MoveIt baseline. In greenhouse trials, the proposed integrated system harvested 281 strawberries, achieving 96.6% reaching success, 91.3% grasp-and-pull success, and 84.3% overall harvesting success. These results illustrate that task-specific perception combined with simulation-trained PPO can serve as a practical and resource-efficient alternative to conventional planner-dependent reaching in manipulation, enabling reliable closed-loop robotic harvesting in complex agricultural environments.

标题：基于鲁棒视觉与深度强化学习的机器人草莓采摘系统
本研究提出了一种闭环机器人草莓采摘系统，结合了鲁棒视觉模块、仿真训练的深度强化学习（DRL）控制及基于ROS的真实机器人执行。在感知方面，我们提出了HRAttnEdge-YOLO26-seg，一种改进的YOLO26-seg架构，融合高分辨率P2分支、分割路径注意力及边缘监督原型学习，以提升复杂场景下的实例分割性能。在控制方面，我们在Isaac Lab中训练了目标条件化的近端策略优化（PPO）策略，生成UR10e机械臂的平滑关节位置指令，并部署于UR10e机器人上实现目标果实抓取与采摘。该仿真方法降低了硬件依赖，降低了开发成本，无需大量物理试验即可实现可扩展的策略训练。所提出的视觉模型在评估方法中表现最佳。在自采集与公开数据集上，模型分割性能提升10%至14%。在室内控制测试中，PPO控制器比基于逆运动学（IK）的MoveIt基线产生更稳定、动态更平滑的运动。在温室试验中，该集成系统成功采摘281颗草莓，达到96.6%的到达成功率、91.3%的抓取与拉取成功率及84.3%的整体采摘成功率。结果表明，任务特定感知结合仿真训练的PPO可作为传统规划器依赖的到达方法的实用且资源高效的替代方案，实现复杂农业环境中可靠的闭环机器人采摘。
