Edge Assisted Multi-Camera Vehicle Tracking Framework for Real-Time and Scalable Deployment
Authors: Yuqiang Lin, Sam Lockyer, Shucheng Zhang, Florian Stanek, Markus Zarbock, Adrian Evans, Wenbin Li, Yinhai Wang, Nic Zhang
First: 2025-11-17T20:55:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:20:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cameras are a core sensing modality in modern intelligent transportation systems (ITS), providing rich visual information on road-user activities. Multi-Camera Vehicle Tracking (MCVT) uses this data to reconstruct vehicle trajectories across camera networks, supporting applications such as traffic flow prediction and optimisation. However, most existing MCVT studies emphasise tracking accuracy while paying limited attention to real-time performance and scalability, both essential for real-world and city-scale deployment. To address this gap, we propose Edge-Assisted, Scalable and Efficient MCVT (EASE-MCVT), a distributed edge--server framework designed for real-time throughput and scalable operation. On the edge side, each camera stream is processed through object detection, single-camera tracking, geo-mapping and feature extraction, while only lightweight metadata, including vehicle locations and appearance features, is sent to the central server for cross-camera association. To improve both tracking accuracy and system efficiency, EASE-MCVT is optimised from algorithmic and system perspectives. Algorithmically, it introduces a dynamic workload scheme for tracklet-level feature extraction, a server-side re-match module to reconnect fragmented tracklets, and a self-supervised camera link model that learns spatio-temporal constraints to accelerate and stabilise cross-camera association. Systemically, it integrates production-oriented data engineering components to standardise deployment and data exchange for large-scale operation. To the best of our knowledge, EASE-MCVT is the first MCVT framework explicitly designed to address both real-time performance and scalability in a distributed edge--server setting. Experiments on the RoundaboutHD and CityFlow datasets demonstrate real-time throughput with competitive tracking accuracy, paving the way for city-wide real-time traffic management.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向实时与可扩展部署的边缘辅助多摄像头车辆跟踪框架
摄像头是现代智能交通系统（ITS）的核心感知模态，提供丰富的道路使用者视觉信息。多摄像头车辆跟踪（MCVT）利用这些数据在摄像头网络中重建车辆轨迹，支持交通流预测与优化等应用。然而，现有大多数MCVT研究侧重于跟踪精度，而对实时性能和可扩展性关注不足，这两者对于实际部署和城市级应用至关重要。为解决此问题，我们提出边缘辅助、可扩展且高效的MCVT框架（EASE-MCVT），一种面向实时吞吐量和可扩展操作的分布式边缘-服务器架构。在边缘侧，每个摄像头流经目标检测、单摄像头跟踪、地理映射和特征提取，仅轻量级元数据（包括车辆位置和外观特征）发送至中央服务器进行跨摄像头关联。为提升跟踪精度与系统效率，EASE-MCVT从算法与系统层面进行优化：算法上引入动态工作负载方案进行轨迹片段级特征提取、服务器端重匹配模块以重新连接碎片化轨迹片段、自监督摄像头关联模型学习时空约束以加速并稳定跨摄像头关联；系统上集成面向生产的工程组件，标准化大规模部署与数据交换。据我们所知，EASE-MCVT是首个明确针对分布式边缘-服务器架构下实时性能与可扩展性设计的MCVT框架。在RoundaboutHD和CityFlow数据集上的实验表明，该框架具备实时吞吐量与竞争力的跟踪精度，为全市域实时交通管理奠定基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for real-time and scalable multi-camera vehicle tracking in large-scale intelligent transportation systems, this paper introduces EASE-MCVT, a distributed edge-server framework that balances accuracy with performance. On edge devices, each camera processes object detection, single-camera tracking, geo-mapping, and feature extraction, transmitting only lightweight metadata to a central server for cross-camera association. Algorithmically, it employs dynamic tracklet-level feature extraction, a server-side re-match module to reconnect fragmented tracks, and a self-supervised camera link model to learn spatio-temporal constraints for faster and more stable association. Systemically, it incorporates production-ready data engineering for scalable deployment. Experiments on RoundaboutHD and CityFlow show real-time throughput with competitive accuracy, validating its suitability for city-wide traffic management.
为满足大规模智能交通系统中实时、可扩展的多摄像头车辆跟踪需求，本文提出EASE-MCVT分布式边缘-服务器框架，兼顾性能与扩展性。边缘端对每个摄像头流进行目标检测、单摄像头跟踪、地理映射及轻量级元数据提取，仅传输车辆位置与外观特征至中央服务器进行跨摄像头关联。算法层面引入动态轨道级特征提取、服务器端重匹配模块以恢复碎片化轨迹，以及自监督相机关联模型学习时空约束以加速稳定匹配。系统层面集成面向生产的工程组件，支持大规模部署。在RoundaboutHD与CityFlow数据集上的实验表明，该框架具备实时吞吐量与竞争力的跟踪精度，适用于城市级交通管理。
Leveraging Foundation Models for Causal Generative Modeling
Authors: Aneesh Komanduri, Xintao Wu
First: 2026-05-22T17:20:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:20:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Causal generative modeling is essential for developing reliable and transparent AI systems capable of counterfactual reasoning. While existing approaches focus on integrating causal constraints during the training of generative models, they often lack a unified framework to leverage the zero-shot reasoning capabilities of pretrained foundation models. We introduce FM-CGM, a modular framework for end-to-end visual causal reasoning using pretrained foundation models. FM-CGM formalizes the causal pipeline through three core components: a concept extractor, a concept manipulator, and a counterfactual generator. By leveraging a large reasoning model for causal inference and a text-to-image diffusion model for generation, our approach enables zero-shot causal discovery, intervention, and counterfactual generation. We then develop Causal Semantic Guidance (CSG), a cross-attention-based mechanism that ensures semantic interventions propagate to descendant concepts while preserving invariant regions. We empirically show that our approach can identify plausible causal structures and is suitable for faithful counterfactual image generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用基础模型进行因果生成建模
因果生成建模对于开发具备反事实推理能力的可靠、透明的AI系统至关重要。现有方法虽侧重于在生成模型训练过程中整合因果约束，但往往缺乏统一框架以利用预训练基础模型的零样本推理能力。我们提出了FM-CGM，一个基于预训练基础模型的端到端视觉因果推理模块化框架。FM-CGM通过三个核心组件形式化因果流程：概念提取器、概念操作器和反事实生成器。通过利用大型推理模型进行因果推断和文本到图像扩散模型进行生成，我们的方法实现了零样本因果发现、干预与反事实生成。随后，我们开发了因果语义引导（CSG），一种基于跨注意力的机制，确保语义干预能传播至后代概念，同时保留不变区域。实证表明，我们的方法能够识别合理的因果结构，适用于忠实的反事实图像生成。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for transparent AI systems capable of counterfactual reasoning, this work proposes FM-CGM, a modular framework that leverages pretrained foundation models for causal generative modeling. It integrates a concept extractor, a concept manipulator, and a counterfactual generator to enable zero-shot causal discovery, intervention, and image generation using large reasoning models and text-to-image diffusion models. Experimental results demonstrate that FM-CGM can identify plausible causal structures and generate faithful counterfactual images while preserving invariant regions through Causal Semantic Guidance.
为构建具备反事实推理能力的透明AI系统，本文提出FM-CGM框架，利用预训练基础模型实现因果生成建模。该框架包含概念提取器、概念操作器和反事实生成器，结合大模型推理与文本到图像扩散模型，支持零样本因果发现、干预与图像生成。实验表明，FM-CGM能识别合理因果结构，生成忠实反事实图像，并通过因果语义引导保持不变区域。
Strong Teacher Not Needed? On Distillation in LLM Pretraining
Authors: Taiming Lu, Zhuang Liu
First: 2026-05-22T17:16:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:16:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Knowledge distillation generally assumes a strong-to-weak relationship where stronger teachers yield better students. In this work, we examine this assumption about distillation in large language model pretraining. By varying architecture sizes and training token budgets, we create strong-to-weak, same-level, and weak-to-strong teacher-student relationships, and study distillation's effectiveness under each. We find that the teacher need not be strong: with proper mixing of the language modeling and knowledge distillation losses, even small and undertrained teachers improve larger students. At the same time, a stronger teacher is not always better: pushing the teacher further, through more parameters or more training tokens, can saturate or even reverse the distillation gains. We further observe that distillation improves generalization (out-of-distribution and downstream performance) more readily than in-domain fitting. Together, these results challenge the common belief that distillation pretraining always requires a strong teacher.
中文标题/摘要
标题：强教师非必需？关于大语言模型预训练中的蒸馏
知识蒸馏通常假设强教师到弱学生的关系，即更强的教师能带来更好的学生。本研究探讨了大语言模型预训练中蒸馏的这一假设。通过调整模型架构大小和训练token预算，我们构建了强到弱、同级、弱到强的教师-学生关系，并研究每种关系下蒸馏的有效性。我们发现，教师不必强大：通过适当混合语言建模与知识蒸馏损失，即使小型且训练不足的教师也能提升大型学生。同时，更强的教师并不总是更好：进一步增强教师（如增加参数或训练token）可能使蒸馏收益饱和甚至逆转。我们还观察到，蒸馏更易提升泛化能力（分布外及下游任务表现），而非领域内拟合。这些结果挑战了蒸馏预训练总需强教师的普遍认知。
Summary / 总结
This study challenges the conventional assumption that distillation in large language model pretraining requires a strong teacher by systematically testing distillation across varying teacher-student strength relationships. Through controlled experiments manipulating architecture sizes and training token budgets, the authors demonstrate that even small, undertrained teachers can effectively improve larger students when the language modeling and distillation losses are properly balanced. Surprisingly, stronger teachers do not always yield better results, as further increasing teacher capacity or training tokens can saturate or even degrade distillation gains. Additionally, distillation consistently enhances generalization performance—both out-of-distribution and downstream—more than in-domain fitting, suggesting its value lies in improving robustness rather than merely fitting training data.
该研究挑战了大语言模型预训练中蒸馏通常需要强教师的传统假设，通过控制模型架构规模和训练token数量，系统测试了不同强弱关系下的蒸馏效果。实验发现，即使教师模型较小且训练不足，只要合理平衡语言建模与蒸馏损失，仍能有效提升学生模型性能。值得注意的是，教师模型更强并不总能带来更好效果，进一步增加参数或训练token反而可能使蒸馏收益饱和甚至下降。此外，蒸馏在提升模型泛化能力（如分布外和下游任务表现）方面比提升训练数据拟合效果更显著，表明其核心价值在于增强模型鲁棒性而非单纯拟合训练数据。
Benchmarking Commercial ASR Systems on Code-Switching Speech: Arabic, Persian, and German
Authors: Sajjad Abdoli, Ghassan Al-Sumaidaee, Clayton W. Taylor, Ahmad ElShiekh, Ahmed Rashad
First: 2026-05-18T19:50:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:14:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Code-switching -- the natural alternation between two languages within a single utterance -- remains one of the most challenging and under-studied conditions for automatic speech recognition (ASR). We present a benchmark evaluating five commercial ASR providers across four language pairs: Egyptian Arabic--English, Saudi Arabic (Najdi/Hijazi)--English, Persian (Farsi)--English, and German--English, comprising 300 samples per pair selected by a two-stage pipeline combining heuristic filtering with a GPT-4o and Gemini 1.5 Pro ensemble scorer, reducing LLM costs by $\approx$91\%. We evaluate on both WER and BERTScore, showing that while both metrics agree on the ordinal ranking of systems for all Arabic and Persian pairs ($τ= 1.0$), WER inflates the magnitude of quality gaps by approximately 3$\times$ by penalising semantically correct transliteration choices. ElevenLabs Scribe v2 achieves the lowest WER (13.2\% overall) and leads on BERTScore (0.936 overall). Difficulty-stratified analysis reveals performance gaps masked by aggregate averages, and BERT embedding projections confirm semantic proximity between reference and hypothesis despite surface-level script differences. The dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Perle-ai/ASR_Code_Switch.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在混合语言语音上对商业自动语音识别系统的基准测试：阿拉伯语、波斯语和德语
语言切换——在单句内自然交替使用两种语言——仍是自动语音识别（ASR）中最具挑战性且研究最少的场景之一。我们提出一个基准测试，评估五个商业ASR提供商在四个语言对上的表现：埃及阿拉伯语—英语、沙特阿拉伯语（纳杰迪/希贾兹方言）—英语、波斯语（法尔西语）—英语和德语—英语，每个语言对选取300个样本，通过两阶段管道结合启发式过滤与GPT-4o和Gemini 1.5 Pro集成评分器，将大语言模型成本降低约91%。我们在WER和BERTScore两个指标上进行评估，结果显示，对于所有阿拉伯语和波斯语语言对，两个指标在系统排序上完全一致（τ=1.0），但WER因惩罚语义正确的音译选择，使质量差距放大约3倍。ElevenLabs Scribe v2在WER上表现最佳（整体13.2%），并在BERTScore上领先（整体0.936）。按难度分层分析揭示了聚合平均值所掩盖的性能差距，BERT嵌入投影证实了参考文本与假设文本在语义上的接近性，尽管表面书写系统不同。该数据集公开于https://huggingface.co/datasets/Perle-ai/ASR_Code_Switch。
Summary / 总结
Code-switching -- the natural alternation between two languages within a single utterance -- remains one of the most challenging and under-studied conditions for automatic speech recognition (ASR).
R$^3$L: Reflect-then-Retry Reinforcement Learning with Language-Guided Exploration, Pivotal Credit, and Positive Amplification
Authors: Weijie Shi, Yanxi Chen, Zexi Li, Xuchen Pan, Yuchang Sun, Jiajie Xu, Xiaofang Zhou, Yaliang Li
First: 2026-01-07T09:04:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:13:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reinforcement learning drives recent advances in LLM reasoning and agentic capabilities, yet current approaches struggle with both exploration and exploitation. Exploration suffers from low success rates on difficult tasks and high costs of repeated rollouts from scratch. Exploitation suffers from coarse credit assignment and training instability: Trajectory-level rewards penalize valid prefixes for later errors, and failure-dominated groups overwhelm the few positive signals, leaving optimization without constructive direction. To this end, we propose R$^3$L, Reflect-then-Retry Reinforcement Learning with Language-Guided Exploration, Pivotal Credit, and Positive Amplification. To synthesize high-quality trajectories, R$^3$L shifts from stochastic sampling to active synthesis via reflect-then-retry, leveraging language feedback to diagnose errors, transform failed attempts into successful ones, and reduce rollout costs by restarting from identified failure points. With errors diagnosed and localized, Pivotal Credit Assignment updates only the diverging suffix where contrastive signals exist, excluding the shared prefix from gradient update. Since failures dominate on difficult tasks and reflect-then-retry produces off-policy data, risking training instability, Positive Amplification upweights successful trajectories to ensure positive signals guide the optimization process. Experiments on agentic and reasoning tasks demonstrate 5\% to 52\% relative improvements over baselines while maintaining training stability. Our code is released at https://github.com/shiweijiezero/R3L.
中文标题/摘要
标题：R³L：基于语言引导探索、关键信用分配与正向放大机制的反思后重试强化学习
强化学习推动了大语言模型推理与代理能力的近期进展，但现有方法在探索与利用方面均存在挑战。探索因在困难任务上成功率低且从头重复 rollout 成本高昂而受限；利用则因粗粒度的信用分配与训练不稳定而受挫：轨迹级奖励惩罚后续错误导致的有效前缀，失败主导的样本淹没稀少的正向信号，使优化缺乏建设性方向。为此，我们提出 R³L（Reflect-then-Retry Reinforcement Learning with Language-Guided Exploration, Pivotal Credit, and Positive Amplification）。为合成高质量轨迹，R³L 从随机采样转向主动合成，通过“反思后重试”机制，利用语言反馈诊断错误、将失败尝试转化为成功轨迹，并通过从已识别失败点重启以降低 rollout 成本。错误诊断与定位后，关键信用分配仅更新存在对比信号的分歧后缀，排除共享前缀的梯度更新。由于困难任务中失败主导且“反思后重试”产生离策略数据，易引发训练不稳定，正向放大机制则提升成功轨迹权重，确保正向信号引导优化过程。在代理与推理任务上的实验表明，R³L 相较基线方法提升 5% 至 52%，同时保持训练稳定。代码已开源：https://github.com/shiweijiezero/R3L。
Summary / 总结
Reinforcement learning drives recent advances in LLM reasoning and agentic capabilities, yet current approaches struggle with both exploration and exploitation.
为解决当前强化学习方法在LLM探索与利用中的不足，R$^3$L提出“反思后重试”框架，利用语言反馈诊断并修正失败，降低重 rollout 成本。其采用关键信用分配机制，仅更新轨迹中差异化的后缀部分，避免前缀梯度噪声，并通过正向放大机制平衡失败主导的数据，提升训练稳定性。实验表明，在代理与推理任务中，相较基线模型平均提升5%至52%，且保持训练稳定。
Point Tracking Improves World Action Models
Authors: Jiarui Guan, Wenshuai Zhao, Yue Pei, Ziliang Chen, Arno Solin, Juho Kannala
First: 2026-05-22T17:08:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:08:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robot policy learning benefits from world-action models that capture environment dynamics, but pixel-level prediction entangles dynamics with nuisance factors such as lighting and texture, making learned representations vulnerable to task-irrelevant visual variation. We propose JOPAT, a JOint Pixel-And-Track World-Action Model that predicts latent visual observations, 2D point tracks with visibility, and actions in a single denoising diffusion transformer. The key insight is that tracks provide an explicit representation of motion that captures long-horizon dynamics and remains robust under occlusion or partial out-of-frame motion, offering greater utility than modeling pixel appearance alone. On LIBERO and real-world LeRobot tasks, JOPAT improves over pixel-based baselines, with the largest gains on long-horizon tasks involving occlusion, object interaction, and off-screen motion.
中文标题/摘要
标题：点追踪提升世界动作模型
机器人策略学习受益于捕捉环境动态的世界-动作模型，但像素级预测将动态与光照、纹理等干扰因素纠缠在一起，导致学习到的表示易受任务无关视觉变化的影响。我们提出JOPAT（联合像素与追踪的世界-动作模型），该模型在单个去噪扩散Transformer中同时预测潜在视觉观测、带可见性标记的2D点追踪和动作。其核心思想是：追踪提供显式运动表示，能捕捉长时程动态并在遮挡或部分出画运动下保持鲁棒性，比仅建模像素外观更具实用性。在LIBERO和真实世界LeRobot任务上，JOPAT优于基于像素的基线方法，尤其在涉及遮挡、物体交互和画面外运动的长时程任务中收益最大。
Summary / 总结
To address the issue that pixel-level predictions in robot policy learning are sensitive to visual nuisances like lighting and texture, which degrade generalization, the authors introduce JOPAT, a joint model that simultaneously predicts latent visual observations, 2D point tracks with visibility, and actions using a denoising diffusion transformer. The method leverages point tracks to explicitly encode motion dynamics, which are more robust to occlusion and partial motion than pixel appearance alone. Experiments on LIBERO and real-world LeRobot tasks show JOPAT outperforms pixel-based baselines, with the most significant improvements on long-horizon tasks involving occlusion, object interaction, and off-screen motion.
为了解决机器人策略学习中像素级预测对光照和纹理等视觉干扰敏感的问题，作者提出了JOPAT模型，该模型通过一个去噪扩散Transformer同时预测潜在视觉观测、带可见性标记的2D点轨迹和动作。该方法利用点轨迹显式建模运动，能捕捉长时程动态并在遮挡或物体移出画面时保持鲁棒性，优于仅建模像素外观的方法。在LIBERO和真实世界LeRobot任务上的实验表明，JOPAT显著优于像素基线方法，尤其在涉及遮挡、物体交互和画面外运动的长时程任务中提升最为明显。
Entrywise Error Bounds for Spectral Ranking with Semi-Random Adversaries
Authors: Dongmin Lee, Anuran Makur, Japneet Singh
First: 2026-05-22T17:08:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:08:07+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 2 figures, 32nd ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining V.2
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bradley-Terry-Luce (BTL) model estimation is a well-established strategy to rank a collection of items given a dataset of pairwise comparisons. Although the theoretical performance of BTL estimation methods, such as spectral and maximum likelihood estimation, is well studied in the regime of uniformly sampled graphs, generalizing such results to a wider class of random graphs has proved challenging. In this work, we investigate the entry-wise error of spectral algorithms against a semi-random adversary that can arbitrarily boost the sampling probabilities of certain edges. We find that the performance of the unweighted spectral method is heavily dependent on the spectral properties of the generated graph. Furthermore, we show that asymptotic performance approaching that of uniformly sampled graphs can be recovered by appropriately reweighting the observed edges to counteract the adversary and restore the spectral gap. Finally, we provide numerical simulations that support our theoretical findings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：半随机对手下谱排序的逐项误差界
布拉德利-特里-卢斯（BTL）模型估计是基于成对比较数据对一组项目进行排序的成熟策略。尽管BTL估计方法（如谱估计和最大似然估计）在均匀采样图情形下的理论性能已得到充分研究，将其推广至更广泛的随机图类仍具挑战性。本文研究了谱算法在面对可任意提升特定边采样概率的半随机对手时的逐项误差。我们发现，无权重谱方法的性能高度依赖于生成图的谱性质。此外，我们证明，通过适当重加权观测边以抵消对手影响并恢复谱隙，可恢复接近均匀采样图的渐近性能。最后，我们提供了数值模拟以支持理论结果。
Summary / 总结
Bradley-Terry-Luce (BTL) model estimation is a well-established strategy to rank a collection of items given a dataset of pairwise comparisons.
为解决谱排序方法在非均匀采样场景下理论保证难以推广的问题，本文研究了半随机对手操纵边采样概率时谱算法的逐元素误差。研究发现，图的谱间隙是性能的关键决定因素。作者提出通过重加权边来抵消对手的采样偏差，从而恢复接近均匀采样时的渐近误差表现。数值实验验证了理论分析，证实了该重加权策略的有效性。
Linear Regression with Unknown Truncation Beyond Gaussian Features
Authors: Alexandros Kouridakis, Anay Mehrotra, Alkis Kalavasis, Constantine Caramanis
First: 2026-02-13T02:29:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:08:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In truncated linear regression, samples $(x,y)$ are shown only when the outcome $y$ falls inside a certain survival set $S^\star$ and the goal is to estimate the unknown $d$-dimensional regressor $w^\star$. This problem has a long history of study in Statistics and Machine Learning going back to the works of (Galton, 1897; Tobin, 1958) and more recently in, e.g., (Daskalakis et al., 2019; 2021; Lee et al., 2023; 2024). Despite this long history, however, most prior works are limited to the special case where $S^\star$ is precisely known. The more practically relevant case, where $S^\star$ is unknown and must be learned from data, remains open: indeed, here the only available algorithms require strong assumptions on the distribution of the feature vectors (e.g., Gaussianity) and, even then, have a $d^{\mathrm{poly} (1/\varepsilon)}$ run time for achieving $\varepsilon$ accuracy. In this work, we give the first algorithm for truncated linear regression with unknown survival set that runs in $\mathrm{poly} (d/\varepsilon)$ time, by only requiring that the feature vectors are sub-Gaussian. Our algorithm relies on a novel subroutine for efficiently learning unions of a bounded number of intervals using access to positive examples (without any negative examples) under a certain smoothness condition. This learning guarantee adds to the line of works on positive-only PAC learning and may be of independent interest.
中文标题/摘要
标题：未知截断集下高斯特征的线性回归
在截断线性回归中，仅当结果 y 落入某个生存集 S* 时才显示样本 (x,y)，目标是估计未知的 d 维回归器 w*。该问题在统计学和机器学习领域已有悠久研究历史，可追溯至 Galton (1897) 和 Tobin (1958) 的工作，近期研究包括 Daskalakis 等人 (2019; 2021) 以及 Lee 等人 (2023; 2024)。然而，大多数先前工作仅限于 S* 精确已知的特殊情况。更实际相关的情形——S* 未知且需从数据中学习——仍属未解：事实上，目前仅有的算法要求特征向量分布满足强假设（如高斯性），即使如此，其达到 ε 精度的时间复杂度仍为 d^{poly(1/ε)}。 本工作中，我们首次提出一种针对未知生存集的截断线性回归算法，其时间复杂度为 poly(d/ε)，仅需特征向量满足次高斯性。该算法依赖于一个新颖的子程序：在满足特定平滑条件下，仅通过正例访问即可高效学习有限个区间并集。该学习保证拓展了正例仅学习的 PAC 学习研究方向，可能具有独立研究价值。
Summary / 总结
In truncated linear regression, samples $(x,y)$ are shown only when the outcome $y$ falls inside a certain survival set $S^\star$ and the goal is to estimate the unknown $d$-dimensional regressor $w^\star$.
为解决实际中目标值范围未知时的截断线性回归问题，本文首次提出一种仅需特征向量满足次高斯性质、运行时间为poly(d/ε)的算法，突破了此前依赖高斯假设且维度依赖指数级的算法限制。该方法核心是一个新颖的子程序，能在仅使用正例且满足平滑性条件下高效学习有限区间并集，从而实现对未知回归系数的ε精度恢复，其正例学习技术本身亦具独立研究价值。
Ceci n'est pas une explication: Evaluating Explanation Failures as Explainability Pitfalls in Language Learning Systems
Authors: Ben Knight, Wm. Matthew Kennedy, Danielle Carvalho, Isaac Pattis, James Edgell
First: 2026-04-28T22:05:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T17:07:34+00:00
Comments: Accepted to Misleading Impacts Resulting from AI Generated Explanations (MIRAGE) Workshop @ IUI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI-powered language learning tools increasingly provide instant, personalised feedback to millions of learners worldwide. However, this feedback can fail in ways that are difficult for learners--and even teachers--to detect, potentially reinforcing misconceptions and eroding learning outcomes over extended use. We present a portion of L2-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating AI systems in language education that includes (but is not limited to) six critical dimensions of effective feedback: diagnostic accuracy, awareness of appropriacy, causes of error, prioritisation, guidance for improvement, and supporting self-regulation. We analyse how AI systems can fail with respect to these dimensions. These failures, which we argue are conducive to "explainability pitfalls," are AI-generated explanations that appear helpful on the surface but are fundamentally flawed, increasing the risk of attainment, human-AI interaction, and socioaffective harms. We discuss how the specific context of language learning amplifies these risks and outline open questions we believe merit more attention when designing evaluation frameworks specifically. Our analysis aims to expand the community's understanding of both the typology of explainability pitfalls and the contextual dynamics in which they may occur in order to encourage AI developers to better design safe, trustworthy, and effective AI explanations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：这并非解释：将解释失败视为语言学习系统中的可解释性陷阱
AI驱动的语言学习工具正为全球数百万学习者提供即时个性化反馈。然而，此类反馈可能以学习者甚至教师难以察觉的方式失效，从而强化误解并长期削弱学习成果。本文介绍了L2-Bench基准的一部分，该基准用于评估AI在语言教育中的表现，涵盖（但不限于）六个关键的反馈维度：诊断准确性、恰当性意识、错误原因、优先级排序、改进建议及自我调节支持。我们分析了AI系统在这些维度上的失效方式。我们认为，这些失效属于“可解释性陷阱”——即表面看似有益但本质有缺陷的AI生成解释，会增加学习成效、人机交互及社会情感层面的风险。我们探讨语言学习特定语境如何放大这些风险，并指出在设计评估框架时值得更多关注的开放性问题。我们的分析旨在拓展社区对可解释性陷阱类型及其发生情境的理解，以鼓励AI开发者设计更安全、可信且有效的AI解释。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to understand how AI-generated explanations in language learning tools can mislead users despite appearing helpful, this study introduces L2-Bench, a benchmark evaluating six key dimensions of feedback quality. By analyzing real-world AI system failures across these dimensions—including diagnostic accuracy, error causality, and self-regulation support—the authors identify explainability pitfalls: explanations that seem useful but contain fundamental flaws, potentially reinforcing misconceptions and harming learning outcomes. Their analysis reveals that language learning’s context uniquely amplifies these risks, and they call for more nuanced evaluation frameworks that account for both the typology of failures and their socioaffective consequences.
为理解AI语言学习系统如何在看似有益的情况下误导用户，本研究引入L2-Bench基准，从诊断准确性、适切性意识等六个关键维度评估AI反馈。通过对AI生成解释的系统分析，作者识别出‘可解释性陷阱’——表面有益但本质缺陷的解释，可能强化误解并损害学习效果。研究指出语言学习的特殊语境加剧了此类陷阱，呼吁构建更注重情境的评估框架，以引导更安全、可信的AI设计。
Instrumentation for Imitation Learning: Enhancing Training Datasets for Clothes Hanger Insertion
Authors: Remko Proesmans, Thomas Lips, Francis wyffels
First: 2026-05-22T16:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:59:55+00:00
Comments: Accepted for presentation at ICRA2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large behaviour models have transformed the field of robotic manipulation, but prohibitive data requirements have thus far prevented a revolution similar to vision language models. We believe that instrumentation, i.e. sensor integration in objects, can provide invaluable state information and enable efficient learning for robotic manipulation. In this paper, we present instrumented imitation learning of clothes hanger insertion. Using 180 teleoperated demonstrations, we train diffusion policies with and without access to instrumentation data. Results show that policies leveraging instrumentation outperform vision-only counterparts by 14-25 %pt and exhibit greater task awareness. Crucially, a black-box imitation learning policy learns to prioritise instrumentation signals without explicit guidance. In addition, enhancing the teleoperation dataset with rollouts from an instrumented expert policy, enables a vision-only student policy to achieve performance comparable to the instrumented expert, thereby surpassing the original vision-only policy. These findings establish instrumentation as a promising strategy to enhance imitation learning for robotic manipulation. Datasets are available on Zenodo.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于模仿学习的仪器化：增强衣物挂钩插入任务的训练数据集
大型行为模型已改变机器人操作领域，但其高昂的数据需求至今阻碍了类似视觉语言模型那样的革命。我们认为，仪器化（即在物体中集成传感器）可提供宝贵的环境状态信息，从而有效支持机器人操作的学习。本文提出一种仪器化模仿学习方法，用于衣物挂钩插入任务。通过180个远程操作演示，我们训练了有无仪器化数据支持的扩散策略。结果表明，利用仪器化数据的策略比仅依赖视觉的策略性能高出14-25个百分点，且更具任务意识。关键的是，一个黑盒模仿学习策略无需显式引导即可学会优先使用仪器化信号。此外，通过在远程操作数据集中加入仪器化专家策略的 rollout 数据，仅依赖视觉的学生策略可达到与仪器化专家相当的性能，从而超越原始仅依赖视觉的策略。这些发现确立了仪器化作为增强机器人操作模仿学习的有前景策略。数据集已发布于Zenodo。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high data demands limiting robotic manipulation progress, this study explores instrumentation—sensor integration into objects—to improve imitation learning for clothes hanger insertion. Using 180 teleoperated demonstrations, the authors train diffusion policies with and without instrumentation data, finding that instrumented policies outperform vision-only ones by 14–25 percentage points and show greater task awareness. Notably, a black-box policy autonomously prioritizes instrumentation signals without explicit instruction, and augmenting the teleoperation dataset with rollouts from an instrumented expert enables a vision-only student to match the expert’s performance, surpassing the original vision-only baseline.
为解决机器人操作领域因数据需求过高而受限的问题，本研究探索在物体中集成传感器（即‘仪器化’）以提升模仿学习效果，应用于衣架插入任务。基于180个遥操作演示，作者训练了有无仪器化数据的扩散策略，结果表明仪器化策略比纯视觉策略高出14–25个百分点且更具任务感知能力。值得注意的是，黑盒策略能自主优先使用仪器化信号而无需显式引导；此外，将仪器化专家策略的 rollout 数据加入遥操作数据集后，纯视觉学生策略可达到专家水平，超越原始纯视觉策略。
Mechanistic Interpretability of EEG Foundation Models via Sparse Autoencoders
Authors: William Lehn-Schiøler, Magnus Ruud Kjær, Rahul Thapa, Magnus Guldberg Pedersen, Anton Mosquera Storgaard, Nick Williams, Radu Gatej, Tue Lehn-Schiøler, Andreas Brink-Kjær, Sadasivan Puthusserypady, Sándor Beniczky, James Zou, Lars Kai Hansen
First: 2026-05-13T16:02:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:58:48+00:00
Comments: Preprint. 14 pages, 7 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
EEG foundation models achieve state-of-the-art clinical performance, yet the internal computations driving their predictions remain opaque: a barrier to clinical trust. We apply TopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) across three architecturally distinct EEG transformers: SleepFM, REVE, and LaBraM to extract sparse feature dictionaries from their embeddings. By grounding these features in a clinical taxonomy (abnormality, age, sex, and medication), we benchmark monosemanticity and entanglement across architectures. A single hyperparameter procedure, driven by an intrinsic dictionary health audit, transfers robustly across all three architectures. Via concept steering, we introduce a "target vs. off-target" probe area metric to quantify steering selectivity and reveal three operational regimes: selectively steerable, encoded but entangled, and non-encoded. This framework exposes critical representational failures: "wrecking-ball" interventions that collapse global model performance, and clinical entanglements, such as age-pathology confounding, where it is impossible to suppress one concept without corrupting the other. Finally, a spectral decoder maps these interventions back to the amplitude spectrum, translating latent manipulations into physiologically interpretable frequency signatures, such as pathological slow-wave suppression and $α$-band restoration.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过稀疏自编码器对EEG基础模型的机制可解释性研究
EEG基础模型已实现临床性能的最先进水平，但驱动其预测的内部计算过程仍不透明，这阻碍了临床信任。我们对三种结构各异的EEG变压器（SleepFM、REVE和LaBraM）应用TopK稀疏自编码器（SAEs），从其嵌入中提取稀疏特征字典。通过将这些特征与临床分类体系（异常、年龄、性别和用药）相结合，我们评估了不同架构间的单义性和纠缠程度。一个由内在字典健康审计驱动的单一超参数过程可在所有三种架构间稳健迁移。通过概念引导，我们引入“目标 vs. 非目标”探针区域指标，量化引导选择性，并揭示三种操作模式：选择性可引导、编码但纠缠、未编码。该框架揭示了关键的表征缺陷：“拆除式”干预导致全局模型性能崩溃，以及临床纠缠（如年龄-病理混淆），即无法抑制一个概念而不破坏另一个。最后，频谱解码器将这些干预映射回幅度谱，将潜在操纵转化为生理可解释的频率特征，如病理性慢波抑制和α波恢复。
Summary / 总结
To address the lack of interpretability in EEG foundation models despite their clinical performance, researchers applied TopK Sparse Autoencoders across three distinct EEG transformer architectures—SleepFM, REVE, and LaBraM—to extract sparse feature dictionaries grounded in clinical concepts like abnormality, age, sex, and medication. A single hyperparameter-driven procedure, validated via intrinsic dictionary health audits, transferred consistently across models, enabling benchmarking of monosemanticity and entanglement. Experimental results revealed three operational regimes in concept steering: selectively steerable, encoded but entangled, and non-encoded, exposing critical failures such as 'wrecking-ball' interventions that degrade global performance and clinical entanglements like age-pathology confounding. A spectral decoder further translated latent manipulations into physiologically interpretable frequency signatures, including pathological slow-wave suppression and α-band restoration.
为解决EEG基础模型虽临床表现优异但内部机制不透明的问题，研究者在SleepFM、REVE和LaBraM三个不同架构的EEG Transformer上应用TopK稀疏自编码器，提取与临床概念（如异常、年龄、性别、用药）相关的稀疏特征字典。通过单一超参数驱动的内在字典健康审计流程，该方法在各架构间稳健迁移，支持概念引导并揭示三种操作模式：可选择性引导、编码但纠缠、未编码。主要发现包括“毁灭性干预”导致整体性能崩溃，以及临床纠缠现象（如年龄与病理相互干扰），抑制一个概念会破坏另一个。最终，频谱解码器将潜空间干预映射为生理可解释的频谱特征，如病理性慢波抑制与α波恢复。
Learning a Particle Dynamics Model with Real-world Videos
Authors: Chanho Kim, Suhas V. Sumukh, Li Fuxin
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-22T16:56:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:56:27+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026 Findings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Data-driven learning approaches for physics simulation, sometimes referred to as world models, have emerged as promising alternatives to traditional physics simulators due to their differentiable nature. Prior work has demonstrated impressive results in predicting the motions of rigid and non-rigid objects in complex scenes involving multiple interacting bodies. However, these models are typically trained in simulated environments because obtaining perfect state information such as complete scene point clouds and point correspondences over time is challenging in real-world settings. This reliance on synthetic data can limit their applicability when the sim-to-real gap is large. In this work, we aim to overcome these limitations by introducing a novel framework for training neural object dynamics models directly from unlabeled real-world videos. Specifically, we propose to learn a particle-based dynamics model compatible with a Gaussian splatting framework, which operates on dense particles derived from Gaussians (i.e., particles with scales and rotations) and predicts their position and rotation changes over time. The model is trained via rendering supervision, enabling learning from real-world videos without requiring particle-level labeled states. Our model operates directly on dense Gaussians without relying on heuristic subsampling anchor points. To enable this study, we also present a real-world dataset consisting of about 500 videos capturing diverse object interactions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用真实世界视频学习粒子动力学模型
数据驱动的物理仿真学习方法（有时称为世界模型）因其可微特性，已成为传统物理模拟器的有前景替代方案。先前工作已在复杂多体交互场景中成功预测刚性和非刚性物体的运动。然而，这些模型通常在模拟环境中训练，因为真实世界中获取完整场景点云及时间对应关系等完美状态信息极具挑战性。对合成数据的依赖限制了其在仿真与现实差距较大的场景中的适用性。本文旨在通过提出一种新颖框架，直接从无标签真实世界视频中训练神经物体动力学模型。具体而言，我们提出学习一种与高斯点渲染框架兼容的粒子动力学模型，该模型基于从高斯分布中导出的密集粒子（即带尺度和旋转的粒子），预测其随时间的位置和旋转变化。模型通过渲染监督训练，无需粒子级标注状态即可从真实视频中学习。该模型直接作用于密集高斯分布，无需依赖启发式采样锚点。为支持本研究，我们还构建了一个包含约500个视频的真实世界数据集，涵盖多样化的物体交互。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of physics simulation models trained solely on synthetic data due to the sim-to-real gap, this work proposes a novel framework to learn particle-based dynamics directly from unlabeled real-world videos. The method employs a Gaussian splatting-compatible model that operates on dense particles derived from Gaussians, predicting their position and rotation over time using rendering supervision without requiring labeled particle states. Evaluated on a new real-world dataset of 500 videos capturing diverse object interactions, the model demonstrates strong generalization and accuracy in predicting motion dynamics without relying on synthetic training data or heuristic anchor point subsampling.
为克服传统物理模拟器依赖合成数据导致的仿真到现实差距问题，本文提出一种直接从无标签真实视频中训练神经动力学模型的新方法。该方法构建了一个与高斯点渲染框架兼容的粒子动力学模型，基于密集高斯派生的粒子预测其位置与旋转变化。模型通过渲染监督训练，无需粒子级标注状态。在包含500段真实视频的自建数据集上测试，模型在复杂物体交互场景中展现出良好的真实世界泛化能力，且无需依赖启发式采样或仿真环境。
MuellerPT: Decomposition Driven Pretraining for Dense Learning in Mueller Polarimetry
Authors: Adam Tlemsani, Yingdian Li, Maxime Giot, Naim Slim, Christopher J. Peters, Abhijeet Ghosh, Daniel S. Elson
Venue: MICCAI 2026
First: 2026-05-22T16:48:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:48:57+00:00
Comments: Accepted to 29th International Conference on Medical Image Computing and Computer Assisted Intervention (MICCAI 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mueller matrix imaging provides rich, physically meaningful contrast for biomedical tissue analysis, but supervised learning is hindered by scarce dense annotations and strong domain shifts across specimens and acquisition settings. We introduce MuellerPT, a physics guided pre-training approach that learns transferable dense representations by predicting Lu-Chipman decomposition maps from per-pixel 4x4 Mueller matrices. To scale pre-training, we collected a new large Multispectral Animal Polarimetric Organ dataset (MAP-Org). The pre-trained encoder is adapted with a segmentation head for grey vs. white matter segmentation in lamb brain. A classification head is used for colorectal cancer vs. non-cancer classification. Both segmentation and classification are evaluated across few-shot learning scenarios. In segmentation, MuellerPT improves label efficiency and cross specimen transfer compared to models without pre-training, achieving an absolute DICE gain of over 20% compared to the baseline trained from scratch when using 5% of the training data. In classification, MuellerPT also enhances label efficiency, improving overall accuracy by 8% compared to the baseline when using 1% of the training data. We demonstrate MuellerPT's robustness to domain shift with a qualitative evaluation of its predicted Lu-Chipman maps on an ex vivo human oesophagus sample. These results suggest that predicting Lu-Chipman decomposition is an effective and practical pretext task for robust biomedical inference from Mueller polarimetry and can pave the way for future work on label efficient Mueller imaging.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of dense annotations and domain shifts in Mueller polarimetry for biomedical imaging, MuellerPT introduces a physics-guided pre-training method that learns transferable representations by predicting Lu-Chipman decomposition maps from per-pixel Mueller matrices. Trained on a newly collected large multispectral animal polarimetric organ dataset, the pre-trained encoder is adapted for grey-white matter segmentation and colorectal cancer classification tasks. Experiments show MuellerPT significantly improves label efficiency and cross-specimen generalization: achieving over 20% absolute DICE gain with 5% training data in segmentation and 8% accuracy boost with 1% data in classification, outperforming models trained from scratch. Qualitative validation on human tissue confirms robustness to domain shift, demonstrating the effectiveness of Lu-Chipman decomposition as a pretext task for label-efficient Mueller imaging.
为了解决穆勒偏振成像中密集标注稀缺和跨样本域偏移的问题，MuellerPT提出一种基于物理引导的预训练方法，通过从每个像素的4x4穆勒矩阵预测卢-奇普曼分解图来学习可迁移的表示。该方法在新构建的大规模多光谱动物偏振器官数据集上训练，预训练编码器随后用于灰质与白质分割和结直肠癌分类任务。实验表明，MuellerPT显著提升标注效率：在分割任务中，仅用5%训练数据即可比从头训练基线模型提升20%以上的DICE值；在分类任务中，仅用1%训练数据即可提升8%的准确率。对人组织样本的定性评估验证了其对域偏移的鲁棒性，表明该方法在生物医学推理中具有高效标注和迁移能力的潜力。
Optimal Solutions for the Moving Target Vehicle Routing Problem with Obstacles via Lazy Branch and Price
Authors: Anoop Bhat, Geordan Gutow, Surya Singh, Zhongqiang Ren, Sivakumar Rathinam, Howie Choset
First: 2026-03-23T12:10:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:48:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Moving Target Vehicle Routing Problem with Obstacles (MT-VRP-O) seeks trajectories for several agents that collectively intercept a set of moving targets. Each target has one or more time windows where it must be visited, and the agents must avoid static obstacles and satisfy speed and capacity constraints. We introduce Lazy Branch-and-Price with Relaxed Continuity (Lazy BPRC), which finds optimal solutions for the MT-VRP-O. Lazy BPRC applies the branch-and-price framework for VRPs, which alternates between a restricted master problem (RMP) and a pricing problem. The RMP aims to select a sequence of target-time window pairings (called a tour) for each agent to follow, from a limited subset of tours. The pricing problem adds tours to the limited subset. Conventionally, solving the RMP requires computing the cost for an agent to follow each tour in the limited subset. Computing these costs in the MT-VRP-O is computationally intensive, since it requires collision-free motion planning between moving targets. Lazy BPRC defers cost computations by solving the RMP using lower bounds on the costs of each tour, computed via motion planning with relaxed continuity constraints. We lazily evaluate the true costs of tours as-needed. We compute a tour's cost by searching for a shortest path on a Graph of Convex Sets (GCS), and we accelerate this search using our continuity relaxation method. We demonstrate that Lazy BPRC runs up to an order of magnitude faster than two ablations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于延迟分支定界与标价法的带障碍移动目标车辆路径问题的最优解法
移动目标车辆路径问题（MT-VRP-O）旨在为多个代理规划轨迹，使其协同拦截一组移动目标。每个目标有至少一个必须访问的时间窗，代理需避开静态障碍并满足速度与容量约束。本文提出一种名为“延迟分支定界与标价法（Lazy BPRC）”的算法，用于求解MT-VRP-O。该算法基于车辆路径问题的分支定界与标价框架，交替求解受限主问题（RMP）与定价问题。RMP旨在从有限的路径集合中为每个代理选择目标-时间窗配对序列（称为路径）。定价问题则向该有限集合添加新路径。传统方法需计算代理执行每条路径的成本，但在MT-VRP-O中，因需进行移动目标间的无碰撞路径规划，计算成本极为耗时。Lazy BPRC通过使用松弛连续性约束下的路径成本下界求解RMP，延迟真实成本计算，按需评估。路径成本通过在凸集图（GCS）上搜索最短路径计算，并利用连续性松弛方法加速搜索。实验表明，Lazy BPRC比两种消融实验快一个数量级。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently route multiple agents to intercept moving targets while avoiding static obstacles and respecting speed and capacity constraints, this paper introduces Lazy Branch-and-Price with Relaxed Continuity (Lazy BPRC) for the Moving Target Vehicle Routing Problem with Obstacles. The method extends the branch-and-price framework by deferring exact cost computations for tours in the restricted master problem, instead using relaxed continuity-based lower bounds to accelerate RMP solution. True tour costs are computed lazily via shortest path search on a Graph of Convex Sets, accelerated by the continuity relaxation technique. Experimental results show Lazy BPRC achieves up to an order of magnitude faster performance compared to two ablated versions.
为高效解决多智能体拦截移动目标并避开障碍物、满足速度与容量约束的问题，本文提出一种名为Lazy Branch-and-Price with Relaxed Continuity（Lazy BPRC）的算法。该方法基于分支定界与列生成框架，通过受限主问题选择路径、定价问题添加新路径，但利用松弛连续性约束计算路径成本下界以避免昂贵的碰撞规避规划。实际路径成本仅在需要时按需计算，采用凸集图与连续性松弛加速搜索。实验表明，Lazy BPRC相较两种消融版本提速达一个数量级，且保持最优解质量。
Compression as Adaptation: Implicit Visual Representation with Diffusion Foundation Models
Authors: Zongyu Guo, Jiajun He, Zhaoyang Jia, Xiaoyi Zhang, Jiahao Li, Xiao Li, Bin Li, José Miguel Hernández-Lobato, Yan Lu
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-03-08T12:55:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:41:22+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern visual generative models acquire rich visual knowledge through large-scale training, yet existing visual representations (such as pixels, latents, or tokens) remain external to the model and cannot directly exploit this knowledge for compact storage or reuse. In this work, we introduce a new visual representation framework that encodes a signal as a function, which is parametrized by low-rank adaptations attached to a frozen visual generative model. Such implicit representations of visual signals, \textit{e.g.}, an 81-frame video, can further be hashed into a single compact vector, achieving strong perceptual video compression at extremely low bitrates. Beyond basic compression, the functional nature of this representation enables inference-time scaling and control, allowing additional refinement on the compression performance. More broadly, as the implicit representations directly act as a function of the generation process, this suggests a unified framework bridging visual compression and generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：压缩作为适应：基于扩散基础模型的隐式视觉表示
现代视觉生成模型通过大规模训练获得丰富的视觉知识，但现有视觉表示（如像素、潜在变量或标记）仍独立于模型，无法直接利用该知识实现紧凑存储或复用。本文提出一种新的视觉表示框架，将信号编码为函数，该函数由附加在冻结视觉生成模型上的低秩适应参数化。此类视觉信号的隐式表示（例如81帧视频）可进一步哈希为单个紧凑向量，在极低码率下实现强大的感知视频压缩。除基本压缩外，该表示的函数特性支持推理时的扩展与控制，从而进一步优化压缩性能。更广泛地说，由于隐式表示直接作为生成过程的函数，这暗示了一种统一框架，连接视觉压缩与生成。
Summary / 总结
Modern visual generative models acquire rich visual knowledge through large-scale training, yet existing visual representations (such as pixels, latents, or tokens) remain external to the model and cannot directly exploit this knowledge for compact storage or reuse.
为解决现有视觉生成模型中外部存储视觉表征效率低的问题，本文提出一种隐式表征框架，将视觉信号编码为由低秩适配器参数化的函数，附加于冻结的扩散模型之上。该方法可将如81帧视频等复杂信号压缩为单个向量，实现极低码率下的高感知质量压缩。实验表明，该方法在压缩性能上表现优异，同时支持推理时的可扩展性与可控性，揭示了压缩与生成之间统一框架的潜力。
SFG-ROS: A Resource-Aware Framework for Dense Multi-Agent Perception
Authors: Constantin Blessing, Elias Geiger, Jakob Häringer, Dennis Grewe, Markus Enzweiler
First: 2026-05-22T16:41:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:41:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Deploying heterogeneous multi-agent robot fleets for collaborative perception requires robust data exchange and scalable software architectures. However, standard ROS 2 implementations often suffer from network saturation, namespace collisions, and severe computational overhead when distributing dense sensor streams across devices. To address these bottlenecks, we present SFG-ROS, a resource-aware multi-agent software framework designed for dynamic fleet deployments. SFG-ROS addresses these challenges through three primary contributions. First, schema-driven traffic routing isolates high-frequency intra-agent traffic from the global network using a programmatic fully qualified name schema and targeted Fast DDS routing. Second, an on-demand centralized decoding pipeline automatically offloads high-bandwidth sensor data decompression, eliminating redundant processing across local consumer nodes. Finally, a hardware-agnostic container pipeline dynamically adapts to heterogeneous accelerators, seamlessly bridging development environments with zero-touch, field-ready execution. We evaluate the framework using a fleet of wheeled and legged robots equipped with LiDAR and stereo depth cameras. Experimental results show SFG-ROS bounds network traffic to $\mathcal{O}(1)$ and, by replacing redundant decompression with lightweight IPC, reduces the per-subscriber CPU scaling penalty by 72.3\% versus standard ROS 2, all while maintaining low latency. Finally, we publish SFG-ROS under a permissive license, available via \href{https://iis-esslingen.github.io/sfg-ros}{iis-esslingen.github.io/sfg-ros}.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SFG-ROS：面向密集多智能体感知的资源感知框架
部署异构多智能体机器人集群进行协同感知，需要稳健的数据交换和可扩展的软件架构。然而，标准ROS 2实现常因在设备间分发密集传感器流而出现网络饱和、命名空间冲突和严重计算开销。为解决这些瓶颈，我们提出SFG-ROS，一种面向动态集群部署的资源感知多智能体软件框架。其主要贡献包括：首先，基于模式的流量路由通过程序化完全限定名称模式和定向Fast DDS路由，将高频智能体内部流量与全局网络隔离；其次，按需集中解码管道自动卸载高带宽传感器数据解压缩，消除本地消费者节点的冗余处理；最后，硬件无关容器管道动态适配异构加速器，无缝连接开发环境与零接触、现场就绪的执行环境。我们通过配备LiDAR和立体深度相机的轮式与腿式机器人集群评估该框架。实验结果表明，SFG-ROS将网络流量限制在O(1)，并通过以轻量级IPC替代冗余解压缩，相较标准ROS 2降低每订阅者CPU扩展开销72.3%，同时保持低延迟。最后，我们以宽松许可发布SFG-ROS，可通过https://iis-esslingen.github.io/sfg-ros获取。
Summary / 总结
Deploying heterogeneous multi-agent robot fleets for collaborative perception requires robust data exchange and scalable software architectures.
为支持异构多智能体机器人集群的高效协同感知，SFG-ROS 提出了一种资源感知的软件框架，旨在解决 ROS 2 在分布式密集传感器数据传输中常见的网络拥塞、命名空间冲突和计算开销问题。该框架通过基于模式的流量路由分离智能体内部高频通信与全局网络通信，通过按需集中解码管道卸载传感器数据解压缩任务以避免重复处理，并通过硬件无关容器系统动态适配不同加速器。在配备激光雷达和立体相机的轮式与腿式机器人集群上测试表明，SFG-ROS 将网络流量控制在 O(1) 级别，相较标准 ROS 2 减少了 72.3% 的每订阅者 CPU 开销，同时保持低延迟。
Broken Memories: Detecting and Mitigating Memorization in Diffusion Models with Degraded Generations
Authors: Yuanmin Huang, Mi Zhang, Chen Chen, Feifei Li, Geng Hong, Xiaoyu You, Min Yang
Venue: KDD 2026
First: 2026-05-21T06:36:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:38:43+00:00
Comments: KDD 2026, extended version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While diffusion models excel at generating high-quality images, their tendency to memorize training data poses significant privacy and copyright risks. In this work, we for the first time identify that memorization induces internal numerical instability, often manifesting as visually ``broken'' artifacts. Inspired by stability analysis in numerical methods, we introduce empirical stability regions based on latent update norms to quantitatively characterize stable behavior during generation. Leveraging this, we propose a principled, on-the-fly framework for step-wise detection and adaptive mitigation. Our approach suppresses memorization without altering prompts or guidance, thereby preserving semantic fidelity and image quality. Extensive experiments on Stable Diffusion 1.4 demonstrate that our method achieves an AUC $>0.999$ detection performance and a $0.0\%$ memorization rate after mitigation with negligible overhead ($\approx0.01$s per image).
中文标题/摘要
标题：破碎的记忆：通过退化生成检测和缓解扩散模型中的记忆化
尽管扩散模型擅长生成高质量图像，但其记忆训练数据的倾向会带来严重的隐私和版权风险。本研究首次发现，记忆化会导致内部数值不稳定，常表现为视觉上“破碎”的伪影。受数值方法稳定性分析的启发，我们基于潜在更新范数引入经验稳定区域，以定量表征生成过程中的稳定行为。基于此，我们提出了一种原理性的、实时的分步检测与自适应缓解框架。该方法在不改变提示或引导的情况下抑制记忆化，从而保持语义保真度和图像质量。在Stable Diffusion 1.4上的大量实验表明，我们的方法检测性能AUC>0.999，缓解后记忆率为0.0%，且开销极小（每张图像约0.01秒）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the privacy and copyright risks from diffusion models memorizing training data, this work identifies that memorization causes internal numerical instability, visible as 'broken' artifacts. It introduces empirical stability regions based on latent update norms to quantify generation stability and proposes an on-the-fly framework for step-wise detection and adaptive mitigation without altering prompts. Experiments on Stable Diffusion 1.4 show the method achieves near-perfect detection (AUC >0.999) and eliminates memorization (0.0%) with minimal computational overhead (~0.01s per image).
鉴于扩散模型易记忆训练数据带来的隐私与版权风险，本文首次发现记忆会导致内部数值不稳定，表现为生成过程中的“破损”视觉异常。作者基于潜空间更新范数构建经验稳定域以量化生成稳定性，并提出一种实时检测与自适应缓解框架，无需修改提示或引导即可抑制记忆。在Stable Diffusion 1.4上的实验表明，该方法检测准确率接近完美（AUC > 0.999），完全消除记忆（0.0%），且计算开销极小（约0.01秒/张图像）。
Interactive Query Answering on Knowledge Graphs with Soft Entity Constraints
Authors: Daniel Daza, Alberto Bernardi, Luca Costabello, Christophe Gueret, Masoud Mansoury, Michael Cochez, Martijn Schut
First: 2025-08-19T09:09:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:37:41+00:00
Comments: Accepted in Transactions on Machine Learning Research (2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Methods for query answering over incomplete knowledge graphs retrieve entities that are likely to be answers, which is particularly useful when such answers cannot be reached by direct graph traversal due to missing edges. However, existing approaches have focused on queries formalized using first-order-logic. In practice, many real-world queries involve constraints that are inherently vague or context-dependent, such as preferences for attributes or related categories. Addressing this gap, we introduce the problem of query answering with soft constraints. We formalize the problem and introduce two efficient methods designed to adjust query answer scores by incorporating soft constraints without disrupting the original answers to a query. These methods are lightweight, requiring tuning only two parameters or a small neural network trained to capture soft constraints while maintaining the original ranking structure. To evaluate the task, we extend existing QA benchmarks by generating datasets with soft constraints. Our experiments demonstrate that our methods can capture soft constraints while maintaining robust query answering performance and adding very little overhead. With our work, we explore a new and flexible way to interact with graph databases that allows users to specify their preferences by providing examples interactively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于软实体约束的知识图谱交互式查询回答
针对不完整知识图谱的查询回答方法通常检索可能的答案实体，这在因缺失边而无法通过直接图遍历获得答案时尤其有用。然而，现有方法主要针对使用一阶逻辑形式化的查询。在实践中，许多现实世界查询包含固有模糊或依赖上下文的约束，如属性偏好或相关类别。为填补这一空白，我们引入了软约束查询回答问题，形式化该问题并提出两种高效方法，通过整合软约束调整查询答案得分，而不破坏原始查询答案。这些方法轻量级，仅需调整两个参数或一个小型神经网络以捕捉软约束，同时保持原始排序结构。为评估该任务，我们扩展现有QA基准，生成包含软约束的数据集。实验表明，我们的方法能有效捕捉软约束，同时保持稳健的查询回答性能且增加极少开销。通过本工作，我们探索了一种新的灵活方式与图数据库交互，允许用户通过交互式示例指定偏好。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to handle vague or context-dependent user preferences in real-world knowledge graph queries, this work introduces interactive query answering with soft entity constraints. It formalizes the problem and proposes two lightweight methods that adjust answer scores by incorporating soft constraints without altering original query results, requiring only two parameters or a small neural network. Experiments on extended QA benchmarks show that these methods effectively capture soft constraints while preserving strong baseline performance and minimal computational overhead, enabling flexible, example-driven interaction with graph databases.
为应对现实世界知识图谱查询中用户偏好模糊或依赖上下文的问题，本文提出带软实体约束的交互式查询回答方法。该方法形式化了问题并设计了两种轻量级方案，通过调整答案得分引入软约束，同时保留原始查询结果，仅需两个参数或小型神经网络即可实现。实验表明，这些方法能有效捕捉软约束，同时保持高查询准确率和极低计算开销，支持用户通过示例交互式地表达偏好。
Are Targeted Data Poisoning Attacks as Effective as We Think?
Authors: William Xu, Chenyu Zhang, Yihan Wang, Matthew Y. R. Yang, Zuoqiu Liu, Gautam Kamath, Yaoliang Yu, Yiwei Lu
First: 2025-09-08T17:14:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:37:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Targeted data poisoning attacks manipulate model predictions on specific test samples by injecting malicious data into training. Yet existing evaluations report average attack success rates over randomly selected targets, obscuring true worst-case effectiveness. We argue that the right evaluation focuses on the hardest samples to poison. The same reasoning applies to defense: since targeted attacks leave no footprint at the distribution level, defenders should proactively identify the most vulnerable samples and apply targeted countermeasures. Given a test dataset, this paper identifies both the easiest and hardest to poison examples based on only clean model information. Specifically, we offer coarse evaluations using clean training dynamics, and fine-grained classification on poison class using poison distances and budgets. Our experiments show these metrics reliably stratify samples by poisoning vulnerability, enabling both rigorous worst-case evaluation and proactive vulnerability-aware defense.
中文标题/摘要
标题：定向数据投毒攻击的有效性是否如我们所想的那样高？
定向数据投毒攻击通过向训练数据中注入恶意数据，操纵模型对特定测试样本的预测。然而现有评估报告的是在随机选择的目标样本上的平均攻击成功率，掩盖了真实最坏情况下的效果。我们认为，正确的评估应聚焦于最难被投毒的样本。同样的推理也适用于防御：由于定向攻击在分布层面不留痕迹，防御者应主动识别最脆弱的样本并采取针对性的反制措施。给定一个测试数据集，本文仅基于干净模型信息，识别出最容易和最难被投毒的样本。具体而言，我们提供基于干净训练动态的粗粒度评估，以及基于投毒距离和预算的投毒类别细粒度分类。实验表明，这些指标能可靠地区分样本的投毒脆弱性，从而支持严格的最坏情况评估和主动的脆弱性感知防御。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the gap in evaluating targeted data poisoning attacks by arguing that current methods, which average success rates over random targets, fail to capture the true worst-case effectiveness. The authors propose evaluating attacks on the most vulnerable samples instead, and suggest defenders should proactively identify and protect these high-risk instances. Using only clean model information, they develop metrics—based on training dynamics and poison distance/budget—to classify samples as easy or hard to poison. Experiments demonstrate that these metrics reliably rank samples by vulnerability, enabling both accurate worst-case attack assessment and targeted defense strategies.
该论文质疑当前针对数据投毒攻击的评估方式，即通常在随机目标上计算平均成功率，认为应聚焦于最易被攻击的样本。作者提出仅利用干净模型行为和投毒距离指标，即可识别出最易和最难被投毒的样本，无需依赖已投毒数据。实验表明，这些指标能有效区分样本的投毒脆弱性，从而支持更严格的最坏情况评估，并指导防御方优先对高风险样本实施针对性防护。
Operator-Based Generalization Bound for Deep Learning: Insights on Multi-Task Learning
Authors: Mahdi Mohammadigohari, Giuseppe Di Fatta, Giuseppe Nicosia, Panos M. Pardalos
First: 2025-12-22T09:18:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:31:56+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 11th International Conference on Machine Learning, Optimization, and Data Science (LOD), Castiglione della Pescaia, Italy, September 21-24, 2025. To appear in Lecture Notes in Computer Science (LNCS), volume 16467
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents novel generalization bounds for vector-valued neural networks and deep kernel methods, focusing on multi-task learning through an operator-theoretic framework. Our key development lies in strategically combining a Koopman based approach with existing techniques, achieving tighter generalization guarantees compared to traditional norm-based bounds. To mitigate computational challenges associated with Koopman-based methods, we introduce sketching techniques applicable to vector valued neural networks. These techniques yield excess risk bounds under generic Lipschitz losses, providing performance guarantees for applications including robust and multiple quantile regression. Furthermore, we propose a novel deep learning framework, deep vector-valued reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces (vvRKHS), leveraging Perron Frobenius (PF) operators to enhance deep kernel methods. We derive a new Rademacher generalization bound for this framework, explicitly addressing underfitting and overfitting through kernel refinement strategies. This work offers novel insights into the generalization properties of multitask learning with deep learning architectures, an area that has been relatively unexplored until recent developments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于算子的深度学习泛化界：多任务学习的见解
本文为向量值神经网络和深度核方法提出了新颖的泛化界，通过算子理论框架聚焦多任务学习。我们的核心进展在于巧妙结合Koopman方法与现有技术，相较传统范数基泛化界获得更紧的保证。为缓解Koopman方法相关的计算挑战，我们引入适用于向量值神经网络的草图技术，这些技术在通用Lipschitz损失下提供超额风险界，为鲁棒及多分位数回归等应用提供性能保障。此外，我们提出一种新型深度学习框架——深度向量值再生核希尔伯特空间（vvRKHS），利用Perron-Frobenius算子增强深度核方法。我们为此框架推导出新的Rademacher泛化界，通过核优化策略明确处理欠拟合与过拟合问题。本工作为深度学习架构下多任务学习的泛化特性提供了新颖见解，该领域此前相对未被充分探索。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for tighter generalization guarantees in multi-task deep learning, this paper introduces operator-based bounds using a Koopman-inspired framework combined with sketching techniques to reduce computational burden. The authors derive excess risk bounds for vector-valued neural networks under generic Lipschitz losses, enabling robust applications like quantile regression. They further propose a deep vector-valued reproducing kernel Hilbert space framework leveraging Perron-Frobenius operators, and establish a new Rademacher generalization bound that explicitly controls underfitting and overfitting via kernel refinement, offering theoretical insights into multitask learning’s generalization behavior.
为解决多任务深度学习中泛化误差的紧致性问题，本文提出基于算子理论的泛化界，结合Koopman方法与抽样技巧以降低计算复杂度。作者在通用Lipschitz损失下推导出向量值神经网络的超额风险界，支持鲁棒性应用如分位数回归。进一步提出一种基于Perron-Frobenius算子的深度向量值再生核希尔伯特空间框架，并建立新的Rademacher泛化界，通过核函数优化显式控制欠拟合与过拟合，为多任务深度学习的泛化特性提供理论洞见。
On the Koopman-Based Generalization Bounds for Multi-Task Deep Learning
Authors: Mahdi Mohammadigohari, Giuseppe Di Fatta, Giuseppe Nicosia, Panos M. Pardalos
First: 2025-12-22T09:36:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:31:31+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 11th International Conference on Machine Learning, Optimization, and Data Science (LOD), Castiglione della Pescaia, Italy, September 21-24, 2025. To appear in Lecture Notes in Computer Science (LNCS), volume 16467
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The paper establishes generalization bounds for multitask deep neural networks using operator-theoretic techniques. The authors propose a tighter bound than those derived from conventional norm based methods by leveraging small condition numbers in the weight matrices and introducing a tailored Sobolev space as an expanded hypothesis space. This enhanced bound remains valid even in single output settings, outperforming existing Koopman based bounds. The resulting framework maintains key advantages such as flexibility and independence from network width, offering a more precise theoretical understanding of multitask deep learning in the context of kernel methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于Koopman算子的多任务深度学习泛化界研究
本文利用算子理论技术，为多任务深度神经网络建立了泛化界。作者通过利用权重矩阵的小条件数，并引入定制的Sobolev空间作为扩展的假设空间，提出了比传统范数方法更紧的界。该增强的界在单输出设置下依然有效，优于现有的基于Koopman的界。所得框架保持了灵活性和与网络宽度无关等关键优势，为核方法背景下多任务深度学习提供了更精确的理论理解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for tighter theoretical guarantees in multitask deep learning, the paper derives generalization bounds using Koopman operator theory, improving upon conventional norm-based methods by exploiting small condition numbers in weight matrices and introducing a tailored Sobolev space as an expanded hypothesis space. The resulting bound is tighter than existing Koopman-based results and remains valid even in single-task settings, while preserving flexibility and independence from network width, thereby offering a more precise theoretical understanding of multitask learning within kernel methods.
为提升多任务深度学习的理论保证，该文采用算子理论方法推导泛化界，通过利用权重矩阵的小条件数并引入定制的Sobolev空间作为扩展假设空间，使新界优于传统范数方法及现有Koopman方法。该界在单任务场景下依然成立，同时保持灵活性与对网络宽度的无关性，从而为核方法框架下的多任务学习提供了更精确的理论理解。
Decomposing Queries into Tool Calls for Long-Video Keyframe Retrieval
Authors: Michal Shlapentokh-Rothman, Prachi Garg, Yu-Xiong Wang, Derek Hoiem
First: 2026-05-22T16:29:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:29:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Keyframe selection is a direct way to provide verifiable visual evidence for long-video question answering (QA). Queries differ in what they require, and finding the right frames depends on knowing what to look for. Existing keyframe selectors either score every frame against a single query, or decompose the query into a fixed schema evaluated by a single visual tool. We propose ToolMerge, a keyframe retrieval method based on decomposition and merging: an Large Language Model (LLM) based planner decomposes the query into tool calls and specifies how their per-tool rankings are merged using boolean operators. To evaluate retrieval directly, we construct Molmo-2 Moments (M2M), a benchmark in which every question is anchored to a specific time interval by construction. Across QA, question retrieval, and caption retrieval, ToolMerge is competitive with prior keyframe selectors, most notably on caption retrieval, outperforming other methods by 5%. Code and data can be found at https://github.com/michalsr/ToolMerge .
中文标题/摘要
标题：将查询分解为工具调用以实现长视频关键帧检索
关键帧选择是为长视频问答（QA）提供可验证视觉证据的直接方法。查询需求各异，找到正确帧取决于明确查找目标。现有关键帧选择器要么对每个帧按单一查询评分，要么将查询分解为固定模式并由单一视觉工具评估。我们提出ToolMerge方法，基于分解与合并：基于大语言模型（LLM）的规划器将查询分解为工具调用，并指定各工具排名如何通过布尔运算合并。为直接评估检索效果，我们构建了Molmo-2 Moments（M2M）基准，其中每个问题均通过构造锚定至特定时间区间。在QA、问题检索和字幕检索任务中，ToolMerge与现有关键帧选择器表现相当，尤其在字幕检索任务中优于其他方法5%。代码和数据见https://github.com/michalsr/ToolMerge。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to retrieve precise visual evidence from long videos, this work addresses the challenge of adapting keyframe selection to diverse query types. The authors propose ToolMerge, a method that uses an LLM-based planner to decompose complex queries into multiple tool calls, then merges per-tool rankings using boolean operators to produce final keyframe scores. Evaluated on Molmo-2 Moments, a benchmark where each question is tied to a specific time interval, ToolMerge achieves competitive performance across QA, question retrieval, and caption retrieval tasks, notably outperforming other methods by 5% in caption retrieval.
为应对长视频关键帧检索中多样化查询的需求，作者提出ToolMerge方法，利用大语言模型将查询分解为多个工具调用任务，并通过布尔运算合并各工具的排序结果。在Molmo-2 Moments基准测试中，该方法在问答、问题检索和标题检索任务上表现优异，尤其在标题检索任务中比其他方法高出5%。
It's the humans, not the data: Geopolitical bias in LLMs originates in post-training, amplified by the language of the prompt
Authors: Stuart Bladon, Brinnae Bent
First: 2026-05-22T16:29:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:29:02+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 6 figures, 2 tables, 3 appendices. Code and scenario bank: https://github.com/recozers/LLM-Bias
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
It has generally been assumed that geopolitical bias in language models originates from the training data used during the pre-training phase. We tested seven open-weight LLM pairs consisting of the base model (pre-training only) and the chat model (pre-training and post-training) from seven labs on a paired-scenario forced-choice probe over 28 country pairs in English, French, and Chinese, and found that geopolitical bias originates in post-training rather than in pre-training. Across seven AI labs, six showed shifts in the direction associated with the country or region of the model developer after post-training. This shift is strongest in Alibaba's Qwen 2.5: while the base is neutral on China-favourability (-0.15 log-odds, p=0.15), the post-trained chat variant is at +2.91 (p<10^-4), an 18x shift in odds. We also observe shifts in biases toward other countries across all models. Additionally, the magnitude of this shift depends on the language used to prompt the model: the French-made Mistral becomes pro-France only under French prompting (FR-EN shift +1.91, p<10^-4). These findings suggest that geopolitical preferences in language models are not simply inherited from large-scale internet data but are actively shaped during post-training, highlighting the need for greater transparency, auditing, and oversight of alignment processes that influence how models represent nations, cultures, and political perspectives.
Summary / 总结
This study challenges the assumption that geopolitical bias in large language models stems from pre-training data, instead demonstrating that bias emerges primarily during post-training. Researchers evaluated seven LLM pairs across seven labs using a forced-choice probe over 28 country pairs in three languages, finding that six out of seven models exhibited directional shifts in bias aligned with their developer’s country after post-training. Notably, Alibaba’s Qwen 2.5 showed an 18-fold increase in pro-China bias post-training, while Mistral’s bias toward France was language-dependent, appearing only under French prompts. These results indicate that geopolitical preferences are actively shaped during alignment, not merely inherited from training data, underscoring the need for greater scrutiny of post-training processes.
该研究挑战了语言模型地缘政治偏见源于预训练数据的传统假设，实证表明偏见主要在后训练阶段形成。研究人员对七家机构的七对LLM（基础模型与对话模型）在三种语言中对28个国家配对进行强制选择测试，发现六家机构的模型在后训练后出现与开发者所在国方向一致的偏移。例如，阿里Qwen 2.5在后训练后对中国偏好的强度提升18倍，而Mistral在法语提示下才表现出亲法倾向。结果表明，地缘政治偏好是在对齐过程中主动塑造的，而非单纯继承自训练数据，凸显了对后训练流程加强透明度与监管的必要性。
Physics-Informed Machine Learning Regulated by Finite Element Analysis for Simulation Acceleration of Melt Pool Dynamics in Laser Powder Bed Fusion
Authors: R. Sharma, Y. B. Guo
First: 2025-06-25T15:25:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:27:15+00:00
Comments: Further investigation revealed that the current version reflects an incomplete formulation and limited validation of the proposed method. We have since developed a substantially revised and extended study with updated assumptions and results, and therefore withdraw this version to prevent citation of superseded findings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Efficient simulation of Laser Powder Bed Fusion (LPBF) is crucial for process prediction due to the lasting issue of high computational cost associated with traditional numerical methods such as finite element analysis (FEA). While a Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) can predict solution fields with small training data and enables the generalization of new process parameters via transfer learning, it suffers from accuracy degradation in time-dependent problems due to the accumulation of residual and the difficulty in capturing the steep spatial and temporal gradients inherent in the LPBF process. To overcome this issue, this study develops an efficient modeling framework, FEA-Regulated Physics-Informed Neural Network (FEA-PINN), to accelerate the prediction of melt pool dynamics phenomena in an LPBF process while maintaining the FEA accuracy. The innovation of FEA-PINN manifested itself in two aspects. First, a novel strategy has been developed within the PINN model to capture the dynamic phase change of powder-liquid-solid, enabling the tracking of material status during laser melting. The model further incorporates temperature-dependent material properties, phase change behavior of the powder bed, Marangoni convection, and natural convection within the melt pool. Second, the FEA-PINN framework integrates corrective FEA simulations during inference to enforce physical consistency, reduce error drift, and capture the steep gradients. A comparative analysis shows that FEA-PINN achieves accuracy comparable to FEA while significantly reducing computational cost. The framework has been validated against benchmark FEA data for single-track scanning in LPBF.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于有限元分析调控的物理信息机器学习方法用于激光粉末床熔融熔池动力学模拟加速
由于传统数值方法（如有限元分析，FEA）计算成本高，高效模拟激光粉末床熔融（LPBF）过程对工艺预测至关重要。虽然物理信息神经网络（PINN）可利用少量训练数据预测解场并借助迁移学习实现新工艺参数的泛化，但在时间依赖性问题中因残差累积及难以捕捉LPBF过程固有的陡峭空间与时间梯度而出现精度下降。为解决此问题，本研究开发了一种高效建模框架——FEA调控物理信息神经网络（FEA-PINN），在保持FEA精度的同时加速LPBF熔池动力学现象的预测。FEA-PINN的创新体现在两方面：首先，在PINN模型中提出新策略以捕捉粉末-液态-固态的动态相变，实现激光熔化过程中材料状态的追踪，并进一步纳入温度依赖材料属性、粉末床相变行为、Marangoni对流及熔池内自然对流；其次，FEA-PINN框架在推理阶段集成校正性FEA模拟，以强制物理一致性、减少误差漂移并捕捉陡峭梯度。对比分析表明，FEA-PINN在保持与FEA相当精度的同时显著降低计算成本。该框架已通过LPBF单轨扫描的基准FEA数据验证。
Summary / 总结
Efficient simulation of Laser Powder Bed Fusion (LPBF) is crucial for process prediction due to the lasting issue of high computational cost associated with traditional numerical methods such as finite element analysis (FEA).
为解决传统有限元分析在激光粉末床熔融模拟中计算成本过高的问题，本研究提出FEA-Regulated物理信息神经网络（FEA-PINN），旨在加速熔池动力学预测并保持有限元精度。该方法结合物理信息神经网络，引入新型相变追踪机制，并融入材料属性、Marangoni对流和自然对流效应；推理阶段通过嵌入修正有限元模拟以确保物理一致性并抑制误差累积。实验结果表明，FEA-PINN在单轨扫描基准数据上与完整有限元模拟精度相当，同时显著降低计算成本。
RT-NeRV: Rethinking Hybrid Neural Representations for Video via Residual Tokenization
Authors: Yunjie Xu, Xiang Feng, Chengkai Wang, Alan Wee-Chung Liew, Xuefei Yin, Yanming Zhu
First: 2024-03-19T03:19:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:27:01+00:00
Comments: Under Review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural Representations for Videos(NeRV) have emerged as a promising paradigm for video compression by representing videos as compact neural networks with efficient decoding. Hybrid NeRV methods further improve reconstruction quality through content adaptive embeddings, but still struggle to preserve fine details at low bitrates. A key limitation is that shallow residual support in formation, although highly beneficial for reconstruction, is costly to transmit in its continuous form and is therefore underutilized. In this paper, we rethink hybrid NeRV and present RT-NeRV, a residual tokenization framework for hybrid neural video representations. The core idea is to discretize shallow residual features and inter-frame residual cues into compact residual tokens, allowing informative reconstruction support to be transmitted efficiently and exploited by the decoder. To this end, we design a residual tokenizer together with a residual-aware codebook learning strategy that improves token utilization and stabilizes training. RT-NeRV can be readily integrated into modern hybrid NeRV hosts, consistently enhancing detail preservation, reconstruction quality, and bitrate quality trade-offs. Extensive experiments on video regression and related restoration tasks show that RT-NeRV outperforms strong hybrid NeRV baselines and remains competitive with recent INR based video compression methods. These results demonstrate that residual tokenization is an effective and complementary direction for advancing hybrid neural video representations
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of transmitting continuous shallow residuals in hybrid neural video representations, this paper proposes RT-NeRV, a residual tokenization framework that discretizes residual features into compact tokens for efficient transmission and decoding. The method introduces a residual tokenizer and a residual-aware codebook learning strategy to enhance token utilization and training stability. Experimental results across video regression and restoration tasks show RT-NeRV consistently improves detail preservation and reconstruction quality while maintaining favorable bitrate trade-offs, outperforming existing hybrid NeRV baselines and remaining competitive with INR-based compression methods.
为了解决混合神经视频表示中浅层残差连续传输效率低的问题，本文提出RT-NeRV框架，将残差特征与帧间残差离散化为紧凑的残差令牌，以提升传输与解码效率。该方法设计了残差令牌器与残差感知码本学习策略，以增强令牌利用率并稳定训练。实验表明，RT-NeRV在视频回归与恢复任务中持续提升细节保留与重建质量，优化码率效率，超越现有混合NeRV基线，并与基于INR的视频压缩方法保持竞争力。
"I can't read your mind": A Study of Neurodivergent Computing Students' Experiences with Collaborative Active Learning
Authors: Cynthia Zastudil, Srishty Muthusekaran, Rayhona Nasimova, Stephen MacNeil
First: 2026-05-22T16:24:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:24:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Computing courses often feature active learning techniques that promote collaboration and social interaction between students. However, neurodivergent students' preferences and experiences with these techniques are not well understood. We conducted a survey of neurodivergent computing students (n=24), specifically autistic students or students with ADHD, and neurotypical computing students (n=20) to understand how the structure of collaborative active learning affects their comfort in computing courses. We also interviewed four computing students on the autism spectrum or with ADHD to gain more contextualized insights into their experiences and accessibility recommendations. Our survey surfaces how team dynamics and assignment structure can impact neurodivergent students' comfort in computing courses. Neurodivergent students expressed discomfort with assignments that lack structure or have ambiguous expectations. Neurodivergent students prefer smaller teams that work together frequently with explicitly defined roles. Our interviews identified ways that neurodivergent students cope with discomfort in collaborative active learning, including self-selecting roles and self-disclosure. While preliminary, our results highlight how instructors can design collaborative active learning to be more equitable and accessible for neurodivergent students.
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how neurodivergent computing students—particularly those autistic or with ADHD—experience collaborative active learning compared to neurotypical peers, motivated by the lack of understanding around their specific needs in such environments. Through surveys of 24 neurodivergent and 20 neurotypical students, and interviews with four neurodivergent participants, the research identifies that neurodivergent students feel most comfortable when assignments are clearly structured, team sizes are small, and roles are explicitly defined. Findings indicate that ambiguity in expectations and large or unstructured teams cause discomfort, while self-selected roles and self-disclosure serve as coping mechanisms. The results suggest instructors can improve equity and accessibility by tailoring collaborative activities to accommodate neurodivergent preferences.
本研究旨在探究神经多样性计算学生在协作主动学习中的体验，因当前对这类学生在该环境中的具体需求缺乏理解而发起。通过对24名神经多样性学生（自闭症或ADHD）和20名神经典型学生进行问卷调查，并对4名神经多样性学生进行访谈，研究识别了影响其舒适度的结构因素。结果表明，当任务缺乏结构或预期模糊时，神经多样性学生会感到不适，他们更偏好小团队且角色明确的协作方式。访谈还揭示了他们通过自我选择角色和自我披露等策略应对不适，为教师设计更具包容性的协作学习提供了实践建议。
Hierarchical Concept Geometry in Language Models Emerges from Word Co-occurrence
Authors: Andres Nava, Matthieu Wyart
First: 2026-05-22T16:24:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:24:30+00:00
Comments: 34 pages, 12 figures, including appendices
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a distributional theory of how hypernymy -- the ``is-a'' relation between general and specific concepts -- is encoded geometrically in language representations. Starting from the empirically verified assumption that words closer on the WordNet hypernym graph co-occur more often, we characterize theoretically the spectrum of the resulting embedding Gram matrix of word2vec embeddings. Under mild positivity and decay conditions on the co-occurrence kernel, we prove that the leading eigenvectors first separate broad taxonomic branches and then progressively finer sub-branches, producing a \emph{hierarchical splitting geometry} with a coarse-to-fine spectral organization that mirrors the tree. We confirm these predictions in word2vec embeddings across many sampled WordNet subtrees, and show that the same signature extends strikingly well to Gemma 2B unembeddings. Our results indicate that hierarchical concept geometry in LLMs need not reflect a hierarchy-specific functional mechanism, but emerges from the spectral structure of pairwise word statistics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型中的层级概念几何结构源于词共现
我们提出了一种分布理论，解释超阶词（即一般与具体概念之间的“是…的一种”关系）如何在语言表示中几何编码。从经实证验证的假设出发——即在WordNet超阶图中位置相近的词更常共现——我们理论上刻画了由此产生的词2vec嵌入Gram矩阵的谱结构。在共现核满足温和的正性与衰减条件下，我们证明主特征向量首先分离宽泛的分类分支，随后逐步细化子分支，形成一种“层级分裂几何”，其谱组织从粗到细，与树结构相仿。我们在多个采样自WordNet的子树中验证了该预测，并表明相同特征在Gemma 2B的未嵌入中同样显著成立。我们的结果表明，大语言模型中的层级概念几何结构无需依赖特定层级的功能机制，而是源于词对统计量的谱结构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that words sharing hypernymic relationships in WordNet co-occur more frequently, the authors propose a distributional theory explaining how such hierarchical semantic relations emerge geometrically in language models. They analyze the spectrum of the Gram matrix derived from word2vec embeddings under mild assumptions about co-occurrence kernels, proving that leading eigenvectors hierarchically separate taxonomic branches from coarse to fine, forming a spectral geometry mirroring tree structures. Experimental validation across multiple WordNet subtrees and Gemma 2B unembeddings confirms this hierarchical splitting pattern, suggesting that such geometry arises from statistical word co-occurrence rather than any hierarchy-specific mechanism.
基于词在WordNet超纲关系图中越近越常共现的实证观察，作者提出了一种分布理论，解释语言模型中层级概念如何通过几何结构编码。他们分析词向量Gram矩阵的谱结构，证明在温和的正性与衰减条件下，主特征向量会先分离大类分支，再逐步细化子分支，形成与树结构一致的层级分裂几何。实验在多个WordNet子树和Gemma 2B未嵌入中验证，结果表明这种谱结构与层级树高度吻合，说明语言模型中的层级概念几何并非依赖特定功能机制，而是源于词对共现统计的谱结构。
EgoInteract: Synthetic Egocentric Videos Generation for Interaction Understanding and Anticipation
Authors: Rosario Leonardi, Francesco Ragusa, Daniele Materia, Alessandro Passanisi, James Fort, Jakob Engel, Giovanni Maria Farinella
First: 2026-05-18T10:58:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:21:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Collecting large-scale egocentric video datasets with dense spatial and temporal annotations is costly, slow, and often constrained by environmental biases, privacy constraints, and limited coverage of interaction patterns. While synthetic data has shown strong potential in several vision domains, its use for egocentric perception remains relatively underexplored, especially for tasks requiring temporally coherent human-object interactions. In this work, we introduce EgoInteract, a controllable simulator for egocentric video generation designed to model fine-grained egocentric interactions and their temporal dynamics. The simulator enables precise control over camera, human body and hand motion, object manipulation, and scene composition across diverse environments. Building on this framework, we generate a synthetic egocentric video dataset with dense spatial and temporal annotations for temporal action segmentation, next-active object detection, interaction anticipation, and hand-object interaction detection. We evaluate models trained with simulated data on multiple real-world egocentric benchmarks spanning diverse environments, object categories, and interaction patterns. Results show consistent improvements over strong baselines across tasks and datasets, demonstrating the effectiveness and transferability of our simulation-based approach.

标题：EgoInteract：用于交互理解与预测的合成第一人称视频生成
收集具有密集空间和时间标注的大规模第一人称视频数据集成本高昂、耗时且常受限于环境偏见、隐私约束及交互模式覆盖不足。尽管合成数据在多个视觉领域展现出强大潜力，但其在第一人称感知中的应用仍相对较少，尤其在需要时序一致的人机交互任务中。本文提出EgoInteract，一个用于第一人称视频生成的可控模拟器，旨在建模精细的第一人称交互及其时序动态。该模拟器支持对相机、人体与手部运动、物体操作及场景构成在多样化环境中的精确控制。基于此框架，我们生成了一个具有密集时空标注的合成第一人称视频数据集，适用于时序动作分割、下一活跃物体检测、交互预测及手-物交互检测。我们在多个真实世界的第一人称基准数据集上评估了使用模拟数据训练的模型，涵盖多样化的环境、物体类别和交互模式。结果表明，我们的模拟方法在各项任务和数据集上均显著优于强基线模型，验证了其有效性与迁移能力。
