Not Too Generative, Not Too Discriminative: The Human Alignment Sweet Spot
Authors: Jorge Chang Ortega, Bastien Le Lan, Thomas Serre, Victor Boutin
First: 2026-05-22T16:21:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:21:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A central question in computational vision is whether human-like visual representations are better explained by discriminative or generative learning. Existing comparisons, however, often confound the learning objective with architecture, scale, and training data, leaving open whether the objective itself drives alignment. We address this confound using Joint Energy-Based Models (JEMs), which interpolate continuously between discriminative and generative training within a fixed architecture. By varying a single mixing coefficient, we isolate the effect of the learning objective and evaluate the resulting models across six human-alignment benchmarks spanning perceptual similarity, gloss perception, human response uncertainty, robustness, shape-texture cue conflict, and diagnostic feature attribution. Across this diverse suite, human alignment is consistently maximized at intermediate points of the generative-discriminative continuum, rather than at either endpoint. Hybrid JEMs combine the categorical structure induced by discriminative learning with the sensitivity to input structure induced by generative learning, yielding more human-like behavior across multiple levels of vision. These results suggest that the generative-discriminative dichotomy is the wrong axis for understanding human-aligned vision: alignment emerges not from choosing one objective over the other, but from balancing both.
中文标题/摘要
标题：不过度生成，也不过度判别：人类对齐的黄金平衡点
计算视觉中的一个核心问题是：人类般的视觉表征更应由判别式还是生成式学习来解释。然而，现有比较常将学习目标与架构、规模和训练数据混为一谈，未能明确学习目标本身是否驱动对齐。我们使用联合能量模型（JEMs）解决这一混淆问题，该模型在固定架构内连续插值于判别式与生成式训练之间。通过调节单一混合系数，我们隔离了学习目标的影响，并在六个涵盖感知相似性、光泽感知、人类响应不确定性、鲁棒性、形状-纹理线索冲突及诊断性特征归因的人类对齐基准上评估模型。在这一多样化的评估体系中，人类对齐始终在生成式与判别式之间的中间点达到最大，而非在任一端点。混合JEMs结合了判别式学习诱导的类别结构与生成式学习对输入结构的敏感性，从而在视觉多个层次上展现出更接近人类的行为。这些结果表明，生成式-判别式二分法并非理解人类对齐视觉的正确维度：对齐并非源于选择某一目标，而是源于平衡两者。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether human-like visual representations are better captured by discriminative or generative learning objectives, addressing a key question in computational vision. To isolate the effect of the learning objective from architectural and data confounds, the authors use Joint Energy-Based Models (JEMs), which continuously interpolate between discriminative and generative training within a fixed architecture via a single mixing coefficient. Evaluated across six human-alignment benchmarks—including perceptual similarity, gloss perception, and diagnostic feature attribution—the results show that human alignment peaks at intermediate points along the generative-discriminative spectrum, not at either extreme. Hybrid JEMs, combining discriminative structure with generative sensitivity, produce more human-like behavior across multiple visual tasks, suggesting that alignment arises from balancing both objectives rather than favoring one over the other.
本研究探讨人类视觉表征更应由判别式还是生成式学习目标驱动，这是计算视觉的核心问题。为消除架构、数据等混杂因素，作者采用联合能量模型（JEMs），在固定架构内通过单一混合系数连续插值判别式与生成式训练目标。在六个涵盖感知相似性、光泽感知、人类反应不确定性等的人类对齐基准测试中，结果表明人类对齐性能在生成-判别连续谱的中间区域达到最优，而非两端。混合JEMs结合判别式结构与生成式敏感性，在多个视觉层级上表现出更接近人类的行为，说明对齐并非来自选择单一目标，而是源于两者的平衡。
Safe Reinforcement Learning with Preference-based Constraint Inference
Authors: Chenglin Li, Grant Ruan, Hua Geng
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-03-24T08:54:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:20:02+00:00
Comments: Accepted by the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safe reinforcement learning (RL) is a standard paradigm for safety-critical decision making. However, real-world safety constraints can be complex, subjective, and even hard to explicitly specify. Existing works on constraint inference rely on restrictive assumptions or extensive expert demonstrations, which are not realistic in many real-world applications. How to cheaply and reliably learn these constraints is the major challenge we focus on in this study. While inferring constraints from human preferences offers a data-efficient alternative, we identify popular Bradley-Terry (BT) models fail to capture the asymmetric, heavy-tailed nature of safety costs, resulting in risk underestimation. It is still rare in the literature to understand the impacts of BT models on the downstream policy learning. To address the above knowledge gaps, we propose a novel approach namely Preference-based Constrained Reinforcement Learning (PbCRL). We introduce a novel dead zone mechanism into preference modeling and theoretically prove that it encourages heavy-tailed cost distributions, thereby achieving better constraint alignment. Additionally, we incorporate a Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR) loss to encourage exploration by cost variances, which is found to benefit policy learning. Further, two-stage training strategy is deployed to lower online labeling burdens while adaptively enhancing constraint satisfaction. Empirical results demonstrate that PbCRL achieves superior alignment with true safety requirements and outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in terms of safety and reward. Our work explores a promising and effective way for constraint inference in Safe RL, with great potential in various safety-critical applications.
Summary / 总结
Safe reinforcement learning (RL) is a standard paradigm for safety-critical decision making.
为了解决在缺乏专家演示的情况下学习复杂主观安全约束的难题，本文提出基于偏好的约束强化学习（PbCRL），通过人类偏好推断约束。该方法改进了传统Bradley-Terry模型，引入死区机制以鼓励重尾安全成本分布，减少风险低估，并加入信噪比损失以促进基于成本方差的探索。采用两阶段训练策略降低标注负担并提升约束满足度。实验表明，PbCRL在安全性和奖励上均优于现有基线方法，更贴近真实安全需求。
Beyond the Single Turn: Reframing Refusals as Dynamic Experiences Embedded in the Context of Mental Health Support Interactions with LLMs
Authors: Ningjing Tang, Alice Qian, Qiaosi Wang, Esther Howe, Blake Bullwinkel, Paola Pedrelli, Jina Suh, Hoda Heidari, Hong Shen
First: 2026-02-02T06:08:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:18:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Content Warning: This paper contains participant quotes and discussions related to mental health challenges, emotional distress, and suicidal ideation. Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for mental health support, yet the model safeguards -- particularly refusals to engage with sensitive content -- remain poorly understood from the perspectives of users and mental health professionals (MHPs) and have been reported to cause real-world harms. This paper presents findings from a sequential mixed-methods study examining how LLM refusals are experienced and interpreted in mental health support interactions. Through surveys (N=53) and in-depth interviews (N=16) with individuals using LLMs for mental health support and MHPs, we reveal that refusals are not isolated, single-turn system behaviors but rather constitute dynamic, multi-phase experiences: pre-refusal expectation formation, refusal triggering and encounter, refusal message framing, resource referral provision, and post-refusal outcomes. We contribute a multi-phase framework for evaluating refusals beyond binary policy compliance accuracy and design recommendations for future refusal mechanisms. These findings suggest that understanding LLM refusals requires moving beyond single-turn interactions toward recognizing them as holistic experiences embedded within users' support-seeking trajectories and the broader LLM design pipeline.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the real-world harms caused by poorly understood LLM refusals in mental health support, this study examines how users and mental health professionals experience these refusals as dynamic, multi-phase interactions rather than isolated system responses. Through surveys and interviews with 53 users and 16 professionals, the research identifies five sequential phases: expectation formation, refusal triggering, message framing, resource referral, and post-refusal outcomes. Findings reveal that refusals are contextually embedded and influence users’ emotional states and trust, necessitating a framework that moves beyond binary compliance metrics to evaluate refusal mechanisms holistically within the user’s support journey and LLM design pipeline.
鉴于LLM在心理健康支持中因拒绝机制不明确而引发实际伤害，本研究探讨用户与心理健康专业人士如何将拒绝视为动态多阶段体验，而非孤立系统行为。通过对53名用户和16名专业人士的问卷与深度访谈，研究识别出五个连续阶段：拒绝前预期形成、触发与遭遇、拒绝信息呈现、资源转介及拒绝后结果。研究发现拒绝嵌入用户求助轨迹与LLM设计流程中，影响其情绪与信任，需超越二元合规评估，构建更全面的拒绝机制评价框架。
Advanced AI Service Provisioning in O-RAN through LLM Engine Integration
Authors: Seyed Bagher Hashemi Natanzi, Pranshav Gajja, Bo Tang, Vijay K. Shah
First: 2026-05-22T16:12:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:12:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) architecture allows AI to be embedded directly into the RAN through modular xApps and rApps, yet creating these applications collecting data, training models, writing code, and deploying them safely remains slow and largely manual. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer strong reasoning and code-generation capabilities but are unsuited for the fast, deterministic inference required in real-time RAN control. We present a proof-of-concept Dual-Brain architecture that combines both strengths: an LLM-based orchestrator translates operator intents into data-collection policies and deployment code, while an automated ML engine, NeuralSmith, trains lightweight classifiers on demand via an API. We describe the architecture and provisioning workflow, share practical insights from a containerized O-RAN 5G~SA testbed, and discuss open research directions.

Nonlinear Transformations Against Unlearnable Datasets
Authors: Thushari Hapuarachchi, Jing Lin, Kaiqi Xiong, Mohamed Rahouti, Gitte Ost
First: 2024-06-05T03:00:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:09:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automated scraping stands out as a common method for collecting data in deep learning models without the authorization of data owners. Recent studies have begun to tackle the privacy concerns associated with this data collection method. Notable approaches include Deepconfuse, error-minimizing, error-maximizing (also known as adversarial poisoning), Neural Tangent Generalization Attack, synthetic, autoregressive, One-Pixel Shortcut, Self-Ensemble Protection, Entangled Features, Robust Error-Minimizing, Hypocritical, and TensorClog. The data generated by those approaches, called "unlearnable" examples, are prevented "learning" by deep learning models. In this research, we investigate and devise an effective nonlinear transformation framework and conduct extensive experiments to demonstrate that a deep neural network can effectively learn from the data/examples traditionally considered unlearnable produced by the above twelve approaches. The resulting approach improves the ability to break unlearnable data compared to the linear separable technique recently proposed by researchers. Specifically, our extensive experiments show that the improvement ranges from 0.34% to 249.59% for the unlearnable CIFAR10 datasets generated by those twelve data protection approaches, except for One-Pixel Shortcut. Moreover, the proposed framework achieves over 100% improvement of test accuracy for Autoregressive and REM approaches compared to the linear separable technique. Our findings suggest that these approaches are inadequate in preventing unauthorized uses of data in machine learning models. There is an urgent need to develop more robust protection mechanisms that effectively thwart an attacker from accessing data without proper authorization from the owners.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对抗不可学习数据集的非线性变换
自动化抓取是深度学习模型在未获得数据所有者授权情况下收集数据的常见方法。近期研究开始着手解决该数据收集方式带来的隐私问题。代表性方法包括Deepconfuse、误差最小化/最大化（又称对抗污染）、神经切线泛化攻击、合成、自回归、单像素捷径、自集成保护、纠缠特征、鲁棒误差最小化、伪善攻击及TensorClog。这些方法生成的数据被称为“不可学习”样本，可防止深度学习模型“学习”这些样本。本研究提出并验证了一种有效的非线性变换框架，通过大量实验表明，深度神经网络可有效学习上述十二种方法生成的“不可学习”数据。相较于近期研究人员提出的线性可分技术，本方法在突破不可学习数据方面表现更优。具体而言，针对十二种数据保护方法生成的CIFAR10不可学习数据集，本方法在除单像素捷径外的其他方法中，性能提升范围为0.34%至249.59%；对于自回归和REM方法，测试准确率相比线性可分技术提升超过100%。研究结果表明，上述方法在防止机器学习模型中未经授权使用数据方面存在不足，亟需开发更有效的保护机制，以有效阻止攻击者在未获数据所有者授权情况下访问数据。

Motivated by the need to evaluate the effectiveness of existing data protection methods that generate 'unlearnable' examples to prevent unauthorized model learning, this study proposes a nonlinear transformation framework to demonstrate that deep neural networks can still learn from such data. The method applies nonlinear transformations to unlearnable examples produced by twelve prior techniques, including Deepconfuse and REM, and evaluates performance across CIFAR10 datasets. Experimental results show that the framework achieves significant improvements—ranging from 0.34% to 249.59%—in model accuracy compared to linear separable techniques, with over 100% gains for Autoregressive and REM approaches. These findings indicate that current unlearnable data generation methods are insufficient for protecting data privacy in machine learning.
为应对现有数据保护方法无法阻止深度学习模型学习“不可学习”数据的问题，本研究提出一种非线性变换框架，旨在使神经网络能够从传统上被认为不可学习的数据中有效学习。该方法对十二种已知的不可学习数据生成技术（如Deepconfuse和REM）所生成的数据应用非线性映射，并在多个基准上进行实验评估。实验结果表明，该框架显著优于线性可分方法，在不可学习的CIFAR10数据集上提升幅度介于0.34%至249.59%之间，其中Autoregressive和REM方法的测试准确率提升超过100%。这些结果表明当前不可学习防御机制存在明显不足，亟需开发更有效的数据保护机制以防止未经授权的数据访问。
STM3: Mixture of Multiscale Mamba for Long-Term Spatio-Temporal Time-Series Prediction
Authors: Haolong Chen, Liang Zhang, Zhengyuan Xin, Guangxu Zhu
Venue: KDD 2026
First: 2025-08-17T05:29:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:07:20+00:00
Comments: Accepted by KDD 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recently, spatio-temporal time-series prediction has developed rapidly, yet existing deep learning methods struggle with learning complex long-term spatio-temporal dependencies efficiently. The long-term spatio-temporal dependency learning brings two new challenges: 1) The long-term temporal sequence naturally includes multiscale information, which is hard to extract efficiently; 2) The multiscale temporal information from different nodes is highly correlated and hard to model. To address these challenges, we propose Spatio-Temporal Mixture of Multiscale Mamba (STM3). STM3 integrates a Multiscale Mamba architecture within a novel Disentangled Mixture-of-Experts (DMoE) framework to capture diverse multiscale information efficiently, while utilizing an adaptive graph causal network to model complex spatial dependencies. To ensure robust representation learning, we introduce a stable routing strategy and a causal contrastive learning strategy, which work in tandem with hierarchical information aggregation to guarantee scale distinguishability. We theoretically prove that STM3 achieves superior routing smoothness and guarantees pattern disentanglement for each expert. Extensive experiments on 10 real-world benchmarks across domains demonstrate STM3's superior performance, achieving state-of-the-art results in long-term spatio-temporal time-series prediction. Notably, on the PEMSD8 dataset, it achieves significant improvements, surpassing the second-best model by 7.1% in MAE, 8.5% in RMSE, and 15.9% in MAPE. Code is available at https://github.com/IfReasonable/STM3_KDD26.
Summary / 总结
Recently, spatio-temporal time-series prediction has developed rapidly, yet existing deep learning methods struggle with learning complex long-term spatio-temporal dependencies efficiently.
为解决长期时空序列预测中复杂依赖建模的难题，STM3提出一种解耦的专家混合框架，结合多尺度Mamba架构高效捕捉多尺度时序特征，并引入自适应图因果网络建模空间关联。通过稳定路由策略与因果对比学习确保尺度可区分性与表征鲁棒性。在10个真实基准数据集上的实验表明，STM3达到当前最优性能，尤其在PEMSD8数据集上，相较次优模型MAE提升7.1%，RMSE提升8.5%，MAPE提升15.9%。
GAF: Gaussian Action Field as a 4D Representation for Dynamic World Modeling in Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Ying Chai, Litao Deng, Ruizhi Shao, Jiajun Zhang, Kangchen Lv, Liangjun Xing, Xiang Li, Hongwen Zhang, Yebin Liu
First: 2025-06-17T02:55:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:05:38+00:00
Comments: https://ChaiYing1.github.io/projects/GAF/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Accurate scene perception is critical for vision-based robotic manipulation. Existing approaches typically follow either a Vision-to-Action (V-A) paradigm, predicting actions directly from visual inputs, or a Vision-to-3D-to-Action (V-3D-A) paradigm, leveraging intermediate 3D representations. However, these methods often struggle with action inaccuracies due to the complexity and dynamic nature of manipulation scenes. In this paper, we adopt a V-4D-A framework that enables direct action reasoning from motion-aware 4D representations via a Gaussian Action Field (GAF). GAF extends 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) by incorporating learnable motion attributes, allowing 4D modeling of dynamic scenes and manipulation actions. To learn time-varying scene geometry and action-aware robot motion, GAF provides three interrelated outputs: reconstruction of the current scene, prediction of future frames, and estimation of init action via Gaussian motion. Furthermore, we employ an action-vision-aligned denoising framework, conditioned on a unified representation that combines the init action and the Gaussian perception, both generated by the GAF, to further obtain more precise actions. Extensive experiments demonstrate significant improvements, with GAF achieving +11.5385 dB PSNR, +0.3864 SSIM and -0.5574 LPIPS improvements in reconstruction quality, while boosting the average +7.3% success rate in robotic manipulation tasks over state-of-the-art methods.
Summary / 总结
To address the limitations of existing vision-based robotic manipulation methods that struggle with dynamic scene modeling and action accuracy, this paper introduces GAF, a Gaussian Action Field that extends 3D Gaussian Splatting to a 4D representation by incorporating learnable motion attributes. GAF jointly reconstructs the current scene, predicts future frames, and estimates initial robot actions via Gaussian motion, enabling direct action reasoning from motion-aware 4D data. It further enhances action precision through an action-vision-aligned denoising framework conditioned on unified GAF outputs. Experimental results show GAF significantly outperforms prior methods, achieving +11.5385 dB PSNR, +0.3864 SSIM, and -0.5574 LPIPS improvements in reconstruction, along with a +7.3% average success rate boost in manipulation tasks.
为解决现有视觉驱动机器人操作方法在动态场景建模中的不足，本文提出GAF（高斯动作场），通过引入具备可学习运动属性的4D高斯表示，直接从运动感知的时序场景中推理动作。GAF在3D高斯点云基础上扩展，输出三类信息：当前场景重建、未来帧预测及基于高斯运动的动作估计。结合视觉与动作对齐的去噪框架，利用GAF生成的统一表示进一步优化动作精度。实验表明，GAF在重建质量上提升+11.5385 dB PSNR、+0.3864 SSIM、-0.5574 LPIPS，并将机器人操作成功率提升+7.3%优于当前最优方法。
Perceptually Lossless Tactile Texture Synthesis with Compact Spectral Envelope Models
Authors: Jagan K. Balasubramanian, Yasemin Vardar
First: 2026-05-22T16:05:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:05:33+00:00
Comments: 16 pages and 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern audio-visual media rely on compact representations for efficient storage and transmission, whereas realistic digital touch still depends on high-resolution tactile recordings. Existing approaches for representing tactile signals constrain manipulation and limit the generation of new content. Here, we introduce two compact representations, spectral beta and spectral slope, that capture the temporal spectral structure of finger-surface friction signals while preserving perceptually relevant information. Spectral beta models spectral skewness using a two-parameter beta distribution, whereas spectral slope approximates the spectrum with an asymmetric bandpass filter defined by low- and high-pass orders. We evaluated these representations in a perceptual study with 14 participants using five virtual textures rendered on a friction-modulation display and compared them with physical textures and high-fidelity reproductions of recorded signals. Spectral beta achieved perceptual similarity ratings comparable to those of the original high-fidelity reproductions. Regression analysis further showed that matching spectral energy across nine critical frequency bands was the strongest predictor of perceived realism. Together, these findings suggest that tactile texture perception depends primarily on fundamental temporal spectral patterns and that modeling these patterns is sufficient for perceptually realistic rendering. These results establish an efficient and scalable framework for haptic compression, communication, and synthetic texture generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于紧凑频谱包络模型的感知无损触觉纹理合成
现代音视频媒体依赖紧凑表示以实现高效存储与传输，而逼真的数字触觉仍依赖高分辨率触觉记录。现有触觉信号表示方法限制了操作并限制了新内容的生成。本文引入两种紧凑表示——频谱贝塔和频谱斜率，它们捕捉指尖表面摩擦信号的时频结构，同时保留感知相关的信息。频谱贝塔使用双参数贝塔分布建模频谱偏度，而频谱斜率则用由低通和高通阶数定义的非对称带通滤波器近似频谱。我们在14名参与者参与的感知研究中评估了这些表示方法，使用摩擦调制显示器渲染五种虚拟纹理，并与物理纹理及高保真记录信号复现进行比较。频谱贝塔获得的感知相似度评分与原始高保真复现相当。回归分析进一步表明，匹配九个关键频段的频谱能量是感知真实感最强的预测因子。这些发现共同表明，触觉纹理感知主要依赖基本的时频模式，且建模这些模式足以实现感知逼真的渲染。这些结果确立了一种高效可扩展的触觉压缩、通信及合成纹理生成框架。

Speak-to-Structure: Evaluating LLMs in Open-domain Natural Language-Driven Molecule Generation
Authors: Jiatong Li, Junxian Li, Weida Wang, Yunqing Liu, Changmeng Zheng, Yatao Bian, Dongzhan Zhou, Xiao-yong Wei, Qing Li
Venue: KDD 2026
First: 2024-12-19T08:51:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T16:03:13+00:00
Comments: Accepted by KDD 2026. Our codes and datasets are fully accessible through the https://github.com/phenixace/S2-TOMG-Bench and https://huggingface.co/datasets/phenixace/S2-TOMG-Bench
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated great potential in natural language-driven molecule discovery. However, existing datasets and benchmarks for molecule-text alignment are predominantly built on one-to-one mappings, measuring LLMs' ability to retrieve a single, pre-defined answer, rather than their creative potential to generate diverse, yet equally valid, molecular candidates. To address this critical gap, we propose Speak-to-Structure (S^2-Bench), the first benchmark to evaluate LLMs in open-domain natural language-driven molecule generation. S^2-Bench is specifically designed for one-to-many relationships, challenging LLMs to exhibit genuine molecular understanding and open-ended generation capabilities. Our benchmark includes three key tasks: molecule editing (MolEdit), molecule optimization (MolOpt), and customized molecule generation (MolCustom), each probing a different aspect of molecule discovery. We also introduce OpenMolIns, a large-scale instruction tuning dataset that enables Llama3.1-8B to surpass the most powerful LLMs like GPT-4o and Claude-3.5 on S^2-Bench. Our comprehensive evaluation of 31 LLMs shifts the focus from simple pattern recall to realistic molecular design, paving the way for more capable LLMs in natural language-driven molecule discovery. Our codes and datasets are fully accessible through the Github Repository: https://github.com/phenixace/S2-TOMG-Bench and Huggingface Datasets: https://huggingface.co/datasets/phenixace/S2-TOMG-Bench.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate LLMs beyond simple retrieval in open-domain molecule generation, the authors introduce Speak-to-Structure (S^2-Bench), the first benchmark designed for one-to-many molecule-text alignment tasks. It evaluates LLMs through three core tasks—molecule editing, optimization, and customized generation—to assess creative and structural understanding. Using a new instruction-tuning dataset called OpenMolIns, they train Llama3.1-8B to outperform top models like GPT-4o and Claude-3.5 on S^2-Bench. Across 31 LLMs, the evaluation reveals that most models struggle with open-ended molecular design, highlighting a critical gap in current benchmarks and pointing toward the need for more capable, generative language models in chemistry.
为评估大语言模型在开放域自然语言驱动的分子生成中的创造性能力，作者提出了Speak-to-Structure（S^2-Bench），这是首个针对一对多分子-文本对齐任务的基准。该基准包含三个核心任务——分子编辑、分子优化和定制化分子生成，用于检验模型生成多样且化学上有效的分子结构的能力。通过引入大规模指令微调数据集OpenMolIns，他们训练Llama3.1-8B模型超越GPT-4o和Claude-3.5等顶尖模型。对31个大语言模型的全面评估表明，模型能力从模式记忆转向真实分子设计，凸显开放生成在实际分子发现中的重要性。
SUDP: Secret-Use Delegation Protocol for Agentic Systems
Authors: Xiaohang Yu, Hejia Geng, Xinmeng Zeng, William Knottenbelt
First: 2026-04-27T19:02:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:58:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Agentic systems increasingly act with user secrets for APIs, messaging platforms, and cloud services. Today's agent runtimes typically implement authorization by exposure: enabling action often means placing a reusable secret, or a reusable artifact derived from it, inside the runtime, so a transient prompt-injection or tool-side compromise becomes durable account compromise. Existing defenses cover adjacent pieces such as secret storage, scoped delegation, sender-constrained tokens, and runtime monitoring, but leave the combined agentic obligation without a common specification: an untrusted autonomous requester should be able to cause a user-authorized secret-backed operation without gaining reusable authority over it. We formalize this as the Agent Secret Use (ASU) problem and identify seven security properties any solution must satisfy, spanning authorization integrity and secret confidentiality. We propose the Secret-Use Delegation Protocol (SUDP), in which a requester proposes a canonical operation, the user authorizes it with a fresh authenticator-backed grant, and a custodian redeems the grant to perform the bounded use; reusable authority never crosses the requester boundary. We specialize SUDP for LLM-driven agents, where it applies whenever a tool call would exercise user-enrolled authority-bearing material. Under standard cryptographic assumptions, SUDP satisfies all seven properties when integrated with a hardware-rooted runtime. A reference implementation is available at https://github.com/xhyumiracle/sudp.
Summary / 总结
Agentic systems increasingly act with user secrets for APIs, messaging platforms, and cloud services.
为防止代理系统在运行中暴露用户密钥导致持久账户泄露，本文提出SUDP协议，允许不可信代理在不获取可复用权限的前提下执行授权的密钥相关操作。该协议要求请求方提出操作，用户以新鲜认证器背书的授权凭证授权，托管方赎回凭证执行有限次操作，确保可复用权限不越界。实验表明，在硬件根运行时环境下，SUDP满足七项安全属性，有效防止瞬时攻击导致的持久性泄露。
Debiased Negative Mining Improves Out-of-distribution Detection with Pre-trained Vision-Language Models
Authors: Bo Peng, Jie Lu, Guangquan Zhang, Zhen Fang
Venue: KDD 2026
First: 2026-05-22T15:57:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:57:56+00:00
Comments: KDD 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Aiming at identifying unexpected inputs from unknown classes, out-of-distribution (OOD) detection has emerged as a pivotal approach to enhancing the reliability of machine learning models. This paper focuses on the burgeoning paradigm of post-hoc OOD detection with pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs), where a popular pipeline is to detect OOD inputs by examining their affinities between ID labels and negative labels, i.e., those semantically different from ID labels. Due to the unavailability of target OOD labels, existing works predominantly rely on heuristic rules to mine negative labels from unlabeled wild corpus data. Despite the empirical success, we argue that the power of VLM-based OOD detection has yet to be fully unleashed since the notorious false negative problem is far from addressed in the literature. With this motivation, we are interested in addressing the challenge of mining true negative labels for OOD scoring. To this end, we develop a theoretical framework for correcting the sampling bias of negatives labels by indirectly approximating the distribution of negative labels. Perhaps surprisingly, we show that the debiased negative mining can be naturally converted into Monte-Carlo sampling based on ID labels and the unlabeled wild corpus data. Extensive experiments empirically manifest that our method establishes a new state-of-the-art in a variety of OOD detection setups. Code is publicly available at \href{https://github.com/60pen9/Debiased-Negative-Mining-Improves-OOD-Detection-with-Pre-trained-VLMs}{\textcolor{red}{here}}.
Summary / 总结
To enhance out-of-distribution detection using pre-trained vision-language models, this work addresses the critical issue of false negatives caused by biased negative label mining. The authors propose a debiased negative mining method that corrects sampling bias by approximating the true negative label distribution through Monte-Carlo sampling using ID labels and unlabeled wild corpus data. Experiments across multiple OOD detection setups demonstrate that this approach significantly improves detection performance, establishing a new state-of-the-art without requiring target OOD labels.
为解决预训练视觉-语言模型在分布外检测中普遍存在的假阴性问题，本文提出一种去偏负样本挖掘方法。该方法通过基于ID标签与无标签野生语料的蒙特卡洛采样，间接逼近真实负样本分布，从而校正负样本选择中的偏差。实验表明，该方法在多种分布外检测场景中显著优于现有启发式方法，达到当前最优性能。
DCC: Data-Centric Compilation of Machine Learning Kernels for Processing-In-Memory Architectures
Authors: Peiming Yang, Sankeerth Durvasula, Ivan Fernandez, Mohammad Sadrosadati, Onur Mutlu, Gennady Pekhimenko, Christina Giannoula
First: 2025-11-19T14:58:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:57:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
High-performance Host processors can integrate Processing-In-Memory (PIM) devices, which can accelerate memory-intensive kernels of Machine Learning (ML) models, including Large Language Models (LLMs), by leveraging the large memory bandwidth available at PIM cores. However, Host processor needs consecutive elements distributed across DRAM banks, while PIM cores need consecutive elements within their local banks. This necessitates data rearrangements in ML kernel execution that pose significant performance and programmability challenges, further exacerbated by the need to support diverse PIM devices. Current compilation approaches lack systematic optimization for diverse ML kernels and multiple PIM devices, and may largely ignore data rearrangement costs during the compute code optimization step. We show that data rearrangements and compute code optimization are interdependent, and need to be jointly optimized during the tuning process. Therefore, we design DCC, the first data-centric ML compiler for PIM systems that jointly co-optimizes data rearrangements and compute code in a unified tuning process. DCC integrates a multi-layer PIM abstraction to support multiple PIM backends. DCC enables effective co-optimization of data partitioning strategies with compute loop partitioning schemes. DCC applies PIM-specific code optimizations, and leverages a fast and accurate performance prediction model to select the bestperforming code schedule for a given kernel on a target PIM architecture. Our evaluations in various individual ML kernels show that DCC achieves up to 7.68x speedup (2.21x average) on HBM-PIM, and up to 13.17x speedup (3.92x average) on AttAcc PIM, over GPU-only execution. In end-to-end LLM inference, DCC on AttAcc accelerates GPT-3 and LLaMA-2 by 4.52x average (up to 7.71x in LLaMA-2) over GPU. DCC is open-sourced at https://github.com/SPIN-Research-Group/DCC.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DCC：面向存内计算架构的机器学习内核数据为中心的编译
高性能主机处理器可集成存内计算（PIM）设备，利用PIM核心提供的大内存带宽加速机器学习（ML）模型（包括大语言模型LLM）的内存密集型内核。然而，主机处理器需要跨DRAM银行分布的连续元素，而PIM核心则需要其本地银行内的连续元素，这要求在ML内核执行过程中进行数据重排，带来显著的性能和可编程性挑战，且因需支持多种PIM设备而进一步加剧。当前编译方法缺乏对多样ML内核和多种PIM设备的系统性优化，且在计算代码优化阶段常忽略数据重排成本。我们表明数据重排与计算代码优化相互依赖，需在调优过程中联合优化。因此，我们设计了DCC——首个面向PIM系统的数据为中心的ML编译器，通过统一调优过程联合优化数据重排与计算代码。DCC集成多层PIM抽象以支持多种PIM后端。DCC实现数据分区策略与计算循环分区方案的有效联合优化。DCC应用PIM特定代码优化，并利用快速准确的性能预测模型，为给定内核在目标PIM架构上选择最佳代码调度。在各类ML内核评估中，DCC在HBM-PIM上实现最高7.68倍加速（平均2.21倍），在AttAcc PIM上实现最高13.17倍加速（平均3.92倍），相较仅GPU执行。在端到端LLM推理中，DCC在AttAcc上将GPT-3和LLaMA-2平均加速4.52倍（LLaMA-2最高达7.71倍），相较GPU。DCC开源于https://github.com/SPIN-Research-Group/DCC。
Summary / 总结
High-performance Host processors can integrate Processing-In-Memory (PIM) devices, which can accelerate memory-intensive kernels of Machine Learning (ML) models, including Large Language Models (LLMs), by leveraging the large memory bandwidth available at PIM cores.
为解决主机处理器与PIM架构间因数据访问模式不匹配导致的性能差距，DCC提出一种以数据为中心的编译器，联合优化ML内核在PIM系统上的数据重排与计算代码。它采用多层PIM抽象支持多种硬件后端，并集成数据分区与计算循环划分的协同优化。通过PIM特定代码优化和快速性能预测模型，DCC选择最优代码调度。实验表明，其在HBM-PIM上最高加速7.68倍，在AttAcc PIM上最高加速13.17倍，相比GPU仅执行；在AttAcc上的端到端LLM推理中，平均加速4.52倍（LLaMA-2最高达7.71倍）
Certified Per-Instance Unlearning Using Individual Sensitivity Bounds
Authors: Hanna Benarroch, Jamal Atif, Olivier Cappé
First: 2026-02-17T14:18:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:56:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Certified machine unlearning can be achieved via noise injection leading to differential privacy guarantees, where noise is calibrated to worst-case sensitivity. Such conservative calibration often results in performance degradation, limiting practical applicability. In this work, we investigate an alternative approach based on adaptive per-instance noise calibration tailored to the individual contribution of each data point to the learned solution. This raises the following challenge: how can one establish formal unlearning guarantees when the mechanism depends on the specific point to be removed? To define individual data point sensitivities in noisy gradient dynamics, we consider the use of per-instance differential privacy. For ridge regression trained via Langevin dynamics, we derive high-probability per-instance sensitivity bounds, yielding certified unlearning with substantially less noise injection. We corroborate our theoretical findings through experiments in linear settings and provide further empirical evidence on the relevance of the approach in deep learning settings.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the performance degradation caused by conservative noise calibration in certified machine unlearning, this work proposes an adaptive per-instance noise injection method that calibrates noise based on each data point’s individual contribution to the model. To formalize unlearning guarantees under this adaptive mechanism, the authors introduce per-instance differential privacy and derive high-probability sensitivity bounds for ridge regression trained via Langevin dynamics. Experimental results confirm that this approach achieves certified unlearning with significantly less noise compared to uniform calibration, demonstrating practical efficacy in both linear and deep learning settings.
为解决传统基于最坏情况敏感度的噪声注入导致性能下降的问题，本文提出一种按数据点自适应调整噪声的机制，根据每个样本对模型的具体贡献进行噪声校准。为在该自适应机制下建立形式化遗忘保证，作者引入了按实例差分隐私，并推导出岭回归在朗之万动态训练下的高概率实例敏感度界。实验表明，该方法在保持可验证遗忘性的同时显著减少噪声注入，在线性模型和深度学习场景中均验证了其有效性。
Entropy-Gradient Inversion: Moving Toward Internal Mechanism of Large Reasoning Models
Authors: Junyao Yang, Chen Qian, Kun Wang, Linfeng Zhang, Quanshi Zhang, Yong Liu, Dongrui Liu
First: 2026-05-18T02:41:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:55:02+00:00
Comments: The authors are withdrawing this manuscript due to fundamental inaccuracies in the institutional affiliations and administrative attributions provided at the time of submission. As this version cannot be validated under the correct institutional framework, the authors request its formal withdrawal from the repository. No immediate replacement is intended
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The advancement of Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) has catalyzed a paradigm shift from reactive ``fast thinking'' text generation to systematic, step-by-step ``slow thinking'' reasoning, unlocking state-of-the-art performance in complex mathematical and logical tasks. However, the field faces \textit{the fundamental gap between token-level behavioral analysis and internal reasoning mechanisms, and the instability of reinforcement learning (RL) for reasoning optimization relying on costly external verifiers}. We identify and formally define \textbf{Entropy-Gradient Inversion}, a robust negative correlation between token entropy and logit gradients that acts as a definitive geometric fingerprint for LRM reasoning capability. Building on this, we propose \textbf{Correlation-Regularized Group Policy Optimization (CorR-PO)}, which embeds this inversion signature into RL reward regularization. Extensive experiments on various reasoning benchmarks across multiple model scales show CorR-PO consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, confirming that stronger inversion directly correlates with superior reasoning performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：熵-梯度反转：迈向大推理模型的内部机制
大推理模型（LRMs）的发展推动了从反应式“快速思考”文本生成到系统化、逐步式“慢思考”推理的范式转变，使其在复杂数学和逻辑任务中实现最先进性能。然而，该领域面临两大根本性挑战：一是令牌级行为分析与内部推理机制之间的鸿沟；二是依赖昂贵外部验证器的强化学习（RL）在推理优化中的不稳定性。我们识别并正式定义了**熵-梯度反转**——令牌熵与logit梯度之间稳健的负相关关系，作为LRM推理能力的确定性几何指纹。在此基础上，我们提出**相关性正则化组策略优化（CorR-PO）**，将该反转特征嵌入RL奖励正则化中。在多个模型规模和多种推理基准上的广泛实验表明，CorR-PO持续优于当前最先进基线，证实更强的反转直接关联于更优的推理性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between token-level behavior and internal reasoning mechanisms in Large Reasoning Models and the instability of RL optimization using external verifiers, the authors introduce Entropy-Gradient Inversion — a robust negative correlation between token entropy and logit gradients that serves as a geometric fingerprint of reasoning capability. They then propose Correlation-Regularized Group Policy Optimization (CorR-PO), an RL method that regularizes rewards using this inversion signature. Experiments across multiple model scales and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that CorR-PO consistently outperforms existing baselines, confirming that stronger entropy-gradient inversion correlates with improved reasoning performance.
为弥合大型推理模型中令牌级行为与内部推理机制之间的鸿沟，并解决依赖昂贵外部验证器的强化学习方法不稳定的问题，该文提出熵-梯度反转——即令牌熵与对数梯度之间的负相关关系，作为推理能力的几何指纹。在此基础上，提出基于该反转特征的关联正则化群体策略优化（CorR-PO）方法，将该特征嵌入奖励正则化中。在多个模型规模和推理基准上的实验表明，CorR-PO持续优于现有最先进基线，验证了更强的反转与更优推理性能之间的正相关关系。
Exploring deep learning for Event-Based Saliency Prediction with a Transformer-based model
Authors: Romaric Mazna, Jean Martinet, Sai Deepesh Pokala
First: 2026-05-22T15:52:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:52:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Saliency prediction has been extensively studied in RGB images and videos as a computational model of human visual attention. In contrast, predicting saliency from event-based data remains largely unexplored, despite the biological inspiration and favorable sensing properties of event cameras. Two obstacles have held this direction back: the absence of large-scale event saliency datasets, and the lack of a strong baseline. In this paper, we introduce SEST (Swin Event-based Saliency Transformer), a transformer-based model for saliency prediction from event data, bridging the data scarcity barrier through event-native pretraining and synthetic supervision. SEST leverages a self-supervised pretrained event-based Swin Transformer backbone combined with a lightweight CNN decoder to produce dynamic saliency maps. To address the scarcity of annotated event-based saliency data, we introduce two new benchmark datasets, N-DHF1K and N-UCF Sports, generated from large-scale RGB saliency benchmarks. Experimental results show that SEST clearly outperforms existing event-based saliency methods and narrows the performance gap with state-of-the-art RGB models. Zero-shot evaluation on a real event camera dataset further demonstrates that our model trained on synthetic data remains transferable on real event streams. To the best of our knowledge, this work is the first to apply deep learning to event-based saliency prediction, opening a new research direction at the intersection of event-based vision and neuromorphic visual attention.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underexplored potential of event-based cameras for saliency prediction despite their biological inspiration and sensing advantages, this work introduces SEST, a transformer-based model that leverages event-native pretraining and synthetic supervision to overcome data scarcity. SEST combines a self-supervised Swin Transformer backbone with a lightweight CNN decoder to generate dynamic saliency maps. Evaluated on two newly created benchmark datasets, N-DHF1K and N-UCF Sports, SEST significantly outperforms existing event-based methods and approaches the performance of state-of-the-art RGB models. Zero-shot tests on real event camera data confirm its transferability, marking the first deep learning application in this domain and establishing a new research direction.
鉴于事件相机在生物启发性和传感优势方面的潜力，但其用于显著性预测的研究尚属空白，本文提出SEST模型，基于Transformer架构，通过事件原生预训练与合成监督缓解数据稀缺问题。该模型结合自监督Swin Transformer主干与轻量CNN解码器生成动态显著性图。在新构建的N-DHF1K与N-UCF Sports两个基准数据集上，SEST显著优于现有事件显著性方法，并缩小与顶尖RGB模型的性能差距。在真实事件相机数据上的零样本测试验证了其迁移能力，标志着深度学习首次应用于事件显著性预测，开辟了类神经视觉与视觉注意交叉研究的新方向。
Engagement-Optimized Care: When LLMs become Mental Health Infrastructure
Authors: Briana Vecchione, Meryl Ye, Livia Garofalo, Ranjit Singh
First: 2026-05-22T15:50:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:50:26+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 1 figure
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
General-purpose LLMs are increasingly functioning as mental health infrastructure due to gaps in care left by provider shortages, inadequate insurance coverage, social isolation, and stigma around formal help-seeking. This shift poses a distinct problem for AI ethics: systems neither designed nor governed as care technologies are being used as such, while their dominant design incentives optimize for engagement rather than user well-being. We present findings from a qualitative, longitudinal study with 18 US-based participants who use general-purpose LLMs for socioemotional support and participated in one or more of our study phases, including initial interviews, a four-week diary study, focus groups, and exit interviews. Participants turned to LLMs because other forms of support were unavailable, unaffordable, socially costly, or inadequate. As they continued to use these systems, design features such as anthropomorphic cues, default validation, persistent responsiveness, and weak disengagement mechanisms shaped their ongoing reliance. Participants described meaningful support alongside dependency, epistemic distortion through one-sided validation, privacy expectations without corresponding legal protection, and continued use despite awareness of these risks. We argue these dynamics reflect a structurally unfair tradeoff: users accept risks because support is otherwise absent, while available systems are optimized to deepen engagement and lack care-based accountability. The paper makes three contributions: it traces the arc through which LLMs become care infrastructure and identifies distinct ethical tensions at each stage, shifts analysis from turn-based exchanges to longitudinal trajectories of use, and argues that accountability belongs at the design and incentive conditions through which these systems become care infrastructure rather than at the output or crisis-response layer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：以参与度为导向的关怀：当大语言模型成为心理健康基础设施
由于医疗提供者短缺、保险覆盖不足、社会孤立和寻求正式帮助的污名化，通用大语言模型（LLMs）正日益承担起心理健康基础设施的角色。这一转变对人工智能伦理提出了独特挑战：那些既未被设计也未被治理为关怀技术的系统，却被用作关怀工具，而其主流设计激励机制则优先优化参与度而非用户福祉。我们基于一项对18名美国用户进行的定性纵向研究，他们使用通用LLMs获取社会情感支持，并参与了我们的多个研究阶段，包括初始访谈、四周日记研究、焦点小组和退出访谈。参与者转向LLMs，是因为其他支持形式不可用、不可负担、社会成本高或效果不足。随着持续使用，系统设计特征如拟人化提示、默认验证、持续响应性和弱化退出机制，塑造了他们的持续依赖。参与者描述了有意义的支持与依赖并存、单向验证导致的认知扭曲、隐私预期缺乏法律保障，以及明知风险仍持续使用。我们认为，这些动态反映了结构性不公平的权衡：用户因缺乏其他支持而接受风险，而现有系统则被优化以加深参与度，缺乏基于关怀的责任机制。本文贡献三点：追踪LLMs如何演变为关怀基础设施的路径，并识别各阶段独特的伦理张力；将分析从回合式交互转向使用轨迹的纵向视角；主张问责应置于系统演变为关怀基础设施的设计与激励条件中，而非输出或危机响应层面。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing use of general-purpose LLMs as mental health support amid systemic gaps in care, this study investigates how design features inadvertently shape user dependency and risk. Through a longitudinal qualitative study with 18 US participants, researchers tracked how anthropomorphic cues, default validation, persistent responsiveness, and weak disengagement mechanisms fostered reliance despite known harms. Participants reported receiving meaningful emotional support while simultaneously experiencing epistemic distortion, privacy vulnerabilities, and continued use despite awareness of risks. The study concludes that LLMs are becoming care infrastructure through structural incentives that prioritize engagement over well-being, creating an unfair tradeoff where users accept harm due to lack of alternatives, and systems remain unaccountable at design level.
鉴于医疗资源短缺、保险覆盖不足、社会孤立和寻求帮助的污名化导致心理健康支持缺口，本研究探讨通用大语言模型如何因设计偏向参与度而成为事实上的心理健康基础设施。通过对18名美国用户进行纵向定性研究，发现用户因缺乏可负担、无污名化支持而依赖LLM，其设计如拟人化提示、默认验证和持续响应机制强化了依赖并延长使用，同时用户也报告认知扭曲、隐私风险及风险认知。研究指出，这种动态体现结构性不公平：用户因无替代方案而接受风险，而系统却因激励机制偏向参与度而缺乏关怀导向的责任机制。
A solution to generalized learning from small training sets found in infant repeated visual experiences of individual objects
Authors: Frangil Ramirez, Elizabeth Clerkin, David J. Crandall, Linda B. Smith
First: 2025-10-16T18:21:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:49:24+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 7 figures, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
One-year-old infants rapidly form and generalize categories of the everyday objects they encounter. Here we provide evidence on infants daily-life visual experiences for 8 early-learned object categories. Using a corpus of infant head-camera images recorded at mealtimes (87 mealtimes captured by 14 infants), we measure the frequency of the unique instances of each category and the variability of the visual experiences of each instance. The distribution of instances is highly skewed, containing, for each infant and category, many images of the same few objects along with fewer images of other instances. Graph theoretic measures of the similarity structure for individual categories reveal a lumpy mix of high similarity and high variability, organized into multiple but interconnected clusters of high-similarity images. In computational experiments, we show that artificially-created training sets characterized by a lumpy distribution of similarities support generalization to novel instances after very few training experiences. We discuss implications for visual object recognition, and for learning more generally, by both humans and machines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从婴儿重复视觉体验中发现的针对小规模训练集的通用学习解决方案
一岁婴儿能迅速形成并推广他们遇到的日常物品类别。本文通过记录14名婴儿用餐时的头部摄像头图像（共87次用餐），研究了8个早期学习对象类别的视觉体验频率及每个实例的视觉变化。实例分布高度偏斜，每个婴儿和类别中，多数图像集中在少数几个物体上，其余实例图像较少。对各类别相似性结构的图论度量显示，其呈现高相似性与高变异性混合的“块状”结构，形成多个相互关联的高相似性图像簇。计算实验表明，人工构建的相似性分布呈“块状”的训练集，仅需极少量训练即可实现对新实例的泛化。本文讨论了该发现对视觉物体识别及人类与机器通用学习的启示。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how infants generalize object categories from sparse, repeated visual exposure, motivated by the observation that real-world infant learning involves highly skewed data distributions. Using head-camera recordings from 14 infants across 87 mealtimes, researchers quantified the frequency and variability of object instances, revealing that infants encounter a few dominant objects repeatedly while seeing others rarely. Graph-theoretic analysis of visual similarity showed that object instances cluster into interconnected, high-similarity groups despite overall variability. Computational experiments demonstrated that artificial datasets mimicking this lumpy similarity structure enable rapid generalization to novel instances with minimal training, suggesting that such data distributions may be key to efficient learning in both human infants and machine models.
该研究探讨了婴儿如何在视觉经验稀疏但重复的情况下快速泛化物体类别，动机源于婴儿在有限接触下迅速掌握日常物体的观察。研究人员分析了14名婴儿在87次用餐期间的头部摄像头记录，量化了各物体实例出现频率及其视觉变异度，发现分布高度偏斜——少数物体被反复观察，其余实例较少出现。通过图论方法，他们发现每个类别的相似性结构呈现多个相互关联的高相似性簇与高变异度混合。计算实验表明，人工构建的模拟这种“块状分布”的训练集可在极少量样本下实现对新实例的有效泛化，暗示此类结构化的稀疏性可能是人类婴儿及机器学习高效建模的关键机制。
Routing Equilibrium in Mixed-Autonomy Traffic Networks with Altruistic Autonomous Agents
Authors: Lihui Yi, Ermin Wei
First: 2026-05-22T15:48:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:48:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advancements in vehicle autonomy have drawn interest in understanding the impact of autonomous vehicles on traffic systems. In this paper, we study a traffic assignment problem in a mixed-autonomy setting where both human-driven and autonomous vehicles coexist. We model the interaction as a simultaneous routing game where human drivers are self-interested and aim to minimize their own travel times, while autonomous agents are altruistic and aim to minimize the total social cost. The standard nonatomic congestion game analysis establishes the existence of equilibrium to this game under convex cost functions, and does not have any implication of its uniqueness. In this work, we formulate the equilibrium as a variational inequality (VI), which enables us to establish the equilibrium existence without convexity assumption, and guarantees the uniqueness of the aggregated link flow and social cost at equilibrium under a specific class of cost functions. Leveraging this VI framework, we provide sufficient conditions under which including autonomous agents improves, deteriorates, or has no effect on social cost. While the possibility of deterioration has been established in prior work, our results complement existing worst-case bounds by explicitly characterizing sufficient conditions under which each outcome occurs, thereby providing a deeper understanding of mixed-autonomy traffic systems. Furthermore, we consider a centralized scenario where a social planner optimizes the routing of autonomous agents, and show that the same equilibrium is achieved as in the decentralized scenario when assuming convex costs.Finally, we conduct numerical experiments that illustrate how social cost changes with the amount of autonomous vehicles under different system parameters.
Summary / 总结
Recent advancements in vehicle autonomy have drawn interest in understanding the impact of autonomous vehicles on traffic systems.
Cascaded Transfer: Learning Many Tasks under Budget Constraints
Authors: Eloi Campagne, Yvenn Amara-Ouali, Yannig Goude, Mathilde Mougeot, Argyris Kalogeratos
First: 2026-01-29T10:28:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:47:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In distributed applications, such as energy demand forecasting at the substation level or federated learning, a large number of related tasks must be learned by different models, while the exact task relationships are unknown. We propose the novel Cascaded Transfer Learning (CTL) paradigm in which model parameters cascade hierarchically through tasks organized as a rooted tree, respecting a global training budget. Starting from a source task, the tree specifies the order in which tasks are learned and refined, with the budget allocated along its branches. We design cascade mechanisms based on spanning trees that connect all tasks by minimizing an objective combining pairwise task distances and the available training budget, which yield geometry-aware and depth-bounded transfer graphs. We theoretically characterize how transfer errors accumulate and attenuate along cascade paths: errors introduced at any upstream node are contracted by every downstream refinement, and balanced tree topologies bound this accumulation. Experiments on synthetic and real many-task settings, time-series forecasting and image classification, show that CTL enables more accurate and cost-effective adaptation across large task collections than alternative approaches, with the largest gains at the tightest budgets.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to learn many related tasks under strict budget constraints in distributed systems like federated learning or energy forecasting, this paper introduces Cascaded Transfer Learning (CTL), a method that organizes tasks into a rooted tree structure to guide hierarchical model refinement. CTL allocates training budget along tree branches and uses spanning tree optimization to minimize task distance and budget usage, generating geometry-aware transfer graphs. Theoretical analysis shows that errors propagate and attenuate along cascade paths, with balanced trees limiting error accumulation. Experiments on synthetic and real-world tasks in time-series forecasting and image classification demonstrate that CTL achieves higher accuracy and better budget efficiency than baseline methods, especially under tight budget limits.
为在分布式系统中满足严格预算约束下学习大量相关任务，本文提出级联迁移学习（CTL）框架，将任务组织为根树结构以指导模型分层优化。CTL沿树边分配训练预算，并通过最小化任务距离与预算可用性的联合目标构建几何感知、深度受限的迁移图。理论分析表明，迁移误差沿级联路径被下游优化逐步衰减，平衡树结构可限制误差累积。在合成与真实多任务场景（如时间序列预测与图像分类）的实验中，CTL在预算受限时显著优于其他方法，兼具更高精度与更优预算效率。
Beyond Binary Edits Robust Multimodal Knowledge Editing with Adversarial Subspace Alignment
Authors: Haoyuan Wang, Xiaohao Liu, Jiajie Su, Jianmao Xiao, Chaochao Chen
First: 2026-05-22T15:46:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:46:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) need efficient mechanisms to update knowledge without degrading existing capabilities. While intrinsic multimodal knowledge editing achieves strong reliability and locality, it often exhibits limited generality, failing to propagate edits across semantically equivalent visual and linguistic variations. This issue arises from the lack of explicit semantic supervision, rigid editing scopes, and biased anchoring to individual samples in high-dimensional multimodal spaces. We address robust intrinsic multimodal knowledge editing by explicitly targeting generalization. We formalize robustness through knowledge units that group semantically equivalent multimodal inputs and define generality as consistent predictions within each unit. To expose fragile semantic regions, we introduce Latent Adversarial Robustification (LAR), which generates adversarial yet semantically coherent variants in the joint latent space. We further propose Rank-Constrained Subspace Learning (RCSL), enforcing low-rank alignment of adversarial representations at the edit layer via a singular value-based objective. Extensive analysis demonstrates the effectiveness of ASAM empirically.
Summary / 总结
To enhance the generalization of multimodal knowledge editing in large language models, this work addresses the limitation that intrinsic editing methods fail to propagate edits across semantically equivalent visual and linguistic variations due to lack of semantic supervision and rigid anchoring. The authors introduce a method called ASAM, which formalizes robustness by grouping semantically equivalent inputs into knowledge units and enforces consistent predictions within each unit. To expose fragile semantic regions, they employ Latent Adversarial Robustification (LAR) to generate adversarial yet semantically coherent variants in the joint latent space, followed by Rank-Constrained Subspace Learning (RCSL) to align adversarial representations at the edit layer via a low-rank singular value objective. Experimental results show ASAM significantly improves editing robustness and generalization across diverse multimodal variations without degrading base model performance.
为解决多模态大语言模型中内在知识编辑泛化能力不足的问题，该研究提出一种显式增强语义鲁棒性的方法，通过将语义等价的多模态输入聚类为知识单元，并在单元内强制保持预测一致性。方法引入潜在对抗鲁棒化（LAR）在联合潜在空间生成语义连贯的对抗样本以暴露脆弱区域，再结合秩约束子空间学习（RCSL）通过奇异值目标在编辑层强制对抗表示的低秩对齐。实验表明，该方法显著提升了编辑在视觉与语言语义变体间的泛化能力，同时未损害模型原有性能。
Bridging Data and Physics: A Graph Neural Network-Based Hybrid Twin Framework
Authors: M. Gorpinich, B. Moya, S. Rodriguez, F. Meraghni, Y. Jaafra, A. Briot, M. Henner, R. Leon, F. Chinesta
First: 2025-12-12T16:54:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:44:20+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 14 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Simulating complex unsteady physical phenomena relies on detailed mathematical models, simulated for instance by using the Finite Element Method (FEM). However, these models often exhibit discrepancies from the reality due to unmodeled effects or simplifying assumptions. We refer to this gap as the ignorance model. While purely data-driven approaches attempt to learn full system behavior, they require large amounts of high-quality data across the entire spatial and temporal domain. In real-world scenarios, such information is unavailable, making full data-driven modeling unreliable. To overcome this limitation, we model of the ignorance component using a hybrid twin approach, instead of simulating phenomena from scratch. Since physics-based models approximate the overall behavior of the phenomena, the remaining ignorance is typically lower in complexity than the full physical response, therefore, it can be learned with significantly fewer data. A key difficulty, however, is that spatial measurements are sparse, also obtaining data measuring the same phenomenon for different spatial configurations is challenging in practice. Our contribution is to overcome this limitation by using Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) to represent the ignorance model. GNNs learn the spatial pattern of the missing physics even when the number of measurement locations is limited. This allows us to enrich the physics-based model with data-driven corrections without requiring dense spatial, temporal and parametric data. To showcase the performance of the proposed method, we evaluate this GNN-based hybrid twin on nonlinear heat transfer problems across different meshes, geometries, and load positions. Results show that the GNN successfully captures the ignorance and generalizes corrections across spatial configurations, improving simulation accuracy and interpretability, while minimizing data requirements.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between physics-based simulations and real-world behavior due to unmodeled effects, this work proposes a hybrid twin framework that combines physics-based models with a Graph Neural Network (GNN) to learn the residual 'ignorance' component. The method leverages GNNs to capture spatial patterns of missing physics from sparse measurements, enabling accurate corrections without requiring dense data across space, time, or parameters. Experiments on nonlinear heat transfer problems across varied meshes and geometries demonstrate that the GNN effectively learns and generalizes the ignorance, significantly improving simulation accuracy and interpretability while reducing data demands.
为解决物理模型与现实现象之间的差距（即‘忽略模型’），本文提出一种结合物理模拟与图神经网络（GNN）的混合孪生框架。该方法利用GNN从稀疏测量数据中学习缺失物理的空间模式，从而在无需密集时空参数数据的情况下实现精准修正。在不同网格与几何结构的非线性热传导问题实验中，GNN有效捕捉并泛化忽略部分，显著提升仿真精度与可解释性，同时降低数据需求。
The physics of AI weather models
Authors: George Craig, Tobias Selz, Matthias Beylich, Kirsten I. Tempest
First: 2026-05-22T15:43:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:43:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Could it be that AI weather models are solving physical equations, although they may not be the equations used by conventional NWP models? We compute correlations of forecast skill and Centered Kernel Alignment, providing evidence that different AI weather models represent the atmosphere in similar ways, despite differences in architecture and capacity. We argue that the architecture and training of the AI models constrains the form of the physical laws that they might simulate. In particular, we propose that the models implement a particle description of the atmosphere, where the latent variables at each mesh point correspond to the position of a particle in the high dimensional latent space. We hypothesize that the movement of the particles follows a gradient flow in the latent space towards a minimum of a learned free energy functional. Analysis of the GraphCast and Aurora models show that they make changes on large spatial scales in the early processor layers and move to smaller scale with increasing layer depth, consistent with the gradient flow hypothesis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能天气模型的物理基础
人工智能天气模型是否在解决物理方程，即使它们可能并非传统数值天气预报模型所使用的方程？我们计算了预报技能与中心核对齐的相关性，提供了证据表明，尽管架构和容量不同，不同的AI天气模型以相似方式表征大气。我们认为，AI模型的架构和训练限制了它们可能模拟的物理定律的形式。特别是，我们提出模型实现了大气的粒子描述，其中每个网格点的潜在变量对应于高维潜在空间中的粒子位置。我们假设粒子在潜在空间中沿梯度流移动，趋向于学习到的自由能泛函的最小值。对GraphCast和Aurora模型的分析表明，它们在早期处理器层中在大空间尺度上进行变化，随着层深度增加逐渐转向小尺度，这与梯度流假说一致。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the question of whether AI weather models implicitly solve physical equations, this study investigates whether different AI architectures converge on similar atmospheric representations despite structural differences. Using correlation analysis between forecast skill and Centered Kernel Alignment, the authors find that diverse AI models encode the atmosphere in comparable ways, suggesting a shared physical interpretation. They propose that these models operate via a particle-based latent space representation, where each mesh point corresponds to a particle whose motion follows a gradient descent toward a learned free energy minimum. Experimental analysis of GraphCast and Aurora models confirms that early layers capture large-scale dynamics while later layers resolve finer scales, supporting the gradient flow hypothesis.
该研究旨在探究AI天气模型是否在不使用传统数值天气预报模型所依赖的物理方程的情况下，仍能隐式求解物理规律。通过分析预报技能与中心核对齐度的相关性，研究发现不同架构的AI模型在表征大气时具有相似性，表明其架构与训练过程限制了它们所模拟的物理规律形式。研究提出，这些模型采用粒子描述大气，每个网格点的潜在变量对应高维潜在空间中的粒子位置，粒子运动遵循潜在空间中的梯度下降，趋向于学习到的自由能最小值。对GraphCast和Aurora模型的分析表明，大气大尺度变化发生在网络浅层，随着层数加深逐渐过渡到小尺度，支持梯度流假设。
Machine learning applied to emerald gemstone grading: framework proposal and creation of a public dataset
Authors: FB Pena, D Crabi, Sandro C Izidoro, Érick O Rodrigues, G Bernardes
Venue: Pattern Analysis and Applications 2022
First: 2026-05-22T15:43:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:43:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The grading of gemstones is currently a manual procedure performed by gemologists. A popular approach uses reference stones, where those are visually inspected by specialists that decide which one of the available reference stone is the most similar to the inspected stone. This procedure is very subjective as different specialists may end up with different grading choices. This work proposes a complete framework that entails the image acquisition and goes up to the final stone categorization. The proposal is able to automate the entire process apart from including the stone in the created chamber for the image acquisition. It discards the subjective decisions made by specialists. This is the first work to propose a machine learning approach coupled with image processing techniques for emerald grading. The proposed framework achieves 98% of accuracy (correctly categorized stones), outperforming a deep learning approach. Furthermore, we also create and publish the used dataset that contains 192 images of emerald stones along with their extracted and pre-processed features.

A Novel Approach for the Counting of Wood Logs Using cGANs and Image Processing Techniques
Authors: João VC Mazzochin, Giovani Bernardes Vitor, Gustavo Tiecker, Elioenai MF Diniz, Gilson A Oliveira, Marcelo Trentin, Érick O Rodrigues
Venue: Forests 2025
First: 2026-05-22T15:43:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:43:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This study tackles the challenge of precise wood log counting, where applications of the proposed methodology can span from automated approaches for materials management, surveillance, and safety science to wood traffic monitoring, wood volume estimation, and others. We introduce an approach leveraging Conditional Generative Adversarial Networks (cGANs) for eucalyptus log segmentation in images, incorporating specialized image processing techniques to handle noise and intersections, coupled with the Connected Components Algorithm for efficient counting. To support this research, we created and made publicly available a comprehensive database of 466 images containing approximately 13,048 eucalyptus logs, which served for both training and validation purposes. Our method demonstrated robust performance, achieving an average Accuracy_pixel of 96.4% and Accuracy_logs of 92.3%, with additional measures such as F1 scores ranging from 0.879 to 0.933 and IoU values between 0.784 and 0.875, further validating its effectiveness. The implementation proves to be efficient with an average processing time of 0.713s per image on an NVIDIA T4 GPU, making it suitable for realtime applications. The practical implications of this method are significant for operational forestry, enabling more accurate inventory management, reducing human errors in manual counting, and optimizing resource allocation. Furthermore, the segmentation capabilities of the model provide a foundation for advanced applications such as eucalyptus stack volume estimation, contributing to a more comprehensive and refined analysis of forestry operations. The methodology's success in handling complex scenarios, including intersecting logs and varying environmental conditions, positions it as a valuable tool for practical applications across related industrial sectors.

VFM$^{4}$SDG: Unveiling the Power of VFMs for Single-Domain Generalized Object Detection
Authors: Yupeng Zhang, Ruize Han, Ningnan Guo, Wei Feng, Song Wang, Liang Wan
First: 2026-04-23T10:04:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:42:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-world weather, illumination, and imaging variations often induce severe domain shifts, degrading single-source detectors in unseen environments. Existing single-domain generalized object detection (SDGOD) methods mainly rely on data augmentation or domain-invariant learning, while largely overlooking how domain shift disrupts detector prediction stability. Through analytical experiments, we find that performance degradation is mainly dominated by increasing missed detections. Further analysis shows that this phenomenon stems from reduced cross-domain stability in DETR-style detectors: domain shift disrupts encoder-side object-background and inter-instance relations, and further weakens the semantic-spatial binding between decoder queries and real objects. Motivated by this, we find that vision foundation models (VFMs) still preserve stable relational structures and object responses under severe shifts, making them suitable cross-domain stability priors to compensate for detector degradation. To this end, we propose VFM$^{4}$SDG, a dual-prior learning framework for SDGOD, which introduces a frozen VFM into encoder representation learning and decoder query modeling. Specifically, we propose Cross-domain Stable Relational Prior Distillation to distill stable object-background and inter-instance relations from the VFM into the encoder, compensating for relational degradation. Meanwhile, we propose Semantic-Contextual Prior-based Query Enhancement, which injects category semantic prototypes and global object context into queries before they enter the decoder layer, enhancing semantic-spatial query-object binding stability. Extensive experiments show that VFM$^{4}$SDG significantly outperforms existing advanced methods on standard SDGOD benchmarks and two mainstream DETR-based detection frameworks, demonstrating its effectiveness, robustness, and generality.
Summary / 总结
Real-world weather, illumination, and imaging variations often induce severe domain shifts, degrading single-source detectors in unseen environments.
鉴于现实场景中的域偏移主要通过增加漏检来降低单域泛化目标检测性能，本文提出VFM$^{4}$SDG框架，利用冻结的视觉基础模型（VFM）作为跨域稳定先验。该方法通过跨域稳定关系先验蒸馏，将VFM中的物体-背景与实例间关系注入编码器；同时通过语义-上下文先验增强查询，注入类别原型与全局上下文以提升解码器查询与目标的空间语义绑定。实验表明，VFM$^{4}$SDG在主流SDGOD基准及DETR架构上显著优于现有先进方法，验证其有效性、鲁棒性与通用性。
Agentic Proving for Program Verification
Authors: Alessandro Sosso, Akhil Arora, Bas Spitters
First: 2026-05-22T15:41:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:41:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic systems have recently emerged as state-of-the-art approaches for automated theorem proving in formal mathematics. To assess how far these capabilities extend to program verification, we evaluate Claude Code in an agentic proving framework on CLEVER, a Lean 4 benchmark for verifiable code generation. Our results show that Claude generates arguably valid specifications for 98.8% of problems (with 81.3% also accepted by CLEVER's isomorphism-based scoring on the correct portion of the benchmark), certifies implementations against correct ground-truth specifications for 87.5% of problems, and reaches a 98.1% success rate on the end-to-end program generation and verification pipeline over entries with self-consistent premises. Across all stages, Claude further provides high-quality feedback on its own attempts (as confirmed under manual review), identifying underlying causes of failure and lingering bugs in the dataset. These findings highlight a growing mismatch between the difficulty of existing program verification benchmarks and the capabilities of modern agentic provers, and point to the need for more rigorous, bug-resilient evaluation methodologies, and in particular for alternatives to isomorphism-based scoring of generated specifications. More broadly, our results provide empirical evidence that tight compiler-in-the-loop agentic paradigms are currently the most effective approach for foundational program verification.
Summary / 总结
Agentic systems have recently emerged as state-of-the-art approaches for automated theorem proving in formal mathematics.
鉴于近期代理系统在自动定理证明中的进展，本研究评估了Claude Code在Lean 4基准CLEVER上的程序验证能力。方法是将Claude置于代理证明框架中，生成规范、验证实现并完成端到端的程序生成与验证。结果显示，Claude对98.8%的问题生成了可接受的规范，87.5%的问题能验证正确实现，且在自洽前提下端到端流程成功率达98.1%。Claude还能提供高质量的自我反馈，揭示缺陷与失败原因，表明现有基准与代理证明器能力存在差距，亟需更严谨的评估方法，特别是超越基于同构的规范评分机制。
PhotoFlow: Agentic 3D Virtual Photography Missions
Authors: Jiarui Guo, Haojia Wei, Yiming Zhang, Yifei Liu, Yuning Gong, Hongjie Zhang, Xue Yang, Zhihang Zhong
First: 2026-05-22T15:40:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:40:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Virtual photography asks an agent to enter a prepared 3D scene with no preselected camera pose or reference image, infer a suitable shot from scene information and a language intent, choose executable camera parameters, and render the final photograph. Recent progress in vision-language models makes this kind of spatial agent increasingly plausible, but the task stresses two capabilities that remain hard to evaluate together: complex 3D spatial understanding and abstract aesthetic judgment. We introduce PhotoFlow, a Director-Reviewer-Reflector agent for closed-loop camera search. The Director builds a soft photographic blueprint and proposes diverse candidate cameras; the Reviewer combines rule checks, visual critique, and pairwise incumbent selection; and the Reflector converts failures into region memory, dead-zone suppression, and high-explore relocation. We also introduce VPhotoBench, a benchmark of 47 open-license Blender scenes and 141 language-conditioned photography missions spanning subject placement, relational composition, and atmosphere/style. On held-out experiments, PhotoFlow achieves the strongest external quality-alignment composite and success rate among one-shot prediction, single-chain reflection, anchor-bank selection, and random search under a six-round rendering budget. To our knowledge, this is the first work to make language-conditioned virtual photography in arbitrary Blender scenes an executable agent task, and our results show that an LLM-centered spatial agent can already produce strong photographs in a setting designed to challenge both 3D reasoning and aesthetic choice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PhotoFlow：代理驱动的3D虚拟摄影任务
虚拟摄影要求代理进入一个无预设相机姿态或参考图像的3D场景，根据场景信息和语言意图推断合适的拍摄角度，选择可执行的相机参数并渲染最终照片。视觉-语言模型的最新进展使此类空间代理日益可行，但该任务强调两项难以同时评估的能力：复杂的3D空间理解与抽象审美判断。我们提出了PhotoFlow，一种闭环相机搜索的导演-评审-反思代理。导演构建软性摄影蓝图并提出多样候选相机；评审结合规则检查、视觉批评与成对候选选择；反思则将失败转化为区域记忆、死区抑制与高探索重定位。我们还引入了VPhotoBench，一个包含47个开源Blender场景和141个语言条件摄影任务的基准，涵盖主体布局、关系构图与氛围/风格。在未见实验中，PhotoFlow在单次预测、单链反思、锚点库选择与随机搜索等方法中，在六轮渲染预算下实现了最强的外部质量对齐综合得分与成功率。据我们所知，这是首次将语言条件下的虚拟摄影在任意Blender场景中转化为可执行的代理任务，我们的结果表明，以大语言模型为中心的空间代理已能在旨在挑战3D推理与审美选择的环境中生成高质量照片。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of enabling agents to autonomously generate high-quality virtual photographs from language prompts in arbitrary 3D scenes, the authors propose PhotoFlow, a multi-agent system comprising a Director, Reviewer, and Reflector that collaboratively search for optimal camera parameters in a closed-loop manner. The Director generates diverse camera proposals based on scene and language input, the Reviewer evaluates and selects among them using visual and rule-based criteria, and the Reflector learns from failures to improve future decisions. Evaluated on VPhotoBench—a benchmark of 47 Blender scenes and 141 language-conditioned photography tasks—the system outperforms one-shot prediction, single-chain reflection, anchor-bank selection, and random search in terms of both success rate and external quality alignment under a six-round rendering budget, demonstrating that LLM-centered spatial agents can produce compelling virtual photographs even under demanding 3D reasoning and aesthetic judgment constraints.
为评估智能体在三维空间理解与审美判断方面的综合能力，作者提出了PhotoFlow系统，该系统能根据语言指令在任意3D场景中自动生成虚拟照片。系统由Director（生成软性摄影蓝图并提出多组相机候选）、Reviewer（结合规则检查、视觉评价与成对选择）和Reflector（从失败中学习，优化区域记忆与探索策略）三部分组成。在包含47个Blender场景和141个语言引导摄影任务的VPhotoBench基准上，PhotoFlow在六轮渲染预算下，于质量对齐与成功率两项指标上均优于单次预测、单链反思、锚点库选择及随机搜索方法，是首个能稳定执行任意场景语言引导虚拟摄影的智能体方案。
Naturalistic Computational Cognitive Science: Towards generalizable models and theories that capture the full range of natural behavior
Authors: Wilka Carvalho, Andrew Lampinen
First: 2025-02-27T18:20:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:40:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
How can cognitive science build generalizable theories that span the full scope of natural situations and behaviors? We argue that progress in Artificial Intelligence (AI) offers timely opportunities for cognitive science to embrace experiments with increasingly naturalistic stimuli, tasks, and behaviors; and computational models that can accommodate these changes. We first review a growing body of research spanning neuroscience, cognitive science, and AI that suggests that incorporating a broader range of naturalistic experimental paradigms, and models that accommodate them, may be necessary to resolve some aspects of natural intelligence and ensure that our theories generalize. We review cases from cognitive science and neuroscience where naturalistic paradigms elicit distinct behaviors or engage different processes. We then discuss recent progress in AI that shows that learning from naturalistic data yields qualitatively different patterns of behavior and generalization, and examine how these findings impact the conclusions we draw from cognitive modeling, and can help yield new hypotheses for the roots of cognitive and neural phenomena. We then suggest that integrating recent progress in AI and cognitive science will enable us to engage with more naturalistic phenomena without giving up experimental control or the pursuit of theoretically grounded understanding. We offer practical guidance on how methodological practices can contribute to cumulative progress in naturalistic computational cognitive science, and illustrate a path towards building computational models that solve the real problems of natural cognition, together with a reductive understanding of the processes and principles by which they do so.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自然主义计算认知科学：迈向能够捕捉自然行为全范围的可推广模型与理论
认知科学如何构建能够涵盖自然情境与行为全范围的可推广理论？我们认为，人工智能（AI）领域的进展为认知科学提供了及时机遇，使其能够采用日益自然主义的刺激、任务和行为实验，并构建能够容纳这些变化的计算模型。我们首先综述了神经科学、认知科学与AI领域日益增长的研究成果，表明为解决自然智能的某些方面并确保理论的可推广性，有必要纳入更广泛的自然主义实验范式及相应的模型。我们回顾了认知科学与神经科学中自然主义范式如何引发不同行为或激活不同过程的案例。接着，我们讨论了AI领域的最新进展，即从自然主义数据中学习可产生质异的行为与泛化模式，并探讨这些发现如何影响我们从认知建模中得出的结论，以及如何有助于提出关于认知与神经现象根源的新假设。我们进一步建议，整合AI与认知科学的最新进展，将使我们能够在不放弃实验控制或理论基础理解追求的前提下，更深入地研究自然主义现象。我们还提供了方法论实践如何促进自然主义计算认知科学累积性进步的实用指导，并展示了一条构建能够解决真实自然认知问题的计算模型的路径，同时辅以对这些模型运作过程与原理的还原性理解。

The authors argue that combining AI’s learning capabilities with cognitive science’s theoretical grounding enables more realistic modeling without sacrificing experimental control, and propose methodological guidelines to foster cumulative progress in naturalistic computational cognitive science.
为推动认知科学构建能覆盖自然行为全谱的可推广理论，本文主张结合人工智能最新进展，采用更贴近自然的刺激、任务与计算模型。作者综述了神经科学与认知科学中自然主义范式如何引发不同行为并激活不同认知过程，并指出AI模型在自然数据上训练后表现出与传统方法截然不同的泛化模式。这些发现表明，引入自然主义数据可优化认知建模、催生关于认知与神经机制的新假设，并在不牺牲实验控制与理论基础的前提下，实现对自然认知更真实的模拟。
DELICATE: Diachronic Entity LInking using Classes And Temporal Evidence
Authors: Cristian Santini, Sebastian Barzaghi, Paolo Sernani, Emanuele Frontoni, Mehwish Alam
First: 2025-11-13T15:24:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:35:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In spite of the remarkable advancements in the field of Natural Language Processing, the task of Entity Linking (EL) remains challenging in the field of humanities due to complex document typologies, lack of domain-specific datasets and models, and long-tail entities, i.e., entities under-represented in Knowledge Bases (KBs). The goal of this paper is to address these issues with two main contributions. The first contribution is DELICATE, a novel neuro-symbolic method for EL on historical Italian which combines a BERT-based encoder with contextual information from Wikidata to select appropriate KB entities using temporal plausibility and entity type consistency. The second contribution is ENEIDE, a multi-domain EL corpus in historical Italian semi-automatically extracted from two annotated editions spanning from the 19th to the 20th century and including literary and political texts. Results show how DELICATE outperforms other EL models in historical Italian even if compared with larger architectures with billions of parameters. Moreover, further analyses reveal how DELICATE confidence scores and features sensitivity provide results which are more explainable and interpretable than purely neural methods.
