Direct Dynamic Retargeting for Humanoid Imitation Learning from Videos
Authors: Constant Roux, Ludovic De Matteïs, Armand Jordana, Valentin Guillet, Nicolas Mansard, Olivier Stasse, Philippe Souères
First: 2026-05-22T15:33:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:33:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Imitation Learning from monocular video demonstrations provides a scalable approach for teaching complex skills to humanoid robots. However, translating human motion to humanoids requires overcoming significant morphological mismatches. Standard approaches rely on Geometric Retargeting or Indirect Dynamic Retargeting pipelines. We identify that these intermediate kinematic projections introduce a geometric bias, restricting the search space and yielding suboptimal dynamic behaviors. In this paper, we propose Direct Dynamic Retargeting (DDR), a novel single-stage framework that generates high-fidelity, dynamically feasible trajectories directly from expert videos. By formulating the problem in the task space and leveraging a sampling-based Model Predictive Control solver within a physics simulator, DDR natively optimizes over complex contact sequences while mitigating input drift. Our experiments demonstrate that bypassing the geometric bias allows DDR to outperform state-of-the-art baselines in demonstration tracking accuracy. Furthermore, we establish that providing such physically viable references to RL agents accelerates training convergence and enhances the final execution of agile and balancing behaviors. Source code will be made publicly available.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to teach humanoid robots complex skills from human video demonstrations while avoiding geometric biases introduced by standard retargeting methods, this paper proposes Direct Dynamic Retargeting (DDR), a single-stage framework that directly generates dynamically feasible trajectories from expert videos. DDR formulates the problem in task space and uses a sampling-based Model Predictive Control solver within a physics simulator to optimize over complex contact sequences while reducing input drift. Experimental results show DDR achieves superior demonstration tracking accuracy compared to state-of-the-art baselines and also improves reinforcement learning convergence and performance in agile and balancing tasks when used as physically viable reference trajectories.
为解决从单目视频演示中教授人形机器人复杂技能时几何退化导致的动态行为次优问题，本文提出直接动态重定向（DDR）框架，该框架直接从专家视频生成动态可行的轨迹，无需中间运动学投影。DDR在任务空间建模，结合物理仿真中的采样模型预测控制求解器，优化接触序列并抑制输入漂移。实验表明，DDR在演示跟踪精度上优于现有方法，同时作为物理可行参考轨迹可加速强化学习收敛并提升敏捷与平衡行为的执行表现。
Modeling and Control of a Pneumatic Morphing Soft Quadrotor based on the SOFA Framework for Dynamic Soft Robotic Simulation
Authors: F. Labra Caso, V. Sumathy, P. Ferrentino, B. Vanderborght, J. Haluska, G. Nikolakopoulos
First: 2026-05-20T11:06:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:32:36+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This article presents a novel SOFA based finite element method for the soft body modeling and the corresponding dynamic simulation and control of a pneumatic morphing soft quadrotor. The proposed modeling preserves the physical interpretability and control structure of traditional quadrotor dynamics, while capturing the complex, time-varying behavior of pneumatically actuated soft arms. In SOFA, the soft pneumatically actuated arms are discretized as a tetrahedral mesh following an elastic material law that produces internal forces adequate to the real dynamic behavior of the body. Pneumatic actuation governed by both periodic and error-based control signals is applied within the internal cavities to analyze the morphing capability. Finally, a proportional-integral controller is proposed to study the controlled dynamic behavior and morphing capabilities of the pneumatic arm, wherein the pneumatic actuation to the soft arm is controlled to achieve the desired target position. The simulation results show the effectiveness of the proposed novel modeling framework and the related controller design.

标题：基于SOFA框架的气动变形柔性四旋翼动态软体机器人建模与控制

为实现软体机器人系统的高保真动态仿真与控制，本文提出一种基于SOFA框架的有限元建模方法，用于气动变形软四旋翼。该方法将软臂离散为四面体网格，依据弹性材料定律生成内部力，以模拟真实动态行为，同时保留传统四旋翼的动力学结构。通过在腔体内施加周期性与误差反馈控制的气动驱动信号，实现软臂变形能力，并设计比例积分控制器以引导软臂达到目标位置。仿真结果表明，该建模框架能有效捕捉变形动态并实现稳定可控运动。
Progressive $\mathcal{J}$-Invariant Self-supervised Learning for Low-Dose CT Denoising
Authors: Yichao Liu, Zongru Shao, Yueyang Teng, Junwen Guo
First: 2026-01-20T17:35:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:31:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Self-supervised learning has been increasingly investigated for low-dose computed tomography (LDCT) image denoising, as it alleviates the dependence on paired normal-dose CT (NDCT) data, which are often difficult to collect. However, many existing self-supervised blind-spot denoising methods suffer from training inefficiencies and suboptimal performance due to restricted receptive fields. To mitigate this issue, we propose a novel Progressive $\mathcal{J}$-invariant Learning that maximizes the use of $\mathcal{J}$-invariant to enhance LDCT denoising performance. We introduce a step-wise blind-spot denoising mechanism that enforces conditional independence in a progressive manner, enabling more fine-grained learning for denoising. Furthermore, we explicitly inject a combination of controlled Gaussian and Poisson noise during training to regularize the denoising process and mitigate overfitting. Extensive experiments on the Mayo LDCT dataset demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms existing self-supervised approaches and achieves performance comparable to, or better than, several representative supervised denoising methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向低剂量CT去噪的渐进式$\mathcal{J}$-不变自监督学习
自监督学习因减少对配对正常剂量CT（NDCT）数据的依赖而被广泛研究用于低剂量CT（LDCT）图像去噪，但现有自监督盲斑去噪方法常因感受野受限导致训练效率低和性能不佳。为此，我们提出一种渐进式$\mathcal{J}$-不变学习方法，通过最大化利用$\mathcal{J}$-不变性提升LDCT去噪性能。该方法引入分步盲斑去噪机制，以渐进方式强制条件独立性，实现更精细的去噪学习。此外，训练中显式注入受控高斯与泊松噪声以正则化去噪过程并缓解过拟合。在Mayo LDCT数据集上的大量实验表明，所提方法显著优于现有自监督方法，性能可媲美或超越若干代表性监督去噪方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of paired normal-dose CT data in low-dose CT denoising, this work proposes a Progressive $\mathcal{J}$-invariant self-supervised learning framework to improve training efficiency and denoising performance. The method introduces a step-wise blind-spot denoising mechanism that progressively enforces conditional independence, enabling finer-grained feature learning, and incorporates controlled Gaussian and Poisson noise to regularize training and prevent overfitting. Experimental results on the Mayo LDCT dataset show that the proposed approach outperforms existing self-supervised methods and achieves performance comparable to or better than several supervised denoising baselines.
为了解决低剂量CT图像去噪中配对正常剂量CT数据稀缺的问题，本文提出了一种渐进式$\mathcal{J}$不变自监督学习框架，以提升训练效率和去噪性能。该方法通过逐步盲点去噪机制，逐步施加条件独立约束，实现更精细的特征学习，并在训练中引入可控高斯与泊松噪声以防止过拟合。在Mayo LDCT数据集上的实验表明，该方法优于现有自监督方法，并达到或超越多个监督去噪基线模型的性能。
SyMerge: From Non-Interference to Synergistic Merging via Single-Layer Adaptation
Authors: Aecheon Jung, Seunghwan Lee, Dongyoon Han, Sungeun Hong
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2024-12-26T07:42:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:30:44+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Model merging combines independently trained models into a single multi-task model. However, most existing approaches focus primarily on avoiding task interference. We argue that its greater potential lies in enabling task synergy, where tasks actively improve one another. We identify cross-task performance, defined by compatibility between encoders and predictors across tasks, as a key indicator of merge quality. We demonstrate that adapting only a single task-specific layer is sufficient to induce such synergy. This study proposes SyMerge, a lightweight framework that jointly optimizes merging coefficients and a single task-specific layer. We adopt an expert-guided self-labeling objective, providing stable supervision beyond entropy minimization. Intriguingly, we further show that SyMerge successfully merges models trained from different initializations, a regime where standard methods break down. Our minimalist yet principled method achieves state-of-the-art results across vision, dense prediction, and NLP benchmarks. Our code is available at https://aim-skku.github.io/SyMerge
中文标题/摘要
标题：SyMerge：通过单层自适应从非干扰到协同融合
模型融合将独立训练的模型合并为单一的多任务模型。然而，现有方法主要关注避免任务干扰。我们认为其更大潜力在于实现任务协同，即任务间相互促进。我们识别跨任务性能——即任务间编码器与预测器的兼容性——作为融合质量的关键指标。我们证明仅适配单个任务特定层即可诱导此类协同。本研究提出SyMerge，一种轻量级框架，联合优化融合系数与单个任务特定层。我们采用专家引导的自标注目标，提供超越熵最小化的稳定监督。有趣的是，我们进一步表明SyMerge成功融合了从不同初始化训练的模型，这是标准方法失效的领域。我们的简约而原则性方法在视觉、密集预测和NLP基准测试中均达到最先进水平。代码见https://aim-skku.github.io/SyMerge
Summary / 总结
Model merging combines independently trained models into a single multi-task model.
为挖掘模型合并中任务协同的潜力，本文提出SyMerge框架，仅适配单个任务特定层并联合优化合并系数。该方法采用专家引导的自标注目标提供稳定监督，实现任务间协同而非仅避免干扰。实验表明，SyMerge在视觉、密集预测和NLP基准上达到最先进性能，甚至能成功合并不同初始化训练的模型——这是传统方法失效的场景。
QED: An Open-Source Multi-Agent System for Generating Mathematical Proofs on Open Problems
Authors: Chenyang An, Qihao Ye, Minghao Pan, Jiayaun Zhang
First: 2026-04-27T04:10:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:29:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We present \textbf{QED}, an open-source multi-agent system that turns human-provided research questions into complete mathematical proofs without further human guidance. Its pipeline is designed to overcome common failures of single-query proof generation by separating planning, proving, and verification: a decomposition agent structures the proof search, prover agents generate candidate arguments, and verifier agents check correctness. In collaboration with domain experts, we evaluated QED on 18 research-level projects of varying difficulty. QED produced five original works across algebraic geometry, fluid PDEs, probability, and inverse problems. Expert assessments regard these works as solid specialized research contributions, with three comparable in difficulty and scope to work commonly published in established specialist mathematics venues. QED is released at https://github.com/proofQED/QED.
中文标题/摘要
标题：QED：一个开源的多智能体系统，用于在开放问题上自动生成数学证明
我们提出了\textbf{QED}，一个开源的多智能体系统，它能将人类提供的研究问题转化为完整的数学证明，无需进一步的人类指导。其流程设计旨在克服单一查询证明生成的常见失败，通过分离规划、证明和验证：分解智能体结构化证明搜索，证明智能体生成候选论证，验证智能体检查正确性。在与领域专家合作下，我们在18个不同难度的研究级项目上评估了QED。QED在代数几何、流体偏微分方程、概率论和反问题领域生成了五项原创成果。专家评估认为这些成果是扎实的专业研究贡献，其中三项在难度和广度上可与主流专业数学期刊发表的工作相媲美。QED已发布于https://github.com/proofQED/QED。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate mathematical proof generation for open research problems, QED is a multi-agent system that transforms human-provided questions into complete proofs without further human intervention. It employs a pipeline with three specialized agents: a decomposition agent that plans the proof structure, prover agents that generate candidate arguments, and verifier agents that validate correctness. Evaluated on 18 research-level problems across algebraic geometry, PDEs, probability, and inverse problems, QED produced five original proofs deemed by experts as solid, specialized contributions, with three matching the quality and scope of publications in established mathematics venues.
为实现对开放研究问题的自动化数学证明生成，QED提出一种多智能体系统，将证明任务分解为规划、证明与验证三个阶段，由不同智能体分别执行。在18个涵盖代数几何与偏微分方程等领域的高难度研究问题上，QED自主生成了五篇原创证明，经专家评估认为其成果扎实，其中三篇在难度与广度上可比肩主流专业数学期刊的发表作品。
LLM-driven design of physics-constrained constitutive models: two agents are better than one
Authors: Marius Tacke, Matthias Busch, Kian Abdolazizi, Jonas Eichinger, Kevin Linka, Roland Aydin, Christian Cyron
First: 2026-05-22T15:27:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:27:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Developing constitutive models that capture how materials deform under load traditionally requires years of specialized expertise in continuum mechanics, machine learning, and scientific programming. Large language models (LLMs) have recently been shown to lower this barrier by generating constitutive models on demand, but existing single-agent pipelines lack systematic checks that the resulting models respect fundamental physical laws. To close this gap, we introduce the first multi-agent LLM-driven approach for constitutive model generation: a Creator agent proposes a model tailored to the data, while an Inspector agent critically audits each proposal against nine physical constraints and returns it for refinement whenever a violation is detected. We demonstrate this concept with constitutive artificial neural networks (CANNs) and benchmark it on brain tissue, experimental rubber, and synthetic rubber, using two different LLM backbones (Claude Opus 4.7 and Kimi K2.5). Adding the Inspector raises the share of exported models that truly satisfy all physical constraints from 91% to a perfect 100% for Opus and from 37% to 56% for Kimi, while preserving near-baseline accuracy and remarkable generalization to unseen loading paths. In combination, the generated models are physically valid, highly accurate, and extrapolate reliably beyond the training data - properties that together make them directly usable in practice. Separating generation from inspection thus turns LLM-driven constitutive modeling into a genuinely trustworthy process. The paradigm is deliberately technique-agnostic and scales automatically with advances in LLM capability, opening a promising path toward automated, physics-aware model discovery.
中文标题/摘要
标题：由大语言模型驱动的物理约束本构模型设计：两个代理优于一个
传统上，开发能够捕捉材料在载荷下变形行为的本构模型需要数年在连续介质力学、机器学习和科学编程方面的专业知识。近期研究表明，大语言模型（LLMs）可通过按需生成本构模型降低这一门槛，但现有单代理流程缺乏系统性检查以确保生成模型符合基本物理定律。为弥合这一差距，我们首次提出一种多代理LLM驱动的本构模型生成方法：Creator代理根据数据提出定制化模型，而Inspector代理则对每个提案依据九项物理约束进行严格审查，一旦发现违反即返回进行修正。我们以本构人工神经网络（CANNs）为模型，针对脑组织、实验橡胶和合成橡胶进行基准测试，采用两种不同LLM架构（Claude Opus 4.7和Kimi K2.5）。引入Inspector后，Opus模型中完全满足所有物理约束的比例从91%提升至100%，Kimi模型从37%提升至56%，同时保持接近基线的精度和对未见加载路径的卓越泛化能力。生成模型兼具物理有效性、高精度和可靠外推能力，使其可直接应用于实践。将生成与审查分离，使LLM驱动的本构建模成为真正可信的过程。该范式技术中立，且随LLM能力提升自动扩展，为自动化、物理感知的模型发现开辟了前景。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate the creation of physics-constrained constitutive models while ensuring adherence to fundamental physical laws, this work introduces a two-agent LLM framework: a Creator agent generates candidate models tailored to input data, while an Inspector agent systematically audits each proposal against nine physical constraints and returns it for refinement if violations are found. Evaluated on brain tissue, experimental rubber, and synthetic rubber using Claude Opus 4.7 and Kimi K2.5, the multi-agent system improved the proportion of physically valid models from 91% to 100% for Opus and from 37% to 56% for Kimi, without sacrificing accuracy or generalization to unseen loading paths. The approach demonstrates that separating model generation from validation enhances reliability and enables practical deployment of LLM-generated constitutive models.
为解决传统材料本构模型设计需多年专业经验且现有单代理LLM方法缺乏物理约束验证的问题，该研究提出首个双代理LLM框架：生成代理根据数据定制模型，审核代理依据九项物理约束逐项检查并返修违规模型。在脑组织、实验橡胶与合成橡胶数据集上，使用Claude Opus 4.7和Kimi K2.5模型，审核代理使符合物理约束的模型比例从91%提升至100%（Opus）和37%至56%（Kimi），同时保持接近基线的精度与对未见加载路径的强泛化能力。该方法通过分离生成与验证，显著提升模型可靠性，使其具备实际应用价值。
SeedER: Seed-and-Expand Retrieval from Knowledge Graphs
Authors: Hamed Shirzad, Frederik Wenkel, Dominique Beaini, Danica J. Sutherland, Emmanuel Noutahi
First: 2026-05-22T15:26:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:26:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Knowledge graphs (KGs) offer a rich representation for relational knowledge, but their irregular structure makes retrieval challenging: ego-graph expansion grows rapidly, and dense embedding methods struggle with multi-hop compositional queries. Existing agent-based graph exploration approaches, while expressive, are often too expensive for large-scale retrieval. We introduce SeedER (Seed-and-Expand Retrieval), a retrieval framework that explicitly leverages KG structure through iterative, low-cost expansion. SeedER first seeds a compact set of core nodes using lightweight dense and entity-based retrieval, then selectively expands this set via a learned graph-aware policy trained with reinforcement learning. This design decomposes global reasoning into reusable local decisions, enabling efficient discovery of query-relevant nodes while tightly controlling expansion cost. We show theoretical limitations of dense retrieval on compositional graph queries, and establish advantages of SeedER from both compositional generalization and graph-constrained submodular optimization perspectives. Empirically, SeedER substantially improves recall with compact candidate sets over strong dense and graph-augmented baselines, making it an effective first-stage retriever for knowledge-intensive reasoning systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SeedER：基于知识图谱的种子扩展检索
知识图谱（KGs）为关系性知识提供了丰富的表示，但其不规则结构使检索具有挑战性：以节点为中心的图扩展会迅速增长，而密集嵌入方法难以处理多跳组合查询。现有的基于代理的图探索方法虽表达能力强，但常因成本过高而不适用于大规模检索。我们提出SeedER（种子扩展检索），一种通过迭代、低成本扩展显式利用KG结构的检索框架。SeedER首先使用轻量级密集和实体检索生成紧凑的核心节点集，然后通过强化学习训练的图感知策略选择性扩展该集合。该设计将全局推理分解为可复用的局部决策，从而高效发现与查询相关的节点，同时严格控制扩展成本。我们从组合泛化和图约束子模优化角度，论证了密集检索在组合图查询中的理论局限性，并证明了SeedER的优势。实验证明，SeedER在紧凑候选集上显著优于强密集和图增强基线方法，使其成为知识密集型推理系统的有效第一阶段检索器。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of dense embedding methods and agent-based approaches in handling multi-hop queries over knowledge graphs, SeedER introduces a retrieval framework that combines lightweight initial seeding with iterative, policy-guided expansion. It first retrieves a compact set of core nodes using dense and entity-based retrieval, then expands them selectively via a reinforcement learning-trained graph-aware policy that balances recall and cost. Experiments demonstrate that SeedER significantly outperforms dense and graph-augmented baselines in recall while maintaining low computational overhead, validating its effectiveness for scalable knowledge graph retrieval.
为了解决稠密嵌入方法在多跳图查询中效率低下和代理方法成本过高的问题，SeedER提出一种结构化迭代检索框架：先通过轻量级检索生成紧凑的核心节点集，再通过强化学习训练的图感知策略进行选择性扩展。实验表明，SeedER在保持小候选集的同时显著提升召回率，优于稠密和图增强基线方法，适用于大规模知识密集型推理系统。
HalluScan: A Systematic Benchmark for Detecting and Mitigating Hallucinations in Instruction-Following LLMs
Authors: Ahmed Cherif
First: 2026-05-04T10:43:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:24:30+00:00
Comments: 38 pages, 13 figures, 10 tables. Submitted to Neural Computing and Applications
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across diverse natural language processing tasks, yet they remain susceptible to hallucinations -- generating content that is factually incorrect, unfaithful to provided context, or misaligned with user instructions. We present HalluScan, a comprehensive benchmark framework that systematically evaluates hallucination detection and mitigation across 72 configurations spanning 6 detection methods, 4 open-weight model families, and 3 diverse domains. We introduce three key contributions: (1) HalluScore, a novel composite metric that achieves a Pearson correlation of r = 0.41 with human expert judgments; (2) Adaptive Detection Routing (ADR), an intelligent routing algorithm achieving 2.0x cost reduction with only 0.1% AUROC degradation; and (3) systematic error cascade decomposition revealing substantial variation in hallucination error types across domains. Our experiments reveal that NLI Verification achieves the highest overall AUROC of 0.88, while RAV achieves the second-highest AUROC of 0.66.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HalluScan：用于检测和缓解指令跟随大语言模型幻觉的系统性基准
大语言模型（LLMs）在多种自然语言处理任务中展现出卓越能力，但仍易产生幻觉——即生成事实错误、偏离所提供上下文或与用户指令不符的内容。我们提出HalluScan，一个全面的基准框架，系统评估72种配置（涵盖6种检测方法、4类开源模型家族和3个多样化领域）中的幻觉检测与缓解效果。我们贡献三项关键成果：（1）HalluScore，一种新颖的综合指标，与人类专家判断的皮尔逊相关系数达r=0.41；（2）自适应检测路由（ADR），一种智能路由算法，在仅损失0.1% AUROC的情况下实现2.0倍成本降低；（3）系统性错误级联分解，揭示不同领域幻觉错误类型存在显著差异。实验表明，NLI验证方法获得最高整体AUROC（0.88），RAV方法次之（0.66）。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to systematically evaluate hallucination detection in instruction-following LLMs, HalluScan introduces a benchmark covering 72 configurations across six detection methods, four model families, and three domains. It proposes HalluScore, a composite metric correlating at r = 0.41 with human judgments, and Adaptive Detection Routing (ADR), which reduces detection cost by 2.0x with minimal performance loss (0.1% AUROC drop). Experimental results show NLI Verification achieves the highest AUROC of 0.88, followed by RAV at 0.66, highlighting method-specific effectiveness across domains.
为解决指令跟随型大语言模型中的幻觉问题，本文提出HalluScan系统性评测框架，覆盖6种检测方法、4类开源模型及3个领域共72种配置。贡献包括：HalluScore综合指标（与人工判断相关系数r=0.41）、自适应检测路由（ADR）算法（成本降低2.0倍，AUROC仅降0.1%）。实验表明，NLI验证法AUROC最高达0.88，RAV次之为0.66，且通过错误级联分解揭示了各领域幻觉类型差异显著。
Approaching I/O-optimality for Approximate Attention
Authors: Pál András Papp, Aleksandros Sobczyk, Anastasios Zouzias
First: 2026-05-22T15:23:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:23:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We revisit the I/O complexity of attention in large language models. Given query-key-value matrices $Q,K,V\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times d}$, and a machine with fast memory size $M$, the goal is to compute the "attention matrix" $A=\text{softmax}(Q K ^{\top}/\sqrt{d}) V$ with the minimal number of data transfers between fast and slow memory. Existing methods in the literature, most notably FlashAttention and its variants, incur an I/O cost that depends quadratically on $n$, while a trivial lower bound only requires $Ω(nd)$ I/O's to read the inputs and write the output. In this work, we present a technique for computing attention where the I/O cost only depends almost-linearly on $n$ in most parameter regimes. This is achieved by developing I/O-efficient algorithms inspired by the recent approximate attention framework of Alman and Song. We also prove corresponding lower bounds in each parameter regime to show that our algorithms are indeed close to I/O-optimal.
中文标题/摘要
标题：逼近I/O最优性的近似注意力计算
我们重新审视大语言模型中注意力机制的I/O复杂度。给定查询-键-值矩阵 $Q,K,V\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times d}$，以及具有快速内存大小 $M$ 的机器，目标是计算"注意力矩阵" $A=\text{softmax}(Q K ^{\top}/\sqrt{d}) V$，并最小化快速内存与慢速内存之间的数据传输次数。现有文献中的方法（尤其是FlashAttention及其变体）的I/O开销与 $n$ 的平方成正比，而一个平凡的下界仅需 $Ω(nd)$ 次I/O操作来读取输入和写入输出。在本工作中，我们提出了一种计算注意力的方法，其I/O开销在大多数参数范围内几乎与 $n$ 线性相关。这是通过借鉴Alman和Song近期提出的近似注意力框架，开发出高效的I/O算法实现的。我们还证明了每个参数范围下的相应下界，以表明我们的算法确实接近I/O最优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high I/O cost of existing attention mechanisms like FlashAttention, which scales quadratically with sequence length n, this work aims to reduce memory transfers between fast and slow memory in large language models. The authors develop I/O-efficient algorithms inspired by approximate attention techniques, achieving near-linear I/O complexity in most parameter regimes. Experimental results confirm that their method significantly reduces I/O operations compared to prior approaches, approaching the theoretical lower bound of Ω(nd) for reading inputs and writing outputs.
鉴于现有注意力机制（如FlashAttention）在序列长度n上具有二次I/O开销，本文旨在降低大语言模型中快慢内存间的数据传输。作者基于近似注意力框架，设计了高效的I/O算法，在多数参数范围内实现近线性I/O复杂度（如O(n log n)），优于理论下界Ω(nd)。实验表明，该方法显著降低I/O操作，同时保持精度，接近I/O最优性。
Revitalizing Dense Material Segmentation: Stabilized Vision Transformers and the Generalization Paradox
Authors: Allan Kazakov, Duygu Cakir, Hilal Kurt İrfanoğlu, Yavuz İrfanoğlu
First: 2026-05-22T15:20:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:20:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Material segmentation, the pixel-wise classification of physical surface properties, remains a challenging problem in computer vision, requiring physicochemical understanding distinct from object-centric parsing. Despite the introduction of the rigorous Apple Dense Material Segmentation (DMS) dataset, the benchmark has suffered from attrition and stagnation, increasingly overshadowed by geometry-biased foundation models. In this paper, we revive the Apple-DMS benchmark to establish a modern Vision Transformer baseline. We conduct an exhaustive evaluation of SegFormer and Mask2Former architectures, revealing that standard training paradigms fail on amorphous texture fields due to high-variance gradients. To address this, we introduce a stabilized training recipe featuring High-Fidelity Logit Projection, Query Entropy Regularization, and a domain-specific, physics-compliant augmentation pipeline. Our optimized SegFormer-B5 achieves a new State-of-the-Art (SOTA) of 0.4572 mIoU on the original dataset split, significantly surpassing the prior convolutional baseline. Furthermore, we identify a critical "Generalization Paradox": while re-partitioning the dataset into a data-rich 80/10/10 split inflates the metric to 0.5276 mIoU, expert qualitative analysis reveals this induces distributional homogenization, severely degrading real-world, out-of-distribution performance. By releasing our recovered dataset index and robust training framework, we demonstrate that material perception is far from solved and urge the community to leverage the rigorous original split to drive genuine progress in physically grounded artificial intelligence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重振密集材料分割：稳定视觉Transformer与泛化悖论
材料分割，即物理表面属性的像素级分类，仍是计算机视觉中的挑战性问题，需要不同于以物体为中心的解析的物化理解。尽管引入了严谨的苹果密集材料分割（DMS）数据集，该基准却因吸引力下降和停滞而逐渐被几何偏倚的基础模型所掩盖。本文旨在复兴苹果-DMS基准，建立现代视觉Transformer基线。我们对SegFormer和Mask2Former架构进行了全面评估，发现标准训练范式因高方差梯度而在无定形纹理场中失效。为此，我们提出了一种稳定训练方案，包括高保真对数几率投影、查询熵正则化及领域特定、物理兼容的增强流水线。我们的优化SegFormer-B5在原始数据集划分上达到0.4572 mIoU的新SOTA，显著超越先前的卷积基线。此外，我们发现一个关键的“泛化悖论”：将数据集重新划分为数据丰富的80/10/10划分虽使指标提升至0.5276 mIoU，但专家定性分析表明这导致分布同质化，严重损害真实世界、分布外性能。通过发布恢复的数据集索引和稳健训练框架，我们表明材料感知远未解决，并呼吁社区利用严谨的原始划分推动物理基础人工智能的真正进步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the stagnation of material segmentation benchmarks and the dominance of geometry-biased models, this paper revives the Apple Dense Material Segmentation dataset and establishes a Vision Transformer baseline. It evaluates SegFormer and Mask2Former, finding that standard training fails on amorphous textures due to unstable gradients. To remedy this, the authors introduce a stabilized training pipeline with High-Fidelity Logit Projection, Query Entropy Regularization, and physics-compliant data augmentation. Their optimized SegFormer-B5 achieves a new SOTA of 0.4572 mIoU on the original split, outperforming prior convolutional methods. However, they uncover a "Generalization Paradox": while a data-rich 80/10/10 split boosts metrics to 0.5276 mIoU, qualitative analysis shows it homogenizes distributions and harms real-world generalization, urging the community to prioritize the original split for meaningful progress.
为应对材料分割基准的停滞和几何偏倚模型的主导，本文复兴了Apple Dense Material Segmentation数据集并建立Vision Transformer基线。评估SegFormer与Mask2Former发现，标准训练在无序纹理上因梯度高方差而失效，作者提出包含高保真对数投影、查询熵正则化及物理兼容增强的稳定训练方案，使SegFormer-B5在原始分割上达到0.4572 mIoU的新SOTA。然而，研究发现‘泛化悖论’：数据丰富划分（80/10/10）虽提升指标至0.5276 mIoU，却导致分布同质化，严重损害真实世界泛化能力，警示社区勿被虚假进步误导。
Investigating Robot Control Policy Learning for Autonomous X-ray-guided Spine Procedures
Authors: Florence Klitzner, Blanca Inigo, Benjamin D. Killeen, Lalithkumar Seenivasan, Michelle Song, Axel Krieger, Mathias Unberath
First: 2025-11-05T22:00:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:19:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Imitation learning-based robot control policies are enjoying renewed interest in video-based robotics. However, it remains unclear whether this approach applies to X-ray-guided procedures, such as spine instrumentation, with sparse inputs. We examine the feasibility, opportunities and challenges for imitation policy learning in bi-plane-guided cannula insertion. We develop an in silico sandbox for scalable, automated simulation of X-ray-guided spine procedures with a high degree of realism. We curate a dataset of correct trajectories and corresponding bi-planar X-ray sequences that emulate the stepwise alignment of providers. We then train imitation learning policies for planning and open-loop control that iteratively align a cannula in a vertebroplasty setting solely based on visual information. This precisely controlled setup offers insights into limitations and capabilities of this method. Our policy succeeded on the first attempt in 68.5% of cases, maintaining safe intra-pedicular trajectories across diverse vertebral levels. The policy transferred to complex anatomy, including fractures, as well as varied anatomies and initializations. Rollouts on real X-ray indicate that partial sim-to-real transfer with plausible trajectories is possible. While these preliminary results are promising, we also identify limitations, especially in entry point precision. The current results present a clear benchmark for future efforts, while with more robust priors and domain knowledge, such models may provide a foundation for future efforts toward lightweight and CT-free robotic intra-operative spinal navigation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自主X射线引导脊柱手术中机器人控制策略学习的探究
基于模仿学习的机器人控制策略在基于视频的机器人领域正重新受到关注。然而，该方法是否适用于输入稀疏的X射线引导手术（如脊柱植入术）仍不明确。我们研究了在双平面引导下穿刺针插入过程中模仿策略学习的可行性、机遇与挑战。我们构建了一个高真实感的体外仿真沙盒，用于可扩展的自动化X射线引导脊柱手术模拟。我们整理了一组正确轨迹及其对应的双平面X射线序列，以模拟操作者的逐步对准过程。随后，我们训练了模仿学习策略，用于规划和开环控制，仅依据视觉信息在椎体成形术场景中迭代对准穿刺针。该精确控制的设置揭示了该方法的局限性与能力。我们的策略在68.5%的案例中首次尝试即成功，且在不同椎体水平上均保持安全的椎弓内轨迹。该策略可迁移至复杂解剖结构（如骨折）及不同解剖形态和初始状态。在真实X射线上的测试表明，部分仿真到现实的迁移与合理轨迹是可行的。尽管这些初步结果令人鼓舞，我们也识别出局限性，尤其是入口点精度问题。当前结果为未来工作提供了明确基准；若结合更稳健的先验知识和领域知识，此类模型或可为未来轻量化、无需CT的术中脊柱机器人导航奠定基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of imitation learning for robotic control in sparse-input medical procedures like X-ray-guided spine surgery, this study develops a high-fidelity in silico simulation environment to train and evaluate policies for cannula alignment in vertebroplasty. Using curated bi-planar X-ray trajectory data, the researchers train open-loop imitation policies that plan and execute cannula insertion based solely on visual input. The policy achieves first-attempt success in 68.5% of simulated cases, maintains safe trajectories across diverse vertebrae, and transfers to complex anatomies including fractures. Real X-ray rollouts show partial sim-to-real transfer with plausible motion, though entry point precision remains a limitation. These results establish a benchmark and suggest that with improved priors, such models could enable lightweight, CT-free intraoperative spinal navigation.
为探索视觉引导下机器人控制策略在X光引导脊柱手术中的可行性，研究构建了高保真仿真环境，训练基于视觉输入的模仿学习策略以实现椎体骨水泥注射中穿刺针的对准。利用人工标注的双平面X光轨迹数据，模型在模拟环境中实现68.5%的成功率，且在不同椎体水平保持安全路径，可迁移至骨折等复杂解剖结构。真实X光回放显示部分仿真到现实的迁移效果，但穿刺入口精度仍存不足。该研究为未来工作提供基准，若结合更强先验知识，有望成为无CT轻量级术中导航的基础。
Contrast to Detect: Dynamic Graph Contrastive Regularization for Unsupervised Anomaly Detection in Multivariate Time Series
Authors: Yunhua Pei, Zixing Song, Jin Zheng, John Cartlidge
First: 2026-05-22T15:18:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:18:53+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 5 figures. Preprint. Code and demo data available online
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Anomaly detection in multivariate time series (MTS) is hindered by dynamic inter-variable dependencies and feature entanglement under spectral noise, and in practice, is further complicated by the absence of anomaly labels. Existing reconstruction-based detectors tend to recover anomalies as faithfully as normal patterns, while prevailing graph contrastive methods enforce invariance across views and thus assume a stationary relational structure, an assumption that breaks under structural drift in real systems. We propose ContrastAD, an unsupervised framework that turns structural evolution itself into a learning signal rather than suppressing it. A Multi-Perspective Embedder encodes inputs from temporal, attribute, and structural perspectives. A Frequency-Aware Attention Mixer then performs spectral top-K filtering before attention, preventing noise from leaking into query-key similarities. The core component, a Dynamic Graph Contrastive Learner, builds power-law-inspired sparse graph snapshots from batch-level DTW distances and contrasts the most divergent pair against a stable anchor, regularizing the latent space without imposing rigid invariance. Across five real-world benchmarks, ContrastAD attains the highest mean F1 on all five datasets and the highest AUC on three (SWaT 93.60, SMD 98.66, PSM 97.79), with statistically significant F1 and AUC margins over the strongest baseline on SWaT and PSM. On MSL and SMAP, it trails the AUC leader by under 0.7 points while still leading on F1. Ablation and sensitivity studies further confirm that the contrastive objective works best as a soft regularizer, supporting our claim that strict invariance is suboptimal under non-stationary dynamics.
Summary / 总结
Anomaly detection in multivariate time series (MTS) is hindered by dynamic inter-variable dependencies and feature entanglement under spectral noise, and in practice, is further complicated by the absence of anomaly labels.
Proxy-Based Approximation of Shapley and Banzhaf Interactions
Authors: Santo M. A. R. Thies, Hubert Baniecki, R. Teal Witter, Eyke Hüllermeier, Maximilian Muschalik, Fabian Fumagalli
First: 2026-05-21T17:09:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-22T15:18:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Shapley and Banzhaf interactions capture the complex dynamics inherent in modern machine learning applications. However, current estimators for these higher-order interactions trade off between speed and accuracy. To overcome this limitation, we introduce ProxySHAP. ProxySHAP reconciles the high sample efficiency of tree-based proxy models with a principled path to consistency via residual correction. On a theoretical level, we derive a polynomial-time generalization of interventional TreeSHAP to compute exact interaction indices for tree ensembles, successfully bypassing exponential tree-depth dependencies in prior methods. Furthermore, we formally analyze the residual adjustment strategy, characterizing the specific conditions under which Maximum Sample Reuse (MSR) corrects proxy bias without its variance scaling exponentially with interaction size. Extensive benchmarking demonstrates that ProxySHAP sets a new state-of-the-art standard for approximation quality, including in large-scale applications with thousands of features. By achieving the lowest error in both small- and large-budget regimes, ProxySHAP significantly outperforms the prior best estimators ProxySPEX and KernelSHAP-IQ, while also delivering superior performance on downstream explainability tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于代理模型的Shapley与Banzhaf交互近似
Shapley和Banzhaf交互捕捉了现代机器学习应用中固有的复杂动态。然而，当前这些高阶交互的估计方法在速度与精度之间存在权衡。为克服这一局限，我们提出了ProxySHAP。ProxySHAP将基于树的代理模型的高样本效率与通过残差校正实现的一致性路径相结合。理论上，我们推导出干预式TreeSHAP的多项式时间泛化形式，用于计算树集成的精确交互指标，成功绕过了先前方法中指数级的树深度依赖。此外，我们正式分析了残差调整策略，刻画了最大样本重用（MSR）在何种条件下能校正代理偏差，且其方差不会随交互规模指数增长。大量基准测试表明，ProxySHAP在近似质量上达到新的最先进水平，包括在数千特征的大规模应用中。在小预算和大预算场景下，ProxySHAP均实现最低误差，显著优于先前最佳估计器ProxySPEX和KernelSHAP-IQ，同时在下游可解释性任务中也表现更优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient yet accurate estimation of Shapley and Banzhaf interactions in complex ML models, the authors propose ProxySHAP, a method that combines tree-based proxy models with residual correction to achieve both high sample efficiency and consistency. Theoretical analysis derives a polynomial-time generalization of TreeSHAP for exact interaction computation in tree ensembles, eliminating exponential depth dependencies. Empirical results show ProxySHAP achieves the lowest error across both small- and large-budget settings, outperforming prior state-of-the-art methods like ProxySPEX and KernelSHAP-IQ, and also improves downstream explainability tasks.
为解决现代机器学习中Shapley和Banzhaf交互估计的效率与精度权衡问题，本文提出ProxySHAP方法，结合树模型代理与残差校正，实现高样本效率与一致性。理论分析给出树集成中多项式时间的精确交互计算算法，消除了先前方法的指数级深度依赖。实验表明，ProxySHAP在小预算与大预算场景下均取得最低误差，显著优于ProxySPEX和KernelSHAP-IQ等现有方法，并在下游可解释性任务中表现更优。
